## Practice 452: Community Garden Registration | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two neighbors at a community meeting.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why does the man want to join the community garden?
- 1. His apartment doesn't have enough sunlight.
- 2. He wants to meet his neighbors.
- 3. He recently moved to a house without a yard.
- 4. The woman convinced him to join.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. His apartment doesn't have enough sunlight.: The man explicitly states "My apartment balcony just doesn't get enough sunlight" as his reason for wanting to join.
❌ 2. He wants to meet his neighbors.: While he does meet the woman, this isn't stated as his reason for joining.
❌ 3. He recently moved to a house without a yard.: There's no mention of him recently moving or not having a yard.
❌ 4. The woman convinced him to join.: The man was already interested in joining before talking to the woman; she didn't convince him.

**Q2:** What problem did the woman experience in the garden last year?
- 1. Poor soil quality
- 2. Squirrels damaging her crops
- 3. Insufficient water supply
- 4. Conflict with other gardeners
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Poor soil quality: The woman doesn't mention any issues with soil quality.
✅ 2. Squirrels damaging her crops: The woman specifically states "I had a terrible time with squirrels last summer. They kept digging up my carrots!"
❌ 3. Insufficient water supply: There's no mention of water supply problems.
❌ 4. Conflict with other gardeners: The woman doesn't mention any conflicts with other gardeners.

**Q3:** What is included in the community garden fee?
- 1. Seeds and fertilizer
- 2. Personal gardening tools
- 3. Water access and communal tools
- 4. Weekly gardening lessons
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Seeds and fertilizer: The woman doesn't mention seeds or fertilizer being included in the fee.
❌ 2. Personal gardening tools: The woman mentions communal tools, not personal tools.
✅ 3. Water access and communal tools: The woman specifically states "That includes water access and use of the communal tools in the shed."
❌ 4. Weekly gardening lessons: No gardening lessons are mentioned in the conversation.

**Q4:** What does the woman probably think about community gardening?
- 1. It's too expensive for what you get.
- 2. It's a good way to socialize with neighbors.
- 3. It requires too much volunteer work.
- 4. It's mainly for experienced gardeners.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's too expensive for what you get.: The woman describes the fee as "reasonable" and mentions multiple benefits, not suggesting it's too expensive.
❌ 2. It's a good way to socialize with neighbors.: The woman mentions meeting with others on Saturday mornings, saying "It's more fun that way," and she's friendly with the man, suggesting she values the social aspect.
✅ 3. It requires too much volunteer work.: The woman only mentions two maintenance days per season, which she doesn't present as burdensome.
❌ 4. It's mainly for experienced gardeners.: The woman is welcoming to the man who is new to gardening and offers him advice, indicating she doesn't think experience is necessary.

**Q5:** What will likely happen next Tuesday?
- 1. The man will start planting his vegetables.
- 2. The woman will help install the new fencing.
- 3. They will attend a meeting about plot assignments.
- 4. They will work together in the garden for the first time.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will start planting his vegetables.: The garden season starts April 15th, and there's no indication planting will begin at the meeting.
❌ 2. The woman will help install the new fencing.: While new fencing is mentioned, there's no indication it will be installed at the meeting.
✅ 3. They will attend a meeting about plot assignments.: The man specifically mentions "there's a meeting next Tuesday at the community center about plot assignments" and the woman confirms she'll be there.
❌ 4. They will work together in the garden for the first time.: They mention working together on Saturdays, not Tuesday, and the garden season hasn't started yet.

## Practice 453: Public Library Digital Perks | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a library patron and a librarian about membership benefits.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why is the man particularly interested in e-books?
- 1. He doesn't have space for physical books at home.
- 2. He frequently travels for his job.
- 3. He prefers reading on digital devices.
- 4. He wants to save money on purchasing books.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He doesn't have space for physical books at home: This reason is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. He frequently travels for his job: The man specifically states, "I'm particularly interested in e-books since I travel a lot for work."
❌ 3. He prefers reading on digital devices: While he uses an iPad, he doesn't state a preference for digital reading.
❌ 4. He wants to save money on purchasing books: This reason is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q2:** What will the man most likely do after this conversation?
- 1. Attend a computer skills workshop.
- 2. Install the Libby application.
- 3. Visit the Science Centre immediately.
- 4. Register for the monthly book club.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Attend a computer skills workshop: The man doesn't specifically mention planning to attend the workshop.
✅ 2. Install the Libby application: The man explicitly states at the end of the conversation, "I'll download that Libby app tonight and look into those museum passes right away."
❌ 3. Visit the Science Centre immediately: The man mentions his niece and nephew are visiting next month, not immediately.
❌ 4. Register for the monthly book club: The man doesn't express interest in joining the book club.

**Q3:** How many e-books can the man borrow at one time?
- 1. Four
- 2. Six
- 3. Eight
- 4. Ten
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Four: This is incorrect according to the conversation.
❌ 2. Six: This is incorrect according to the conversation.
✅ 3. Eight: The librarian specifically states, "With your basic membership, you can have up to 8 e-books checked out simultaneously."
❌ 4. Ten: This is incorrect according to the conversation.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the Science Centre passes?
- 1. They are more expensive than Art Gallery passes.
- 2. They are in higher demand than Art Gallery passes.
- 3. They allow entry for fewer people than Art Gallery passes.
- 4. They must be reserved in person at the library.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They are more expensive than Art Gallery passes: The conversation mentions that both passes are free with membership.
✅ 2. They are in higher demand than Art Gallery passes: This can be inferred because the librarian says Science Centre passes need to be booked at least two weeks in advance, while Art Gallery passes are usually available with shorter notice (3-5 days).
❌ 3. They allow entry for fewer people than Art Gallery passes: The conversation states both passes admit the same number of people (two adults and up to four children).
❌ 4. They must be reserved in person at the library: The librarian mentions passes can be reserved online or by phone, not in person.

**Q5:** What is the main suggestion the librarian gives regarding the e-book platforms?
- 1. Use CloudLibrary because it has more titles available.
- 2. Try both platforms to see which one works better.
- 3. Use Libby since it's better suited for iPad users.
- 4. Download books from both platforms to maximize borrowing.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Use CloudLibrary because it has more titles available: The librarian doesn't mention CloudLibrary having more titles.
❌ 2. Try both platforms to see which one works better: The librarian doesn't suggest trying both platforms.
✅ 3. Use Libby since it's better suited for iPad users: The librarian specifically states, "most of our patrons prefer Libby for Apple devices. It has a more user-friendly interface."
❌ 4. Download books from both platforms to maximize borrowing: The librarian doesn't suggest using both platforms to increase borrowing capacity.

**Q6:** Why does the librarian offer to help the man right away?
- 1. To demonstrate how to reserve museum passes.
- 2. To assist with setting up the e-book app on his device.
- 3. To register him for an upcoming workshop.
- 4. To show him how to search the digital catalog.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To demonstrate how to reserve museum passes: The librarian doesn't offer immediate help with museum passes.
✅ 2. To assist with setting up the e-book app on his device: The librarian specifically says, "I can help you set it up on your device right now if you'd like" after discussing the Libby app for his iPad.
❌ 3. To register him for an upcoming workshop: The librarian doesn't offer to register him for any workshops.
❌ 4. To show him how to search the digital catalog: The librarian doesn't offer to show him how to search the catalog.

## Practice 455: Microwave Oven Purchase | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a kitchen appliance store. The woman works at the store, and the man is a customer looking for a new appliance.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Excuse me, I'm looking for a new microwave oven. Mine just stopped working this morning, and I need to replace it as soon as possible.
WOMAN: Of course, I'd be happy to help you with that. Do you have any specific features or size requirements in mind?
MAN: Well, I live in a small apartment, so I don't need anything too big. My old one was pretty basicâjust had a few preset buttons and a timer. I mainly use it for reheating leftovers and defrosting.
WOMAN: I understand. We have several compact models that would work well for apartment living. This one here is quite popularâit's energy efficient and has the basic functions you mentioned, plus a few extra preset options.
MAN: Hmm, that looks good, but what about the price? I'm on a bit of a tight budget right now.
WOMAN: This model is currently on sale for $89.99, down from $119.99. We also have a more basic model for $69.99 if you're looking for something more economical.
MAN: The $89.99 one seems reasonable. Does it come with a warranty?

**Q1:** Why is the man looking for a new microwave?
- 1. His old one is outdated.
- 2. His old one stopped working.
- 3. He is moving to a new apartment.
- 4. He wants to upgrade to a better model.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. His old one is outdated.: The man doesn't mention his old microwave being outdated.
✅ 2. His old one stopped working.: The man explicitly states "Mine just stopped working this morning, and I need to replace it as soon as possible."
❌ 3. He is moving to a new apartment.: There is no mention of the man moving to a new apartment.
❌ 4. He wants to upgrade to a better model.: The man doesn't express a desire to upgrade; he's looking for a replacement because his stopped working.

**Q2:** How much is the microwave that the man is considering?
- 1. $$69.99
- 2. $$89.99
- 3. $$99.99
- 4. $$119.99
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $69.99: This is the price of the more basic model that the woman mentions, not the one the man decides to purchase.
✅ 2. $89.99: The woman states "This model is currently on sale for $89.99" and this is the model the man ultimately chooses.
❌ 3. $99.99: This is the price of the similar white model that is slightly larger, which the man decides against.
❌ 4. $119.99: This is the original price of the model before the sale, not the current price.

**Q3:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He has a large kitchen.
- 2. He uses his microwave for cooking complex meals.
- 3. He lives in a small apartment.
- 4. He has a generous budget for appliances.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He has a large kitchen.: The man specifically states "I live in a small apartment" indicating he does not have a large kitchen.
❌ 2. He uses his microwave for cooking complex meals.: The man says "I mainly use it for reheating leftovers and defrosting" which indicates basic usage, not complex cooking.
✅ 3. He lives in a small apartment.: The man directly states "I live in a small apartment, so I don't need anything too big."
❌ 4. He has a generous budget for appliances.: The man mentions "I'm on a bit of a tight budget right now" and is concerned about the price, showing he does not have a generous budget.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q3:** What concern does the man initially have about the microwave?
- 1. It doesn't have enough preset options.
- 2. It's not the same color as his old one.
- 3. It doesn't have a long enough warranty.
- 4. It's too large for his kitchen.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It doesn't have enough preset options.: The man doesn't express concern about preset options; he actually says his old one "just had a few preset buttons."
✅ 2. It's not the same color as his old one.: The man says "I just realized—my old microwave was white, and this one is stainless steel. Do you have it in white?"
❌ 3. It doesn't have a long enough warranty.: The man asks about the warranty but accepts the standard one-year warranty, saying "I think I'll stick with the standard warranty."
❌ 4. It's too large for his kitchen.: The man is looking for something that isn't "too big" but never expresses concern that the model shown is too large.

**Q4:** How does the man feel about waiting for a special order?
- 1. He is willing to wait for the perfect model.
- 2. He is frustrated by the delay.
- 3. He is reluctant to go without a microwave for that long.
- 4. He is indifferent about the waiting period.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He is willing to wait for the perfect model.: The man decides against waiting for the special order.
❌ 2. He is frustrated by the delay.: The man doesn't express frustration, just reluctance to wait.
✅ 3. He is reluctant to go without a microwave for that long.: The man says "Two weeks is a long time without a microwave" and decides to take the stainless steel model instead of waiting.
❌ 4. He is indifferent about the waiting period.: The man clearly cares about the waiting period, stating it's "a long time without a microwave."

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q6:** Why does the man need delivery service?
- 1. He lives too far from the store.
- 2. The microwave is too heavy to carry.
- 3. He came to the store by bus.
- 4. He has a physical disability.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He lives too far from the store.: The distance from his home to the store is not mentioned as a reason.
❌ 2. The microwave is too heavy to carry.: The man doesn't mention the weight of the microwave as a concern.
✅ 3. He came to the store by bus.: The man specifically states "I took the bus here today" as the reason for needing delivery.
❌ 4. He has a physical disability.: There is no mention of the man having any physical disability.

**Q7:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She is trying to sell the most expensive model.
- 2. She offers solutions to the man's transportation issue.
- 3. She refuses to take the old microwave.
- 4. She doesn't mention the return policy.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She is trying to sell the most expensive model.: The woman offers both a more expensive and a cheaper alternative, showing she's not pushing the most expensive option.
✅ 2. She offers solutions to the man's transportation issue.: When the man mentions he took the bus, she offers delivery service as a solution.
❌ 3. She refuses to take the old microwave.: She actually offers to take the old microwave for disposal for an additional $10 fee.
❌ 4. She doesn't mention the return policy.: She specifically mentions "we do have a 30-day return policy" when discussing the stainless steel model.

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will change his mind about the color.
- 2. The woman will offer an additional discount.
- 3. The man will complete his payment.
- 4. The woman will check for other models.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will change his mind about the color.: The man has already decided to take the stainless steel model.
❌ 2. The woman will offer an additional discount.: There's no indication in the conversation that the woman will offer any further discounts.
✅ 3. The man will complete his payment.: The conversation ends with the total amount calculated ($124.99 plus tax) and the woman asking "How would you like to pay for that today?" indicating payment is the next step.
❌ 4. The woman will check for other models.: The woman has already checked inventory and the man has made his decision on the stainless steel model.

## Practice 458: Autumn Soup Ingredients | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two friends at a local farmer's market.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why is the man at the farmer's market today?
- 1. His sister convinced him to try it.
- 2. He was looking for Sarah.
- 3. The supermarket was closed.
- 4. He needed specialty items not found elsewhere.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. His sister convinced him to try it.: The man says "my sister kept raving about the fresh produce here... I have to say, she wasn't exaggerating."
❌ 2. He was looking for Sarah.: There's no indication he came to the market to find Sarah; their meeting appears to be coincidental.
❌ 3. The supermarket was closed.: There is no mention of the supermarket being closed.
❌ 4. He needed specialty items not found elsewhere.: While he's looking for ingredients for soup, he doesn't say he came because these items couldn't be found elsewhere.

**Q2:** What is the man planning to cook?
- 1. Apple crumble
- 2. Butternut squash soup
- 3. A vegetable stew
- 4. His mother's secret recipe
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Apple crumble: While the man mentions he makes a "pretty decent apple crumble," he's not planning to cook this now. He mentions it as a recipe he could share with the woman.
✅ 2. Butternut squash soup: The man specifically states "I'm trying to find ingredients for this butternut squash soup recipe I want to make."
❌ 3. A vegetable stew: There is no mention of the man planning to make a vegetable stew.
❌ 4. His mother's secret recipe: While he mentions his mom used to make the butternut squash soup, he doesn't refer to it as a secret recipe.

**Q3:** How often does the woman visit the farmer's market?
- 1. Every day
- 2. Only during harvest season
- 3. Weekly on Saturdays
- 4. Whenever she needs fresh herbs
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Every day: The woman does not say she visits the market every day.
❌ 2. Only during harvest season: The woman doesn't limit her visits to harvest season.
✅ 3. Weekly on Saturdays: The woman specifically states "I come here almost every Saturday morning."
❌ 4. Whenever she needs fresh herbs: The woman doesn't say she only visits when she needs herbs.

**Q4:** What does the woman think about the man's cooking abilities?
- 1. He is an excellent chef.
- 2. He probably needs some guidance.
- 3. He should stick to simple recipes.
- 4. He makes better desserts than main courses.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He is an excellent chef.: The woman never suggests the man is an excellent chef. In fact, the man himself says "I'm not exactly a master chef."
✅ 2. He probably needs some guidance.: The woman offers to share her recipe with him after he expresses nervousness about cooking, suggesting she thinks he could use some help.
❌ 3. He should stick to simple recipes.: The woman never suggests he should limit himself to simple recipes.
❌ 4. He makes better desserts than main courses.: While the man mentions making apple crumble, there's no indication that the woman thinks he's better at desserts than main courses.

**Q5:** What will the two friends likely do after shopping?
- 1. Cook soup together at Sarah's house.
- 2. Visit the man's sister.
- 3. Share recipes over coffee.
- 4. Go to the supermarket for additional ingredients.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Cook soup together at Sarah's house.: There is no mention of them cooking together or going to Sarah's house.
❌ 2. Visit the man's sister.: There is no plan mentioned about visiting the man's sister.
✅ 3. Share recipes over coffee.: The woman suggests "Why don't we grab a coffee at that little stand by the entrance after we finish shopping, and I can write down my recipe for you?" and the man agrees.
❌ 4. Go to the supermarket for additional ingredients.: There is no mention of going to a supermarket after shopping at the farmer's market.

## Practice 460: SmartSafe Security System | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a customer and a smart home security consultant.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why does the woman want a home security system?
- 1. She experienced a break-in at her previous home.
- 2. Her insurance company requires it.
- 3. She is often away from home for work.
- 4. Her neighborhood has reported recent thefts.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She experienced a break-in at her previous home: There's no mention of any previous break-in in the conversation.
✅ 2. Her insurance company requires it: Insurance requirements are not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 3. She is often away from home for work: The woman specifically mentions "I do want to feel secure, especially since I travel for work sometimes" as her reason for wanting security.
❌ 4. Her neighborhood has reported recent thefts: There's no mention of neighborhood thefts in the conversation.

**Q2:** What will the woman most likely do after receiving the email?
- 1. Call to change her installation appointment.
- 2. Review the quote before her Wednesday appointment.
- 3. Request additional window sensors.
- 4. Upgrade to the Premium monitoring plan.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Call to change her installation appointment: There's no indication she's unsatisfied with the Wednesday appointment she selected.
❌ 2. Review the quote before her Wednesday appointment: The woman specifically asks for a quote to be emailed so she can "review everything before Wednesday" (her installation appointment).
❌ 3. Request additional window sensors: She's already decided on the additional window sensors during this conversation.
❌ 4. Upgrade to the Premium monitoring plan: She's already decided on the Standard plan and shows no interest in upgrading.

**Q3:** How many window sensors will the woman need to purchase separately?
- 1. Two sensors
- 2. Four sensors
- 3. Six sensors
- 4. None, they're all included
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Two sensors: This is the correct answer. The consultant mentions that the package includes four window sensors, but the woman has six windows on the ground floor, so she needs to purchase two additional sensors for $45.
❌ 2. Four sensors: The package already includes four window sensors, so she doesn't need to purchase these separately.
❌ 3. Six sensors: The woman has six windows total, but doesn't need to purchase all six sensors separately.
❌ 4. None, they're all included: This is incorrect because the package only includes four window sensors, not all six that she needs.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the woman's technical abilities?
- 1. She is comfortable with smartphone technology but not with tools.
- 2. She prefers to have professionals handle all technical matters.
- 3. She has experience with security systems but not installation.
- 4. She is unfamiliar with smart home technology entirely.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. She is comfortable with smartphone technology but not with tools: The woman mentions "I'm not very handy with tools" when choosing professional installation, but she doesn't express any concerns about using the smartphone app to monitor her home.
❌ 2. She prefers to have professionals handle all technical matters: While she chooses professional installation, this is specifically because she's not good with tools, not because she wants professionals to handle everything technical.
❌ 3. She has experience with security systems but not installation: She explicitly states "I've never had one before" regarding security systems.
❌ 4. She is unfamiliar with smart home technology entirely: There's no evidence in the conversation that suggests she's completely unfamiliar with smart home technology.

**Q5:** What is the main recommendation the consultant makes for the woman's situation?
- 1. A basic security package with door sensors only
- 2. A premium package with video surveillance
- 3. A mid-tier package with remote monitoring capabilities
- 4. Glass break detectors instead of window sensors
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A basic security package with door sensors only: The consultant recommends a mid-tier package, not a basic one, and it includes more than just door sensors.
✅ 2. A premium package with video surveillance: While video is included (doorbell camera), the consultant specifically recommends a mid-tier package, not premium.
❌ 3. A mid-tier package with remote monitoring capabilities: The consultant specifically says "For homeowners who travel, we typically recommend our mid-tier package" that includes door/window sensors, motion detectors, and a smart doorbell camera that connects to a smartphone for remote monitoring.
❌ 4. Glass break detectors instead of window sensors: The consultant mentions glass break detectors as an alternative but doesn't specifically recommend them over window sensors.

**Q6:** Why did the woman choose Wednesday for installation instead of Tuesday?
- 1. She needed more time to prepare her home.
- 2. The afternoon time worked better for her schedule.
- 3. She wanted to wait until after receiving the quote.
- 4. She was concerned about the early morning appointment.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. She needed more time to prepare her home: There's no mention of needing time to prepare her home in the conversation.
❌ 2. The afternoon time worked better for her schedule: The woman specifically says "Wednesday at 2:00 would be perfect" when presented with the options of Tuesday at 9:00 a.m. or Wednesday at 2:00 p.m., suggesting the afternoon time fits her schedule better.
❌ 3. She wanted to wait until after receiving the quote: The quote is being sent the same day as their conversation, well before either installation date.
❌ 4. She was concerned about the early morning appointment: While this might be implied, she doesn't explicitly express concern about the early time, just that Wednesday at 2:00 would be "perfect."

## Practice 461: Corporate Catering Consultation | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a catering service. The woman is a customer planning an event, and the man is the head chef.

### Audio Transcript
$4f

**Q1:** What is the woman planning?
- 1. A wedding reception
- 2. A company anniversary party
- 3. A holiday celebration
- 4. A product launch event
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A wedding reception: The conversation clearly states it's for a company anniversary, not a wedding.
✅ 2. A company anniversary party: The woman explicitly states "I'm here to discuss catering options for my company's anniversary party next month" and mentions it's their 10th anniversary.
❌ 3. A holiday celebration: The conversation doesn't mention any holiday, only a company anniversary.
❌ 4. A product launch event: There is no mention of a product launch in the conversation.

**Q2:** How many guests are expected at the event?
- 1. 15
- 2. 30
- 3. 50
- 4. 100
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 15: This number is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. 30: This number is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. 50: The woman clearly states "We're expecting about 50 people" when discussing the event details.
❌ 4. 100: This number is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q3:** What type of food service does the woman prefer?
- 1. A formal sit-down dinner
- 2. A casual cocktail reception
- 3. A buffet-style meal with appetizers
- 4. A food truck experience
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A formal sit-down dinner: The woman specifically says she doesn't want this option.
❌ 2. A casual cocktail reception: The woman says she wants something in between a sit-down dinner and a cocktail reception.
✅ 3. A buffet-style meal with appetizers: The woman states "Maybe some substantial appetizers and a few main dishes that people can serve themselves buffet-style."
❌ 4. A food truck experience: This option is not mentioned anywhere in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q3:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He owns the catering company
- 2. He is the head chef
- 3. He is a sales representative
- 4. He is an event planner
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He owns the catering company: There is no mention of him owning the company.
✅ 2. He is the head chef: The man introduces himself by saying "My name is Daniel, and I'm the head chef here."
❌ 3. He is a sales representative: He identifies himself as the head chef, not a sales representative.
❌ 4. He is an event planner: He identifies himself as the head chef, not an event planner.

**Q4:** How does the woman feel about the dessert options?
- 1. Concerned about their appearance
- 2. Worried about their cost
- 3. Pleased with their suitability
- 4. Disappointed by the limited selection
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Concerned about their appearance: The woman doesn't express any concerns about appearance.
❌ 2. Worried about their cost: The woman doesn't mention any concerns about cost regarding desserts.
✅ 3. Pleased with their suitability: The woman says the mini pavlovas look delicious and is pleased when told they can be made gluten-free, saying "Perfect" after hearing this information.
❌ 4. Disappointed by the limited selection: The woman doesn't express any disappointment about the dessert options.

**Q5:** What beverage option does the woman select?
- 1. Soft drinks and coffee only
- 2. A cash bar for alcoholic drinks
- 3. A hosted bar with alcoholic options
- 4. A limited selection of wines
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Soft drinks and coffee only: The woman doesn't select this option.
❌ 2. A cash bar for alcoholic drinks: The woman specifically chooses not to have a cash bar.
✅ 3. A hosted bar with alcoholic options: The woman clearly states "I think we'll go with the hosted bar option. Our CEO wants this to be a real celebration."
❌ 4. A limited selection of wines: This specific option is not mentioned in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q6:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She needs approval before finalizing the order
- 2. She has already paid the deposit
- 3. She is the company's CEO
- 4. She has used this catering service before

**Q7:** What will most likely happen next?

## Practice 462: Camping Gear Loan | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two friends discussing camping plans for a long weekend.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why does the woman need to borrow a tent?
- 1. Her tent is damaged.
- 2. She forgot to buy one.
- 3. Her tent is too small for her group.
- 4. She left her tent at another campsite.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Her tent is damaged: She specifically says "Our old one has a tear in it" and mentions concerns about rain.
❌ 2. She forgot to buy one: She already owns a tent, just one with a problem.
❌ 3. Her tent is too small for her group: She doesn't mention size as an issue with her tent.
❌ 4. She left her tent at another campsite: There's no mention of leaving equipment at another location.

**Q2:** What is the man doing during the long weekend?
- 1. Going to Muskoka.
- 2. Hosting family visitors.
- 3. Working in the city.
- 4. Joining the woman's camping trip.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Going to Muskoka: The man mentions going to Muskoka last year during a thunderstorm, not for the upcoming weekend.
✅ 2. Hosting family visitors: The man says "My sister's family is visiting from Vancouver, so I'll be playing tour guide around Toronto."
❌ 3. Working in the city: The man says he's staying in the city, but specifically to be a tour guide for his family, not to work.
❌ 4. Joining the woman's camping trip: The man clearly states he's not going camping when he says "I'm staying in the city this weekend."

**Q3:** What problem does the woman mention about her cooking equipment?
- 1. It's completely broken.
- 2. It's too heavy to carry.
- 3. It heats things very slowly.
- 4. It uses too much fuel.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's completely broken: She says it's working but has issues.
❌ 2. It's too heavy to carry: She doesn't mention weight as an issue.
✅ 3. It heats things very slowly: She specifically says "Ours is working but it's so old and takes forever to heat anything."
❌ 4. It uses too much fuel: She doesn't mention fuel consumption as a problem.

**Q4:** Based on the conversation, what does the man probably think about camping?
- 1. It's too uncomfortable to enjoy.
- 2. It's an activity he enjoys and is well-prepared for.
- 3. It's only enjoyable in good weather.
- 4. It's better as a group activity than a solo one.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's too uncomfortable to enjoy: The man speaks positively about camping and even offers extra equipment to make it more comfortable.
✅ 2. It's an activity he enjoys and is well-prepared for: The man mentions his previous camping experiences positively, owns quality camping gear (waterproof tent, portable stove, sleeping pad), and knows about camping conditions.
❌ 3. It's only enjoyable in good weather: The man mentions staying dry during a thunderstorm, suggesting he enjoys camping regardless of weather.
❌ 4. It's better as a group activity than a solo one: There's no indication in the conversation about his preference for group or solo camping.

**Q5:** What will likely happen after the woman returns from her trip?
- 1. She will buy new camping equipment.
- 2. She will plan another trip with the man.
- 3. She will host a meal with the man and his relatives.
- 4. She will visit Vancouver with the man.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She will buy new camping equipment: There's no mention of her planning to buy new equipment.
❌ 2. She will plan another trip with the man: They don't discuss planning a future camping trip together.
✅ 3. She will host a meal with the man and his relatives: The woman specifically invites the man and his sister's family for a barbecue after she returns, saying "when we get back, why don't you and your sister's family come over for a barbecue?"
❌ 4. She will visit Vancouver with the man: There's no mention of plans to visit Vancouver.

## Practice 465: Apartment Sublet Approval | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a tenant and a property manager about subletting an apartment.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why does the man want to sublet his apartment?
- 1. He is moving to a new city permanently.
- 2. He has a temporary work assignment in another city.
- 3. He can no longer afford the rent payments.
- 4. He wants to help his cousin find housing.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He is moving to a new city permanently: The man specifically states he'll be coming back in January.
✅ 2. He has a temporary work assignment in another city: The man clearly states "I've been offered a temporary work assignment in Vancouver that will last about four months."
❌ 3. He can no longer afford the rent payments: Financial difficulties are not mentioned as a reason for subletting.
❌ 4. He wants to help his cousin find housing: While his cousin is the potential subtenant, helping her find housing is not his primary motivation for subletting.

**Q2:** What will the property manager most likely do next?
- 1. Visit the apartment to assess its condition.
- 2. Call the man's cousin to arrange a meeting.
- 3. Send the subletting documents by email.
- 4. Process the $150 administrative fee.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Visit the apartment to assess its condition: There's no mention of needing to assess the apartment's condition.
❌ 2. Call the man's cousin to arrange a meeting: The property manager doesn't offer to contact the cousin directly.
✅ 3. Send the subletting documents by email: The property manager specifically says "I'll send everything to the email address we have on file" after the man requests the application forms and subletting agreement.
❌ 4. Process the $150 administrative fee: The fee will only be processed after the application is submitted, not immediately.

**Q3:** Who will the man's cousin need to pay the security deposit to?
- 1. The property management company.
- 2. The building's maintenance department.
- 3. The man directly.
- 4. No one, as it's already covered.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The property management company: The property manager states the current tenant's deposit will remain with them.
❌ 2. The building's maintenance department: The maintenance department is not mentioned in relation to security deposits.
✅ 3. The man directly: The property manager says "we recommend you collect a deposit from your subtenant directly" and that it would be "a private arrangement between you two."
❌ 4. No one, as it's already covered: While the man's original deposit remains with the property management, the property manager recommends collecting a separate deposit from the subtenant.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the man's relationship with his apartment?
- 1. He is dissatisfied with the living conditions.
- 2. He values keeping his current housing arrangement.
- 3. He finds the rent too expensive for his budget.
- 4. He has had problems with neighbors in the building.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He is dissatisfied with the living conditions: There's no indication of dissatisfaction with the apartment.
✅ 2. He values keeping his current housing arrangement: The man specifically states "I don't want to give up my apartment completely since I'll be coming back in January," showing he wants to maintain his current housing situation.
❌ 3. He finds the rent too expensive for his budget: There's no mention of financial concerns related to the apartment.
❌ 4. He has had problems with neighbors in the building: There's no mention of any issues with neighbors.

**Q5:** What is the main requirement the property manager emphasizes about subletting?
- 1. The subtenant must pay a higher monthly rent.
- 2. The original tenant remains responsible for the apartment.
- 3. The subletting period cannot exceed three months.
- 4. The subtenant must be a family member.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The subtenant must pay a higher monthly rent: There's no mention of increased rent for subtenants.
✅ 2. The original tenant remains responsible for the apartment: The property manager clearly states "your name would remain on the lease. You'd still be ultimately responsible if any issues arise."
❌ 3. The subletting period cannot exceed three months: The conversation mentions the man needs to sublet for four months, and the property manager doesn't object to this timeframe.
❌ 4. The subtenant must be a family member: While the man's cousin is the potential subtenant, there's no requirement that subtenants must be family members.

**Q6:** Why does the man ask if his cousin can view the subletting agreement?
- 1. To make sure she understands her responsibilities.
- 2. Because she doesn't trust the property manager.
- 3. To negotiate better terms for the sublet.
- 4. Because she's currently out of the country.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. To make sure she understands her responsibilities: The man asks if his cousin can view the agreement before proceeding, implying he wants her to understand what she's agreeing to.
❌ 2. Because she doesn't trust the property manager: There's no indication of distrust toward the property manager.
❌ 3. To negotiate better terms for the sublet: The man doesn't express any intention to negotiate the terms of the agreement.
❌ 4. Because she's currently out of the country: There's no mention of the cousin being out of the country.

## Practice 468: Backyard Skating Rink | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two neighbors discussing a winter project.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why is the woman clearing her backyard?
- 1. To build a playground for the children
- 2. To create space for a backyard ice rink
- 3. To install a new garden for the winter
- 4. To make room for lumber storage
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To build a playground for the children: There is no mention of building a playground in the conversation.
✅ 2. To create space for a backyard ice rink: The woman explicitly states they're clearing the yard to build an ice rink, saying "You need to create a level surface first, which is why we've been clearing the yard."
❌ 3. To install a new garden for the winter: There is no mention of a garden project in the conversation.
❌ 4. To make room for lumber storage: While lumber is mentioned, it's for building the rink frame, not for storage.

**Q2:** What weather condition is needed before they can start filling the rink?
- 1. At least a week of snow
- 2. Temperatures below zero for one day
- 3. Three consecutive days below -5°C
- 4. A full day of rain followed by freezing
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. At least a week of snow: The conversation doesn't mention needing snow before filling the rink.
❌ 2. Temperatures below zero for one day: This is incorrect as the woman specifically mentions needing more than one day of cold temperatures.
✅ 3. Three consecutive days below -5°C: The woman explicitly states "Mike says we need at least three consecutive days below -5°C before we can start filling it."
❌ 4. A full day of rain followed by freezing: There is no mention of needing rain before filling the rink.

**Q3:** What is the woman's husband's connection to backyard ice rinks?
- 1. He builds them professionally
- 2. He had one when he was younger
- 3. He recently learned about them online
- 4. He played hockey on them as an adult
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He builds them professionally: There's no indication that the husband builds rinks professionally.
✅ 2. He had one when he was younger: The woman specifically says "My husband grew up with one and has been talking about it for ages."
❌ 3. He recently learned about them online: There's no mention of him learning about rinks online.
❌ 4. He played hockey on them as an adult: While hockey is mentioned in the conversation, there's no indication that the husband played as an adult on backyard rinks.

**Q4:** How does the woman feel about neighborhood children using the rink?
- 1. She's concerned about liability issues
- 2. She wants to limit access to weekends only
- 3. She's open to sharing it with neighbors
- 4. She prefers to keep it for family use
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She's concerned about liability issues: The woman doesn't express any concerns about liability in the conversation.
❌ 2. She wants to limit access to weekends only: While she mentions weekend skating parties, she doesn't restrict access to only weekends.
✅ 3. She's open to sharing it with neighbors: The woman clearly states "Once we get it set up, your kids are more than welcome to come over and use it. We could even organize some neighborhood skating parties on weekends."
❌ 4. She prefers to keep it for family use: This contradicts her invitation to the neighbor's children and suggestion of neighborhood skating parties.

**Q5:** What will the man likely do this weekend?
- 1. Take his children skating at Riverside Arena
- 2. Help the woman's family build the rink frame
- 3. Purchase materials to build his own rink
- 4. Research ice rink maintenance techniques
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Take his children skating at Riverside Arena: While the Riverside Arena is mentioned, there's no indication he plans to take his children there this weekend.
✅ 2. Help the woman's family build the rink frame: The woman says "We're planning to start construction this Saturday morning if you're free" and the man had offered to help, saying "Let me know when you're building the frame - I'd be happy to lend a hand."
❌ 3. Purchase materials to build his own rink: While he mentions possibly building his own rink next winter, there's no indication he's purchasing materials this weekend.
❌ 4. Research ice rink maintenance techniques: While maintenance is discussed, there's no indication he plans to research techniques this weekend.

- 4. She travels too much to care for them
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She doesn't have enough space for them: Space concerns aren't mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. She hasn't been successful with them in the past: The woman directly states "Do you think they're difficult to care for? My track record with houseplants isn't exactly stellar" indicating past difficulties with plant care.
❌ 3. She's allergic to certain varieties: Allergies aren't mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. She travels too much to care for them: Travel isn't mentioned as a concern in the conversation.

**Q4:** What does the man probably think about the woman's final decision?
- 1. She's spending too much money
- 2. She's making a practical choice
- 3. She should wait until after the holidays
- 4. She's becoming enthusiastic about plants
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She's spending too much money: The man actually points out the 20% discount, encouraging her purchases.
❌ 2. She's making a practical choice: While her choice may be practical, this isn't what the man's reaction indicates.
❌ 3. She should wait until after the holidays: The man never suggests waiting; he's supportive of her immediate purchases.
✅ 4. She's becoming enthusiastic about plants: When the woman decides to get multiple plants including the amaryllis for after the holidays, the man says "Now you're thinking like a true plant enthusiast!" showing he thinks she's developing enthusiasm for plants.

**Q5:** What will the woman likely do after their conversation?
- 1. Look for different plants at another store
- 2. Purchase multiple plants and possibly decorative pots
- 3. Ask for more care instructions before buying
- 4. Wait until after her dinner party to buy plants
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Look for different plants at another store: The woman has decided on plants at this store and mentions the discount.
✅ 2. Purchase multiple plants and possibly decorative pots: The woman decides to get both the Christmas cactus and mini pine, plus possibly an amaryllis, and the man suggests looking at decorative pots which she doesn't reject.
❌ 3. Ask for more care instructions before buying: While care is discussed, there's no indication she'll seek more instructions before purchasing.
❌ 4. Wait until after her dinner party to buy plants: She specifically wants plants for her upcoming dinner party, so waiting doesn't make sense.

## Practice 474: EasyMove Packing Consultation | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a customer and a moving service representative.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why did the woman contact the moving service?
- 1. She needs help with the physical transportation of her belongings.
- 2. She wants assistance with organizing and packing before her move.
- 3. She is looking for storage solutions during her transition.
- 4. She needs recommendations for reliable moving companies.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She needs help with the physical transportation of her belongings.: The service specifically does not handle the actual moving, as the man stated they 'help prepare for moves without actually doing the moving part.'
✅ 2. She wants assistance with organizing and packing before her move.: The woman explicitly stated she's 'feeling overwhelmed with all the packing and organizing' and confirmed she heard they offer services to help prepare for moves.
❌ 3. She is looking for storage solutions during her transition.: There was no mention of storage needs in the conversation.
❌ 4. She needs recommendations for reliable moving companies.: The woman already has movers arranged for May 15th and did not ask for recommendations.

**Q2:** Why does the woman prefer a weekend appointment?
- 1. She wants her friends to be able to help as well.
- 2. The service offers discounted rates on weekends.
- 3. Her work schedule makes weekdays difficult.
- 4. The new apartment is only available for viewing on weekends.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She wants her friends to be able to help as well.: There is no mention of friends helping in the conversation.
❌ 2. The service offers discounted rates on weekends.: No weekend discount was mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. Her work schedule makes weekdays difficult.: The woman specifically stated, 'I work long hours and don't have much time during weekdays to pack properly,' which is why she prefers a weekend appointment.
❌ 4. The new apartment is only available for viewing on weekends.: There is no mention of apartment viewing availability in the conversation.

**Q3:** What specific items did the woman mention needing special care?
- 1. Electronics and artwork
- 2. Furniture and clothing
- 3. Books and kitchen items
- 4. Plants and fragile decorations
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Electronics and artwork: The woman did not mention electronics or artwork in the conversation.
❌ 2. Furniture and clothing: While she mentioned not having many large furniture pieces, she didn't express concern about furniture or clothing needing special care.
✅ 3. Books and kitchen items: The woman specifically stated, 'I do have quite a collection of books and kitchen items that need careful packing.'
❌ 4. Plants and fragile decorations: There was no mention of plants or decorations in the conversation.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the woman's approach to moving?
- 1. She is primarily concerned about keeping costs minimal.
- 2. She values organization and proper preparation.
- 3. She prefers to handle most aspects herself.
- 4. She has negative experiences with previous movers.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She is primarily concerned about keeping costs minimal.: While she mentioned budgeting, her main concern appears to be proper preparation despite her busy schedule.
✅ 2. She values organization and proper preparation.: The woman is seeking professional help specifically for organizing and packing because she doesn't have time to 'pack properly' herself, indicating she values proper preparation.
❌ 3. She prefers to handle most aspects herself.: The conversation shows she's specifically hiring help because she cannot handle the packing herself due to work commitments.
❌ 4. She has negative experiences with previous movers.: There is no mention of previous moving experiences in the conversation.

**Q5:** What will the woman most likely do after this conversation?
- 1. Pay the deposit and wait for the confirmation email.
- 2. Purchase additional packing supplies on her own.
- 3. Contact the actual moving company to coordinate.
- 4. Request a different service package with more packers.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Pay the deposit and wait for the confirmation email.: The woman agreed to book the service and the man stated they require a 25% deposit to secure the booking, with the balance due on the day of service. He also mentioned sending a confirmation email.
❌ 2. Purchase additional packing supplies on her own.: The man clearly stated that their standard package includes all necessary packing materials, and the woman seemed satisfied with this.
❌ 3. Contact the actual moving company to coordinate.: There's no indication in the conversation that she needs to contact the moving company at this point.
❌ 4. Request a different service package with more packers.: The woman expressed that the standard package sounded 'perfect' and proceeded to book it without requesting changes.

**Q6:** Why did the man mention the 10% discount?
- 1. To encourage the woman to book additional services.
- 2. To inform her of a benefit she automatically qualifies for.
- 3. To compete with another company's pricing.
- 4. To compensate for a scheduling inconvenience.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To encourage the woman to book additional services.: The man did not try to upsell additional services when mentioning the discount.
✅ 2. To inform her of a benefit she automatically qualifies for.: The man stated, 'We also offer a 10% discount if you book more than two weeks in advance, which you would qualify for,' indicating he was simply informing her of a benefit she already qualified for.
❌ 3. To compete with another company's pricing.: There was no mention of competitors' pricing in the conversation.
❌ 4. To compensate for a scheduling inconvenience.: No scheduling inconvenience was mentioned in the conversation.

❌ 3. Wilson's Farm: This is the name of the farm that supplies strawberries to the store, not the store itself.

✅ 2. Parking can be difficult to find: The man specifically warns her that "parking might be tricky" and that he "had to circle around for about 10 minutes to find a spot," which is why she decides to walk instead.
❌ 3. She prefers public transportation: The woman doesn't mention preferring public transportation.
❌ 4. The store has no parking lot: The conversation doesn't state that the store has no parking lot, only that parking can be difficult to find.

**Q4:** Based on the conversation, what does the woman's daughter seem to care about?

- 2. Supporting local farmers

❌ 2. Supporting local farmers: While the store sells local produce, the woman doesn't mention her daughter caring specifically about supporting local farmers.
✅ 3. Healthy eating options: The woman specifically asks about organic products because her "daughter's been on this health kick lately," indicating her daughter is focused on healthy eating.
❌ 4. Freshly baked bread: The conversation doesn't connect the daughter with any interest in freshly baked bread.

**Q5:** What will likely happen when the woman visits the store?
- 1. She will meet the man's wife for the first time.
- 2. She will take advantage of the opening discount.
- 3. She will apply for a job at the café.
- 4. She will complain about the high prices.

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She will meet the man's wife for the first time: While the man mentions his wife might go to the store, there's no indication the woman will meet her for the first time.
✅ 2. She will take advantage of the opening discount: The man mentions "they're having some kind of grand opening special this weekend - I think 15% off if you spend more than $50," and the woman responds "Perfect timing then! I need to do my weekly shopping anyway," indicating she plans to use the discount.
❌ 3. She will apply for a job at the café: There's no mention of the woman looking for a job at the café or anywhere else.
❌ 4. She will complain about the high prices: While the man mentions prices are higher than Safeway, the woman accepts this as expected for specialty stores and shows no indication she will complain.

## Practice 480: Residence Check-in Desk | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a student and a university residence coordinator.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why did Daniel come to see the residence coordinator?
- 1. To request a different room assignment.
- 2. To learn about the move-in procedure.
- 3. To pay his security deposit in person.
- 4. To report damage in his assigned room.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To request a different room assignment: Daniel never mentions wanting to change rooms.
✅ 2. To learn about the move-in procedure: Daniel states "I'm not sure about the check-in process" and mentions he "wasn't given many details about what I need to do first."
❌ 3. To pay his security deposit in person: The coordinator confirms Daniel already paid his deposit online last month.
❌ 4. To report damage in his assigned room: Daniel hasn't moved in yet, so he couldn't report damage.

**Q2:** What is Daniel most likely to do immediately after this conversation?
- 1. Attend the floor meeting in the common room.
- 2. Complete the room condition form at the front desk.
- 3. Borrow a cart and move his belongings to his room.
- 4. Meet with his Residence Advisor to discuss rules.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Attend the floor meeting in the common room: The floor meeting is at 7:00 PM, not immediately after their conversation.
❌ 2. Complete the room condition form at the front desk: He needs to complete this within 24 hours of moving in, not immediately.
✅ 3. Borrow a cart and move his belongings to his room: Daniel's last statement is "I'll head over to the east entrance and get started with moving in," and the coordinator mentioned carts are available at the front desk.
❌ 4. Meet with his Residence Advisor to discuss rules: The meeting with the RA is scheduled for 7:00 PM at the floor meeting.

**Q3:** Which entrance should Daniel use to move into his residence?
- 1. The main entrance of the university.
- 2. The west entrance of Maple Hall.
- 3. The entrance nearest to the front desk.
- 4. The east entrance of Maple Hall.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The main entrance of the university: This entrance is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. The west entrance of Maple Hall: The coordinator specifically mentions the east entrance, not the west.
❌ 3. The entrance nearest to the front desk: This entrance is not specified in the conversation.
✅ 4. The east entrance of Maple Hall: The coordinator clearly states "For Maple Hall, you'll want to use the east entrance, which has a ramp."

**Q4:** Why is Daniel advised to be thorough when completing the room condition form?
- 1. To avoid being charged for pre-existing damage.
- 2. To help maintenance staff prioritize repairs.
- 3. To demonstrate his attention to detail to staff.
- 4. To ensure his roommate takes responsibility for their space.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. To avoid being charged for pre-existing damage: The coordinator explains it "protects you from being charged for problems that were already there when you moved in."
❌ 2. To help maintenance staff prioritize repairs: Prioritizing repairs is not mentioned as a purpose of the form.
❌ 3. To demonstrate his attention to detail to staff: This reason is not given in the conversation.
❌ 4. To ensure his roommate takes responsibility for their space: A roommate is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q5:** What is the main purpose of the floor meeting mentioned in the conversation?
- 1. To assign cleaning duties to residents.
- 2. To introduce students to university faculty.
- 3. To provide information about residence rules and safety.
- 4. To organize social activities for the semester.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To assign cleaning duties to residents: Cleaning duties are not mentioned as part of the floor meeting.
❌ 2. To introduce students to university faculty: Introduction to faculty is not mentioned as a purpose of the meeting.
✅ 3. To provide information about residence rules and safety: The coordinator explicitly states the RA "will go over important information about fire safety, building rules, and upcoming events."
❌ 4. To organize social activities for the semester: While the coordinator mentions it's a chance to meet neighbors, organizing social activities is not stated as the main purpose.

**Q6:** Why does Daniel need to leave his student ID at the front desk?
- 1. As verification for his room assignment.
- 2. To register for the mandatory floor meeting.
- 3. As a deposit to borrow a moving cart.
- 4. To activate his room key for the first time.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. As verification for his room assignment: His room assignment is already verified in the system.
❌ 2. To register for the mandatory floor meeting: Registration for the floor meeting is not mentioned.
✅ 3. As a deposit to borrow a moving cart: The coordinator specifically states "We have moving carts available at the residence front desk – just leave your student ID as a deposit."
❌ 4. To activate his room key for the first time: There's no mention of needing to activate the room key.

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It has a large deck overlooking the water: While the cottage has this feature, it's not given as a reason for the high price.
✅ 2. It's during the busiest rental season: The woman explains "July is peak cottage season in Ontario" as the reason why more affordable places are already booked, implying this is why prices are high.
❌ 3. It includes kayaks and a canoe: These are mentioned as activities available, not as justification for the price.
❌ 4. It's a newly renovated property: There is no mention of the cottage being newly renovated in the conversation.

**Q2:** How many people will be staying at the cottage?
- 1. Four people
- 2. Seven people
- 3. Nine people
- 4. Ten people
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Four people: This is only the size of the man's family, not the total group.
❌ 2. Seven people: This is not the correct total number mentioned.
✅ 3. Nine people: The woman specifically states "Your family of four, my family of three, plus mom and dad. That's nine total, so we should be fine."
❌ 4. Ten people: While the cottage can sleep up to ten people, the actual number of family members staying is nine.

**Q3:** What concern does the man express about the cottage?
- 1. It might not have enough activities for the children.
- 2. It's too far from grocery stores.
- 3. The deposit is too expensive.
- 4. It doesn't have enough parking space.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It might not have enough activities for the children: The man does ask about activities, but this is a separate question, not his initial concern.
❌ 2. It's too far from grocery stores: The man never expresses concern about distance to stores.
✅ 3. The deposit is too expensive: The man specifically says "I'm a bit concerned about the price. $450 per night seems steep, especially if we're staying Friday through Sunday. That's nearly $1,400 with taxes and everything."
❌ 4. It doesn't have enough parking space: Parking is never mentioned in the conversation.

**Q4:** What does the woman think about the bakery in Bala?
- 1. It's overpriced but worth visiting.
- 2. It makes exceptional desserts.
- 3. It's convenient but not special.
- 4. It's the only food option in the area.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's overpriced but worth visiting: The woman doesn't mention anything about the bakery being overpriced.
✅ 2. It makes exceptional desserts: The woman says "they have this amazing bakery there that makes the best butter tarts you've ever tasted," indicating she thinks their desserts are exceptional.
❌ 3. It's convenient but not special: The woman describes it as "amazing" with the "best butter tarts," showing she thinks it's special, not just convenient.
❌ 4. It's the only food option in the area: The woman mentions it's in a general store area but doesn't say it's the only food option available.

**Q5:** What will the siblings likely do to prepare for the trip?
- 1. Buy new outdoor equipment.
- 2. Coordinate what items each family will bring.
- 3. Visit the cottage beforehand to check it out.
- 4. Ask their parents to handle the meal planning.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Buy new outdoor equipment: There's no mention of buying new equipment for the trip.
✅ 2. Coordinate what items each family will bring: The woman suggests "maybe we should start a shared document for planning meals and who's bringing what? I don't think we need four coolers and three portable BBQs," and the man agrees to set this up.
❌ 3. Visit the cottage beforehand to check it out: There's no mention of visiting the cottage before their stay.
❌ 4. Ask their parents to handle the meal planning: The siblings are planning to coordinate the meals themselves, not delegate this to their parents.

## Practice 485: Riverside Garden Rules | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman about a community garden.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why did the woman visit the community garden?
- 1. She wants to volunteer at the garden.
- 2. She is looking for gardening advice.
- 3. She hopes to secure a garden plot.
- 4. She wants to attend a community event.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She wants to volunteer at the garden.: The woman never mentions wanting to volunteer; she specifically says she's interested in getting a plot.
❌ 2. She is looking for gardening advice.: The woman doesn't ask for gardening advice during the conversation.
✅ 3. She hopes to secure a garden plot.: The woman clearly states "I'm interested in getting a plot for the growing season" when explaining why she came to the garden.
❌ 4. She wants to attend a community event.: The woman doesn't mention wanting to attend any community events as her reason for visiting.

**Q2:** Why does the woman prefer a sunny plot?
- 1. She wants to grow flowers that need full sunlight.
- 2. She plans to grow vegetables that require direct sun.
- 3. She dislikes the maple trees on the east side.
- 4. She wants to be near the south fence for convenience.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She wants to grow flowers that need full sunlight.: The woman doesn't mention wanting to grow flowers that need sunlight.
✅ 2. She plans to grow vegetables that require direct sun.: The woman specifically states "I'm planning to grow tomatoes and peppers, and they need full sun" as her reason for preferring a sunny plot.
❌ 3. She dislikes the maple trees on the east side.: The woman doesn't express any dislike for the maple trees.
❌ 4. She wants to be near the south fence for convenience.: The woman doesn't mention convenience as a factor in her preference for a sunny plot.

**Q3:** What is one rule gardeners must follow at this community garden?
- 1. They must attend monthly potluck dinners.
- 2. They can only grow vegetables, not flowers.
- 3. They need to use organic gardening methods.
- 4. They have to provide their own gardening tools.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They must attend monthly potluck dinners.: The potluck dinners are presented as optional, not a rule.
❌ 2. They can only grow vegetables, not flowers.: The man specifically mentions gardeners can grow "vegetables, herbs, or flowers," so this is not a rule.
✅ 3. They need to use organic gardening methods.: The man clearly states "We also ask that everyone use organic gardening practices - no chemical pesticides or fertilizers," making this a requirement for all gardeners.
❌ 4. They have to provide their own gardening tools.: The man explains that shared tools are available in the tool shed, so providing your own tools is not required.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the woman's gardening experience?
- 1. She has never gardened before moving to this neighborhood.
- 2. She has previously used chemical fertilizers in her garden.
- 3. She has some experience with organic gardening practices.
- 4. She prefers growing flowers rather than vegetables.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She has never gardened before moving to this neighborhood.: There's no indication this is her first time gardening; in fact, her comments suggest previous experience.
❌ 2. She has previously used chemical fertilizers in her garden.: She states "I've always gardened organically," which contradicts this option.
✅ 3. She has some experience with organic gardening practices.: When the man mentions organic gardening requirements, she responds "That works perfectly for me. I've always gardened organically," clearly indicating prior experience with organic methods.
❌ 4. She prefers growing flowers rather than vegetables.: She specifically mentions planning to grow tomatoes and peppers, indicating a preference for vegetables, not flowers.

**Q5:** What is the main advice the man gives about getting started?
- 1. The woman should attend the mandatory orientation session.
- 2. The woman should purchase her own gardening tools.
- 3. The woman should meet with experienced gardeners first.
- 4. The woman should start with easy-to-grow plants.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. The woman should attend the mandatory orientation session.: The man specifically states there's "a brief orientation session this Saturday at 10 AM, which is mandatory for new members" before she can start planting.
❌ 2. The woman should purchase her own gardening tools.: While the man mentions many gardeners bring their own tools, he says shared tools are available and doesn't advise her to purchase her own.
❌ 3. The woman should meet with experienced gardeners first.: The man doesn't suggest meeting with experienced gardeners as a requirement to get started.
❌ 4. The woman should start with easy-to-grow plants.: The man doesn't give any advice about what types of plants the woman should grow.

**Q6:** Why does the man mention the monthly potluck dinners?
- 1. To explain a mandatory community garden requirement.
- 2. To inform the woman about an additional social opportunity.
- 3. To suggest a way for the woman to learn gardening skills.
- 4. To encourage the woman to grow more vegetables than herbs.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To explain a mandatory community garden requirement.: The potluck dinners are not described as mandatory; the man says she "might also be interested" in them.
✅ 2. To inform the woman about an additional social opportunity.: The man mentions the potlucks as something she might be interested in once she's settled in, presenting it as an optional social activity where "everyone brings dishes made with garden produce."
❌ 3. To suggest a way for the woman to learn gardening skills.: The man doesn't connect the potlucks to learning gardening skills.
❌ 4. To encourage the woman to grow more vegetables than herbs.: The man doesn't suggest what the woman should grow or connect the potlucks to her planting choices.

## Practice 488: Oakridge Crescent Barbecue | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two neighbours planning a backyard BBQ.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Where will the BBQ take place?
- 1. at Maple Grove Market
- 2. at Mark's house
- 3. at Sarah's house
- 4. at Elmwood Drive Park
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. at Maple Grove Market: Maple Grove Market is mentioned as where Mark will buy food, not where the BBQ will be held.
✅ 2. at Mark's house: Mark says "My deck doesn't have enough seating for everyone" which indicates the BBQ will be at his house.
❌ 3. at Sarah's house: There's no indication the BBQ will be at Sarah's house.
❌ 4. at Elmwood Drive Park: Elmwood Drive is mentioned as the location of Maple Grove Market, not as the BBQ location.

**Q2:** Why are they including vegetarian options in their menu?
- 1. Mark is a vegetarian.
- 2. Sarah prefers vegetarian food.
- 3. New neighbors don't eat meat.
- 4. To offer more variety.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Mark is a vegetarian: There's no indication in the conversation that Mark is vegetarian.
❌ 2. Sarah prefers vegetarian food: There's no mention of Sarah preferring vegetarian food.
✅ 3. New neighbors don't eat meat: Sarah specifically says "The Patels moved in last month, and I think they're vegetarian" as the reason for including vegetarian options.
❌ 4. To offer more variety: While they do discuss various food options, the specific reason for vegetarian options is because of the Patels.

**Q3:** What will Sarah be responsible for preparing?
- 1. meat and drinks
- 2. sides and dessert
- 3. activities and seating
- 4. invitations and decorations
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. meat and drinks: Mark says "I'll get the meat and veggie options from Maple Grove, plus drinks and ice."
✅ 2. sides and dessert: Mark confirms the division of responsibilities: "So just to confirm, I'll get the meat and veggie options from Maple Grove, plus drinks and ice, and you'll handle the sides and dessert?"
❌ 3. activities and seating: While Sarah mentions having water balloons, there's no clear assignment of activities preparation, and Mark asks people to bring their own chairs.
❌ 4. invitations and decorations: Sarah will send reminder emails, but there's no mention of decorations.

**Q4:** What does Sarah probably think about Mark's planning abilities?
- 1. He is disorganized and forgetful.
- 2. He is too focused on details.
- 3. He is reliable and thoughtful.
- 4. He spends too much money on supplies.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He is disorganized and forgetful: There's no evidence of Mark being disorganized or forgetful in the conversation.
❌ 2. He is too focused on details: While Mark is thorough in planning, there's no indication Sarah thinks he's overly focused on details.
✅ 3. He is reliable and thoughtful: Mark shows reliability by getting flyers, planning to purchase food, and thoughtfulness by remembering details like sunscreen and bug spray. Sarah responds positively to his suggestions.
❌ 4. He spends too much money on supplies: There's no mention of Mark overspending or Sarah being concerned about costs.

**Q5:** What will likely happen at the BBQ?
- 1. Children will help make ice cream.
- 2. The Patels will bring their own food.
- 3. Mark will demonstrate grilling techniques.
- 4. Sarah will cancel due to the heat.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Children will help make ice cream: The woman says "We could set up that old ice cream maker my husband got last year. The kids would love helping with that."
❌ 2. The Patels will bring their own food: The conversation mentions the Patels are vegetarian, but doesn't say they'll bring their own food. Instead, Mark and Sarah plan to provide vegetarian options.
❌ 3. Mark will demonstrate grilling techniques: There's no mention of Mark demonstrating grilling techniques in the conversation.
❌ 4. Sarah will cancel due to the heat: Despite the heat (30 degrees), Sarah is enthusiastic about the BBQ, saying "This is going to be such a fun afternoon! I'm really looking forward to it."
