## Practice 489: Childcare Hours Adjustment | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between an employee and a manager about adjusting a work schedule.

### Audio Transcript
$4c

**Q1:** Why does the woman want to change her work schedule?
- 1. She wants to reduce her working hours.
- 2. She needs to accommodate her daughter's childcare situation.
- 3. She prefers starting work earlier in the morning.
- 4. She wants to avoid rush hour traffic.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She wants to reduce her working hours.: The woman specifically states she would still complete her full 8-hour workday, just with different hours.
✅ 2. She needs to accommodate her daughter's childcare situation.: The woman explains her daughter's after-school program is ending and the new provider can only watch her until 4:30 pm, necessitating the schedule change.
❌ 3. She prefers starting work earlier in the morning.: While she does propose starting earlier, this is to accommodate childcare needs, not personal preference.
❌ 4. She wants to avoid rush hour traffic.: There is no mention of traffic concerns in the conversation.

**Q2:** What will the woman most likely do after this conversation?
- 1. Look for a different childcare provider.
- 2. Start working her new schedule immediately.
- 3. Send an email confirming the schedule details.
- 4. Discuss the changes with her team members.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Look for a different childcare provider.: She has already found a new provider and is adjusting her schedule to accommodate their hours.
❌ 2. Start working her new schedule immediately.: The manager suggests starting next Monday, not immediately.
✅ 3. Send an email confirming the schedule details.: The manager specifically asks her to "send me an email confirming these details, and I'll make sure HR is informed of the change."
❌ 4. Discuss the changes with her team members.: There is no mention of her needing to discuss this with team members.

**Q3:** When will the woman's new schedule begin?
- 1. Tomorrow
- 2. Next Monday
- 3. Next month
- 4. When the school year starts
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Tomorrow: The manager does not suggest starting tomorrow.
✅ 2. Next Monday: The manager specifically says "Why don't we try this new schedule starting next Monday?"
❌ 3. Next month: The conversation mentions her daughter's program is ending next month, but her schedule change will start next Monday.
❌ 4. When the school year starts: There is no mention of waiting until the school year starts.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the company's approach to employee needs?
- 1. They only accommodate employees with children.
- 2. They prioritize client meetings over employee schedules.
- 3. They are generally flexible with long-term employees.
- 4. They require formal HR approval for any schedule changes.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They only accommodate employees with children.: While they are accommodating this employee with a child, there's no indication this is the only circumstance they would be flexible.
❌ 2. They prioritize client meetings over employee schedules.: The manager mentions client meetings, but the woman offers to be flexible for these, showing a balanced approach.
✅ 3. They are generally flexible with long-term employees.: The manager mentions "We do have some flexibility with schedules, especially for valued team members like yourself" and later says "I believe in supporting our team members' work-life balance whenever possible."
❌ 4. They require formal HR approval for any schedule changes.: The manager will inform HR of the change, but there's no indication formal approval is required.

**Q5:** What solution does the woman propose regarding lunch breaks?
- 1. Taking her lunch break earlier in the day
- 2. Skipping lunch on busy days
- 3. Reducing her lunch break to 30 minutes
- 4. Taking lunch at her desk while working
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Taking her lunch break earlier in the day: She doesn't suggest changing the time, only the duration.
❌ 2. Skipping lunch on busy days: There is no mention of skipping lunch.
✅ 3. Reducing her lunch break to 30 minutes: She specifically says "I'd prefer to take a shorter lunch break from 12:00 to 12:30" which is a reduction from the standard hour lunch.
❌ 4. Taking lunch at her desk while working: There is no mention of working through lunch.

**Q6:** Why does the manager mention Tuesday and Thursday morning meetings?
- 1. To remind the woman of her responsibilities
- 2. To check if she can still participate despite her new hours
- 3. To suggest moving these meetings to a different time
- 4. To explain why her request might be difficult to accommodate
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To remind the woman of her responsibilities: The manager isn't simply reminding her of duties but checking if her new schedule will work with these meetings.
✅ 2. To check if she can still participate despite her new hours: The manager specifically asks "Would you be able to dial in for those if you've already started your day at 8:00?"
❌ 3. To suggest moving these meetings to a different time: There is no suggestion to move the meeting times.
❌ 4. To explain why her request might be difficult to accommodate: The manager is working through logistics, not suggesting her request is difficult to accommodate.

## Practice 491: Furniture Assembly Booking | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman. The woman is a customer who has purchased furniture that needs to be assembled, and the man works for a furniture assembly service.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q1:** What furniture items does the woman need assembled?
- 1. A desk, a bookshelf, and a TV stand
- 2. A bookshelf, a dining table with chairs, and a TV stand
- 3. A bed frame, a dining table, and a bookshelf
- 4. A TV stand, a sofa, and a dining table with chairs
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A desk, a bookshelf, and a TV stand: A desk is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. A bookshelf, a dining table with chairs, and a TV stand: The woman specifically lists these items when she says, "Well, I have a bookshelf, a dining table with four chairs, and a TV stand."
❌ 3. A bed frame, a dining table, and a bookshelf: A bed frame is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. A TV stand, a sofa, and a dining table with chairs: A sofa is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q2:** How much will the basic assembly service cost?
- 1. $$90
- 2. $$100
- 3. $$120
- 4. $$150
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $90: This amount is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. $100: This amount is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. $120: The man specifically states, "Our basic service covers assembly of up to three items for $120, which would work for your situation."
❌ 4. $150: This amount is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q3:** How does the woman feel about assembling the furniture herself?
- 1. Confident but lacking time
- 2. Uncertain about her abilities
- 3. Excited to learn new skills
- 4. Frustrated by previous attempts
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Confident but lacking time: She never mentions time constraints as an issue.
✅ 2. Uncertain about her abilities: The woman states, "I'm not very handy with tools, so I was hoping to get some help" and also mentions "I tried to open one box, but honestly, there were so many parts and the instructions looked complicated."
❌ 3. Excited to learn new skills: She shows no interest in learning to assemble furniture herself.
❌ 4. Frustrated by previous attempts: While she did try to open one box, she doesn't express frustration, just uncertainty about the complexity.

**Q4:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He is new to the furniture assembly business
- 2. He is familiar with Modern Home Furnishings products
- 3. He will personally assemble the woman's furniture
- 4. He offers a discount for the inconvenience
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He is new to the furniture assembly business: Nothing in the conversation suggests he is new to the business.
✅ 2. He is familiar with Modern Home Furnishings products: The man explicitly states "I'm familiar with their products" when the woman mentions Modern Home Furnishings.
❌ 3. He will personally assemble the woman's furniture: He mentions "our technician" will arrive, indicating someone else will do the assembly.
❌ 4. He offers a discount for the inconvenience: He mentions an additional charge of $50 for the second visit, not a discount.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Great! I've got you scheduled for tomorrow at 2 PM. Now, I just need to confirm your address and contact information.
WOMAN: Sure. It's 247 Maple Avenue, Apartment 304. My phone number is 604-555-8921.
MAN: Perfect. And your name for the booking?
WOMAN: Jennifer Lawson.
MAN: Thanks, Jennifer. Now, there are a couple of things to prepare before our technician arrives. Make sure all the boxes are in the rooms where you want the furniture assembled, and that there's enough clear space to work.
WOMAN: Oh, that's good to know. The boxes are still in the living room. I'll move them to the appropriate rooms tonight.
MAN: Excellent. Also, did you check if all the boxes have all their parts? Sometimes pieces can be missing.
WOMAN: Um, to be honest, I haven't opened most of them yet. Should I check everything?
MAN: If you could at least verify that none of the boxes appear damaged, that would help. If there are missing parts, we might need to reschedule until replacements arrive.

**Q3:** What is the woman's apartment number?
- 1. 204
- 2. 247
- 3. 304
- 4. 347
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 204: This is not the apartment number mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. 247: This is the street address (247 Maple Avenue), not the apartment number.
✅ 3. 304: The woman states her address as "247 Maple Avenue, Apartment 304."
❌ 4. 347: This number is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q4:** What does the man recommend the woman do before the technician arrives?
- 1. Pay the full amount in advance
- 2. Purchase additional tools
- 3. Move the boxes to their appropriate rooms
- 4. Attempt to start the assembly herself
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Pay the full amount in advance: No advance payment is mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. Purchase additional tools: The man doesn't suggest buying any tools.
✅ 3. Move the boxes to their appropriate rooms: The man specifically advises, "Make sure all the boxes are in the rooms where you want the furniture assembled," and the woman responds that she'll move them from the living room to the appropriate rooms.
❌ 4. Attempt to start the assembly herself: The man never suggests she should start assembling anything herself.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q6:** What problem does the woman discover with her furniture?
- 1. The dining table is the wrong size
- 2. The bookshelf is missing several parts
- 3. The TV stand has a cracked glass shelf
- 4. The chairs are a different color than ordered
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The dining table is the wrong size: No issues with the dining table are mentioned.
❌ 2. The bookshelf is missing several parts: No issues with the bookshelf are mentioned.
✅ 3. The TV stand has a cracked glass shelf: The woman states, "I noticed that the TV stand box is pretty badly damaged. When I opened it, I found that one of the glass shelves is cracked."
❌ 4. The chairs are a different color than ordered: No issues with the chairs' color are mentioned.

**Q7:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The woman will cancel the entire service
- 2. The technician will arrive to assemble two furniture items
- 3. The man will help the woman file a complaint
- 4. The woman will attempt to fix the glass shelf herself

✅ 2. The technician will arrive to assemble two furniture items: The woman and man agree that the technician will come at 2 PM to assemble the bookshelf and dining set, while postponing the TV stand assembly.
❌ 3. The man will help the woman file a complaint: The woman has already contacted Modern Home Furnishings about the damaged part.

**Q1:** Why is the woman interested in job shadowing at this company?
- 1. She wants to fulfill a program requirement.
- 2. She needs to improve her digital marketing skills.
- 3. She is looking for a full-time position after graduation.
- 4. She was referred by her data analysis professor.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. She wants to fulfill a program requirement: The woman states, "My program requires 20 hours of job shadowing experience" as her primary reason.
❌ 2. She needs to improve her digital marketing skills: While she's interested in digital marketing, she doesn't mention needing to improve her skills.
❌ 3. She is looking for a full-time position after graduation: There is no mention of seeking employment at the company.
❌ 4. She was referred by her data analysis professor: While she mentions her professor emphasizes metrics, there's no indication the professor referred her to this company.

**Q2:** What will the woman most likely do after this conversation?
- 1. Prepare a list of questions about analytics.
- 2. Send her resume to the HR coordinator.
- 3. Contact Michael Wong directly.
- 4. Change her class schedule for Thursdays.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Prepare a list of questions about analytics: While she will need to prepare questions, she specifically mentions sending her resume first.
✅ 2. Send her resume to the HR coordinator: The woman explicitly states, "I'll email you my resume and contact information today" at the end of the conversation.
❌ 3. Contact Michael Wong directly: There's no indication she'll contact Michael directly; the HR coordinator will inform Michael about her visit.
❌ 4. Change her class schedule for Thursdays: She already has a light course load on Thursdays, so she doesn't need to change her schedule.

**Q3:** When will the woman begin her job shadowing experience?
- 1. On the 15th of the month
- 2. On the 22nd of the month
- 3. Next Monday morning
- 4. In four weeks' time
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. On the 15th of the month: This was one option offered, but she declined it.
✅ 2. On the 22nd of the month: The woman specifically states, "The 22nd would be perfect" when asked about her start date.
❌ 3. Next Monday morning: There is no mention of starting on a Monday; she arranged to come on Thursdays.
❌ 4. In four weeks' time: While she will spread her shadowing over four weeks, her start date is specifically the 22nd.

**Q4:** Why did the woman choose to shadow Michael instead of Sarah?
- 1. She has more experience with content creation.
- 2. She wants to learn about analytics and measurement.
- 3. Michael has a more flexible schedule.
- 4. Sarah was unavailable on Thursdays.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She has more experience with content creation: This contradicts her choice, as Sarah is the one who focuses on content creation.
✅ 2. She wants to learn about analytics and measurement: She explicitly states, "I'm really interested in learning how companies measure the effectiveness of their campaigns."
❌ 3. Michael has a more flexible schedule: There's no mention of Michael having a more flexible schedule than Sarah.
❌ 4. Sarah was unavailable on Thursdays: The conversation indicates both Sarah and Michael are available on Thursdays.

**Q5:** What is the main advice the man gives about preparing for the job shadowing?
- 1. Study the company's social media campaigns.
- 2. Wear formal business attire.
- 3. Bring identification and prepare questions.
- 4. Complete a confidentiality training course.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Study the company's social media campaigns: The man doesn't advise her to study campaigns beforehand.
❌ 2. Wear formal business attire: The man actually says "our office is business casual, so no need for formal attire."
✅ 3. Bring identification and prepare questions: The man specifically says, "Just bring your student ID, a notebook, and come with some questions prepared."
❌ 4. Complete a confidentiality training course: While a confidentiality agreement is mentioned, there's no training course required.

**Q6:** Why did the woman ask about coming on Thursdays specifically?
- 1. It's when Michael is available to mentor.
- 2. The office is less busy on Thursdays.
- 3. She has fewer classes on that day.
- 4. She needs to attend group meetings other days.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's when Michael is available to mentor: She requested Thursdays before knowing who would mentor her.
❌ 2. The office is less busy on Thursdays: There's no mention of office busyness in the conversation.
✅ 3. She has fewer classes on that day: She specifically states, "That's when I have the lightest course load."
❌ 4. She needs to attend group meetings other days: While she mentions rearranging group project meetings, this wasn't her reason for choosing Thursdays.

## Practice 497: Presentation Printing Quote | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a print shop. The woman works at the print shop, and the man is a customer who needs to print some documents.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there, I'm wondering if you can help me. I need to print some documents for a presentation tomorrow morning, but my home printer just ran out of ink.
WOMAN: Of course, I'd be happy to help you with that. What kind of documents do you need to print?
MAN: I have a PowerPoint presentation with about 30 slides, and I need 10 copies of each. They should be in color, if possible.
WOMAN: That shouldn't be a problem. Do you have the file with you?
MAN: Yes, it's on this USB drive. Um, I was also wondering about the cost. I'm on a bit of a tight budget for this project.
WOMAN: Our color prints are 75 cents per page, but since you're printing multiple copies, I can offer you our bulk rate of 60 cents per page.
MAN: That's still going to add up quite a bit. Are there any other options that might be more affordable?
WOMAN: Well, you could print just the slides with important graphics in color, and do the rest in black and white. Our black and white prints are only 15 cents per page.

**Q1:** What does the man need to print?
- 1. A research paper
- 2. A PowerPoint presentation
- 3. Business cards
- 4. Marketing flyers
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A research paper: This is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. A PowerPoint presentation: The man explicitly states, "I have a PowerPoint presentation with about 30 slides."
❌ 3. Business cards: This is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Marketing flyers: This is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q2:** How much does the woman say color prints cost with the bulk discount?
- 1. 15 cents per page
- 2. 60 cents per page
- 3. 75 cents per page
- 4. 90 cents per page
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 15 cents per page: This is the cost for black and white prints, not color prints with bulk discount.
✅ 2. 60 cents per page: The woman specifically states, "Our color prints are 75 cents per page, but since you're printing multiple copies, I can offer you our bulk rate of 60 cents per page."
❌ 3. 75 cents per page: This is the regular price for color prints without the bulk discount.
❌ 4. 90 cents per page: This price is not mentioned in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q3:** How long will it take to print the man's order?
- 1. 30 minutes
- 2. 45 minutes
- 3. One hour
- 4. Two hours
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 30 minutes: This timeframe is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. 45 minutes: This timeframe is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. One hour: The woman clearly states, "If we do a mix of color and black and white, I can have it ready in about an hour."
❌ 4. Two hours: This timeframe is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q4:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He needs to make some changes to his presentation.
- 2. He is in a hurry to get his printing done.
- 3. He has printed at this shop many times before.
- 4. He wants the most expensive binding option.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He needs to make some changes to his presentation: The man states, "I might need to make some last-minute changes to a few slides" and later confirms "I've finished making my changes."
❌ 2. He is in a hurry to get his printing done: The man accepts the one-hour timeframe and doesn't request rush service, indicating he's not in a hurry.
❌ 3. He has printed at this shop many times before: There's no indication in the conversation that he has used this print shop before.
❌ 4. He wants the most expensive binding option: He chooses spiral binding at $3.50, which is the least expensive of the options mentioned (compared to comb binding at $4.00 and presentation folders at $5.00).

**Q5:** How does the man feel about the cost of printing?
- 1. Unconcerned
- 2. Worried
- 3. Angry
- 4. Confused
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Unconcerned: The man expresses concern about the cost multiple times.
✅ 2. Worried: The man states, "I'm on a bit of a tight budget for this project" and asks about more affordable options, showing he's worried about the cost.
❌ 3. Angry: The man doesn't express anger about the prices at any point.
❌ 4. Confused: The man understands the pricing; he's just concerned about the total cost.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q6:** How many slides did the man decide to print in color?
- 1. 10 slides
- 2. 15 slides
- 3. 20 slides
- 4. 30 slides
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 10 slides: The man needed 10 copies of each slide, but this wasn't the number of color slides.
✅ 2. 15 slides: The man explicitly states, "I decided to go with 15 slides in color and the rest in black and white."
❌ 3. 20 slides: This number is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. 30 slides: This was the total number of slides in his presentation, not the number he chose to print in color.

**Q7:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She refuses to adjust the formatting of the slides.
- 2. She suggests using premium cardstock for durability.
- 3. She charges extra for the use of office supplies.
- 4. She offers to call the man when his order is ready.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She refuses to adjust the formatting of the slides: This is incorrect. When the man asks her to adjust the formatting, she says "No problem."
❌ 2. She suggests using premium cardstock for durability: She mentions premium cardstock as an option but doesn't specifically suggest using it; she just presents it as a choice.
❌ 3. She charges extra for the use of office supplies: This is incorrect. She offers the stapler and other supplies for free at the self-service counter.
✅ 4. She offers to call the man when his order is ready: She explicitly states, "We'll call you when it's done, or you can wait in our lounge area if you prefer."

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will wait in the lounge area.
- 2. The woman will start printing the presentation.
- 3. The man will change his mind about the binding.
- 4. The woman will ask for payment upfront.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will wait in the lounge area: The woman offers this as an option, but the conversation suggests he will return later as she says the order will be ready by 4:30.
✅ 2. The woman will start printing the presentation: This is the most logical next step since all decisions have been made about the printing job.
❌ 3. The man will change his mind about the binding: There's no indication he's unsatisfied with his binding choice.
❌ 4. The woman will ask for payment upfront: While this might happen, there's no indication in the conversation that payment is required before printing begins.

## Practice 499: Delayed Train Rescheduling | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two friends at a train station.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why were the friends initially unable to find each other?
- 1. They were at different parts of the station.
- 2. The woman arrived 15 minutes late.
- 3. The man was buying coffee.
- 4. The station was too crowded to see each other.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. They were at different parts of the station: The woman says "I thought we were meeting at the main entrance, not by the ticket counters" indicating they were in different locations.
❌ 2. The woman arrived 15 minutes late: She waited for 15 minutes, but wasn't late herself.
❌ 3. The man was buying coffee: He offers to buy coffee later in the conversation, not at the beginning.
❌ 4. The station was too crowded to see each other: While the woman mentions the station is crowded, this isn't given as the reason they couldn't find each other.

**Q2:** What is the main problem the friends are facing?
- 1. They missed their train completely.
- 2. Their original train is delayed by an hour.
- 3. The restaurant canceled their reservation.
- 4. The alternative train is fully booked.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They missed their train completely: The conversation indicates they haven't missed their train, just that it's delayed.
✅ 2. Their original train is delayed by an hour: The man specifically states "our 2:30 train. It's been delayed by at least an hour due to some track maintenance near Kingston."
❌ 3. The restaurant canceled their reservation: The reservation wasn't canceled; they're concerned about not making it on time.
❌ 4. The alternative train is fully booked: There's no mention of the alternative train being fully booked; in fact, the man plans to exchange their tickets for it.

**Q3:** Why is the woman concerned about the delay?
- 1. She might miss an important meeting.
- 2. She won't have time to check into their hotel.
- 3. They might not make their dinner reservation.
- 4. She has another train to catch in Montreal.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She might miss an important meeting: No meeting is mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. She won't have time to check into their hotel: No hotel check-in is mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. They might not make their dinner reservation: She specifically says "That's going to mess up our dinner reservation at that place on Rue Saint-Denis. I booked it for 7:30, and we won't make it in time now."
❌ 4. She has another train to catch in Montreal: No connecting train is mentioned in the conversation.

**Q4:** What does the man think about their situation?
- 1. He's frustrated but determined to enjoy their trip.
- 2. He's considering canceling the entire weekend.
- 3. He blames the woman for being late.
- 4. He's worried about the additional cost.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He's frustrated but determined to enjoy their trip: At the end he says "This isn't how I imagined starting our weekend getaway, but we'll make it work!" showing frustration but determination.
❌ 2. He's considering canceling the entire weekend: He's actively finding solutions, not considering cancellation.
❌ 3. He blames the woman for being late: He doesn't blame her at all; in fact, he apologizes for the confusion about meeting location.
❌ 4. He's worried about the additional cost: No concerns about cost are mentioned in the conversation.

**Q5:** What will the friends most likely do next?
- 1. Cancel their trip to Montreal.
- 2. Take a bus instead of the train.
- 3. Change their train and adjust their dinner reservation.
- 4. Find a different restaurant in Montreal.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Cancel their trip to Montreal: They're actively finding solutions, not canceling their trip.
❌ 2. Take a bus instead of the train: No bus option is discussed in the conversation.
✅ 3. Change their train and adjust their dinner reservation: The man says he'll "see if we can exchange our tickets for the 3:15 train" and they discuss calling to "push the reservation back to 8:00."
❌ 4. Find a different restaurant in Montreal: They plan to keep their reservation but change the time, not find a different restaurant.

## Practice 500: Riverside Recreation Tour | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman about booking a recreational facility tour.

### Audio Transcript
$4c

**Q1:** Why is the woman visiting the recreation center?
- 1. To sign up for swimming lessons
- 2. To inquire about tour options
- 3. To use the fitness facilities
- 4. To apply for a job position
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To sign up for swimming lessons: The woman mentions being interested in swimming facilities, but she's not specifically there to sign up for lessons.
✅ 2. To inquire about tour options: The woman states she's 'interested in getting a tour of your facilities,' which is the primary reason for her visit.
❌ 3. To use the fitness facilities: She hasn't come to use the facilities yet; she wants to tour them first.
❌ 4. To apply for a job position: There's no mention of the woman seeking employment at the center.

**Q2:** What is the woman most likely to do after the tour?
- 1. Request a personal training session
- 2. Sign up for specialized swimming lessons
- 3. Purchase an annual membership
- 4. Attend a yoga class
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Request a personal training session: While the membership includes complimentary training sessions, there's no indication this is her priority.
❌ 2. Sign up for specialized swimming lessons: Though she's interested in swimming facilities, she doesn't specifically mention wanting lessons.
✅ 3. Purchase an annual membership: She explicitly states 'I'm considering signing up for a membership if the facilities meet my needs' and discusses the membership discount.
❌ 4. Attend a yoga class: While yoga is mentioned as an available class, there's no indication this is her specific interest after the tour.

**Q3:** What time will the woman's tour begin?
- 1. 10:00 AM
- 2. 1:50 PM
- 3. 2:00 PM
- 4. 2:45 PM
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 10:00 AM: This is one of the tour times offered, but not the one the woman selects.
❌ 2. 1:50 PM: This would be 10 minutes before the tour, but is when she should arrive, not when the tour begins.
✅ 3. 2:00 PM: The woman specifically books the Thursday 2 PM tour, which is confirmed by the man.
❌ 4. 2:45 PM: This time is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q4:** Why might the woman be particularly interested in this recreation center?
- 1. It offers tennis coaching
- 2. It's located in her new neighborhood
- 3. It has personal trainers available
- 4. It provides specialized programs
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It offers tennis coaching: While mentioned, this isn't given as her reason for interest.
✅ 2. It's located in her new neighborhood: The woman specifically states 'I'm new to the neighborhood' at the beginning of the conversation, indicating proximity is a key factor.
❌ 3. It has personal trainers available: While mentioned as part of the membership promotion, this isn't stated as her reason for interest.
❌ 4. It provides specialized programs: She doesn't express particular interest in these programs.

**Q5:** What is the main benefit the man suggests about their membership?
- 1. The Olympic-sized swimming pool
- 2. The current discount for new members
- 3. The variety of included fitness classes
- 4. The outdoor track facilities
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The Olympic-sized swimming pool: While mentioned as a facility, it's not presented as a membership benefit.
✅ 2. The current discount for new members: The man emphasizes 'New members get 15% off the annual membership fee, plus two complimentary personal training sessions' as a special promotion.
❌ 3. The variety of included fitness classes: While mentioned, this isn't presented as the main benefit.
❌ 4. The outdoor track facilities: The track is mentioned as part of the tour but not highlighted as a special benefit.

**Q6:** Why does the man suggest arriving 10 minutes early?
- 1. To meet the tour guide
- 2. To complete registration paperwork
- 3. To find parking at the facility
- 4. To sign in at reception
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To meet the tour guide: The man doesn't mention this as a reason for arriving early.
❌ 2. To complete registration paperwork: While signing in is mentioned, there's no specific reference to paperwork.
❌ 3. To find parking at the facility: Parking is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 4. To sign in at reception: The man specifically says 'We just ask that you arrive about 10 minutes early to sign in at the front desk.'

## Practice 502: Coffee Roast Selection | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a specialty coffee shop. The woman is a barista at the coffee shop, and the man is a customer.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What is the man looking for?
- 1. A strong coffee with a bold flavor
- 2. A coffee without caffeine
- 3. A unique coffee that isn't too strong
- 4. A coffee similar to his regular order
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A strong coffee with a bold flavor: The man specifically says he wants coffee that's "not too strong."
❌ 2. A coffee without caffeine: The man clearly states "Oh, definitely with caffeine. I need that morning boost."
✅ 3. A unique coffee that isn't too strong: The man's exact words are "I'm looking for a coffee that's not too strong but has a unique flavor."
❌ 4. A coffee similar to his regular order: The man says he's "getting a bit tired of my regular drip coffee," indicating he wants something different.

**Q2:** Why does the man express concern about acidity?
- 1. He has experienced heartburn before
- 2. He dislikes sour-tasting coffee
- 3. He is following a special diet
- 4. He wants a stronger flavor profile
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He has experienced heartburn before: The man specifically asks "Is it very acidic? I sometimes get heartburn from certain coffees."
❌ 2. He dislikes sour-tasting coffee: He doesn't mention disliking sour tastes, only concerns about heartburn.
❌ 3. He is following a special diet: There's no mention of any special diet in the conversation.
❌ 4. He wants a stronger flavor profile: He actually states he wants coffee that's "not too strong."

**Q3:** What does the woman suggest to help the man choose a coffee?
- 1. Reading online reviews
- 2. A tasting flight of three varieties
- 3. Trying their most popular blend
- 4. Consulting with another barista
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Reading online reviews: This is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. A tasting flight of three varieties: The woman says, "We actually offer a tasting flight if you'd like to try three different varieties side by side. It's a great way to find your perfect match."
❌ 3. Trying their most popular blend: She doesn't mention popularity of any blends.
❌ 4. Consulting with another barista: There's no suggestion to speak with another employee.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Wow, that Guatemalan blend was delicious! I think I'll get a medium cup of that, please.
WOMAN: Great choice! Would you like that prepared as a pour-over or with our espresso machine?
MAN: Um, I'm not really sure what the difference would be, to be honest.
WOMAN: No problem. The pour-over method brings out more of the subtle flavors, while the espresso machine gives you a more concentrated, robust cup. For this particular bean, I'd recommend the pour-over.
MAN: That sounds perfect. Oh, and do you have any of those pastries left? The ones in the display case with the cinnamon on top?
WOMAN: The morning buns? Yes, we still have a few left. They pair wonderfully with the Guatemalan blend, actually.
MAN: I'll take one of those too, then. Oh wait—I just realized I left my wallet in the car. Would you mind holding my order for a minute while I run out to get it?
WOMAN: Of course! No rush. I'll start preparing your pour-over so it's ready when you get back.

**Q3:** Which brewing method does the woman recommend for the Guatemalan blend?
- 1. French press
- 2. Drip coffee
- 3. Espresso machine
- 4. Pour-over
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. French press: This brewing method is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. Drip coffee: The man mentions he's tired of regular drip coffee, but the woman doesn't recommend this method.
❌ 3. Espresso machine: The woman mentions this as an option but doesn't recommend it for the Guatemalan blend.
✅ 4. Pour-over: The woman specifically says, "For this particular bean, I'd recommend the pour-over."

**Q4:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He is in a hurry to get to work
- 2. He forgot his wallet in his car
- 3. He is a regular customer at the shop
- 4. He doesn't like sweet pastries
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He is in a hurry to get to work: There's no indication he's rushing to work.
✅ 2. He forgot his wallet in his car: The man explicitly states, "I just realized I left my wallet in the car. Would you mind holding my order for a minute while I run out to get it?"
❌ 3. He is a regular customer at the shop: His unfamiliarity with specialty coffees and brewing methods suggests he's not a regular.
❌ 4. He doesn't like sweet pastries: He actually orders a morning bun with cinnamon, which is a sweet pastry.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: I'm back, sorry about that. Oh, that coffee smells amazing!
WOMAN: No worries at all. Here's your pour-over and morning bun. That'll be $8.75 total.
MAN: Perfect, thank you. By the way, I noticed you have bags of beans for sale. If I really like this, I might want to brew it at home. Do you sell the equipment for pour-overs too?
WOMAN: We absolutely do! We have several pour-over setups ranging from basic to more advanced. The starter kit with a ceramic dripper and filters is $24.99. We also offer brewing classes every Saturday morning if you're interested in learning the technique.
MAN: That's actually really tempting. How long do the classes run?
WOMAN: They're about an hour and a half, from 9:00 to 10:30, before we get busy. The class includes a bag of beans to take home and a 10% discount on any equipment purchased that day.
MAN: That sounds perfect. How do I sign up?
WOMAN: I can add you to the list right now if you'd like. We still have three spots open for this Saturday.

**Q6:** How much does the man's order cost?
- 1. $$7.50
- 2. $$8.25
- 3. $$8.75
- 4. $$9.25
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $7.50: This price is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. $8.25: This price is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. $8.75: The woman clearly states, "That'll be $8.75 total" when the man returns with his wallet.
❌ 4. $9.25: This price is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q7:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She is new to the coffee shop
- 2. She teaches brewing classes on weekends
- 3. She prefers espresso over pour-over coffee
- 4. She is offering a special discount today
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She is new to the coffee shop: There's no indication in the conversation that she's new.
✅ 2. She teaches brewing classes on weekends: She mentions they offer brewing classes every Saturday morning, and her knowledge suggests she's involved in teaching them.
❌ 3. She prefers espresso over pour-over coffee: She actually recommends pour-over for the Guatemalan blend, not expressing a personal preference for espresso.
❌ 4. She is offering a special discount today: She mentions a 10% discount for equipment purchased on the day of the class, not a special discount today.

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will purchase a pour-over kit
- 2. The man will sign up for a Saturday class
- 3. The woman will demonstrate brewing techniques
- 4. The woman will offer another coffee sample
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will purchase a pour-over kit: While he shows interest in equipment, his immediate response is about the class.
✅ 2. The man will sign up for a Saturday class: The man's last statement is "That sounds perfect. How do I sign up?" after hearing about the Saturday classes, and the woman responds that she can add him to the list right now.
❌ 3. The woman will demonstrate brewing techniques: There's no indication she'll demonstrate techniques right now; the demonstrations happen during the Saturday classes.
❌ 4. The woman will offer another coffee sample: The man has already decided on his coffee and purchased it.

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She is starting a new job: The conversation doesn't mention Jenna starting a new job.
✅ 2. She is enrolling in educational courses: The woman states that Jenna "can't do afternoons anymore because she's taking some courses at Maple Ridge College."
❌ 3. She wants to earn more money: There's no mention of Jenna changing her schedule to earn more money.
❌ 4. She is moving to a different neighborhood: The conversation doesn't mention Jenna moving anywhere.

**Q2:** What solution do the neighbors agree on?
- 1. Finding a new dog walker who can accommodate their schedule.
- 2. Taking turns walking both dogs and sharing the cost.
- 3. Walking their own dogs at different times.
- 4. Asking Jenna to make an exception for them.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Finding a new dog walker who can accommodate their schedule: They decide to continue with Jenna, not find a new walker.
✅ 2. Taking turns walking both dogs and sharing the cost: The man suggests "Maybe we could coordinate. I could take both dogs in the evening most days, and you could cover Wednesday evenings for both of them?" and they agree to "split the cost."
❌ 3. Walking their own dogs at different times: They plan to have Jenna continue walking their dogs, not walk them themselves.
❌ 4. Asking Jenna to make an exception for them: They adapt to Jenna's new schedule rather than asking for an exception.

**Q3:** Why can't the man choose the morning walking option?
- 1. His dog doesn't like morning walks.
- 2. He has to leave for work too early.
- 3. He prefers to sleep in.
- 4. His dog needs longer walks than the time allows.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. His dog doesn't like morning walks: There's no mention of the dog's preference for walk times.
✅ 2. He has to leave for work too early: The man says "Mornings would be tough for me since I have to be at the office by 7:30."
❌ 3. He prefers to sleep in: The man doesn't mention his sleeping preferences.
❌ 4. His dog needs longer walks than the time allows: While he mentions Bailey needs a 30-minute walk, this isn't given as the reason he can't do mornings.

**Q4:** What does the woman probably think about the new arrangement?
- 1. It's inconvenient but necessary.
- 2. It's a good opportunity for the dogs to socialize.
- 3. It's only temporary until they find another solution.
- 4. It's too complicated to maintain long-term.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It's inconvenient but necessary: The woman responds positively, calling it "a great idea" with no indication she finds it inconvenient.
✅ 2. It's a good opportunity for the dogs to socialize: The woman mentions "Bailey and Rosie get along so well anyway. They're always trying to play when they see each other at the dog park."
❌ 3. It's only temporary until they find another solution: There's no indication this is a temporary arrangement.
❌ 4. It's too complicated to maintain long-term: The woman shows enthusiasm for the plan with no concerns about complexity.

**Q5:** What will likely happen at the dog park on Sunday?
- 1. They will discuss payment details with Jenna.
- 2. They will finalize their new walking schedule.
- 3. The dogs will get acquainted while the owners exchange keys.
- 4. They will train their dogs to walk together on leashes.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They will discuss payment details with Jenna: They plan to text Jenna about payment, not discuss it at the park.
❌ 2. They will finalize their new walking schedule: They've already decided on the schedule during this conversation.
✅ 3. The dogs will get acquainted while the owners exchange keys: The man suggests they "meet up this Sunday at the dog park to let the dogs play together" and mentions making a copy of his key by the weekend.
❌ 4. They will train their dogs to walk together on leashes: There's no mention of training the dogs to walk together.

## Practice 505: Adult Swimming Lessons | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a community recreation center.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why did the woman visit the community center?
- 1. To inquire about swimming lessons for her child
- 2. To learn how to swim as an adult
- 3. To renew her membership at the center
- 4. To apply for a job as a swimming instructor
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To inquire about swimming lessons for her child: The woman is seeking lessons for herself, not a child.
✅ 2. To learn how to swim as an adult: The woman says "I never learned to swim as a child, and I'd like to finally learn."
❌ 3. To renew her membership at the center: The woman states she is not a member yet.
❌ 4. To apply for a job as a swimming instructor: There is no indication she wants to become an instructor.

**Q2:** Why does the woman prefer group lessons over private lessons?
- 1. They are more affordable
- 2. They fit better with her schedule
- 3. She wants to meet other beginners
- 4. This is not directly stated in the conversation
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They are more affordable: While group lessons are less expensive, the woman never states this as her reason for preferring them.
❌ 2. They fit better with her schedule: She does prefer evening sessions, but this isn't stated as a reason for choosing group over private lessons.
❌ 3. She wants to meet other beginners: The woman never mentions wanting to meet others as a reason.
✅ 4. This is not directly stated in the conversation: The woman simply says "I think I'd prefer the group lessons to start" without explaining why she prefers them over private lessons.

**Q3:** What is one item the woman will need to bring to her swimming lessons?
- 1. A kickboard
- 2. A flotation device
- 3. A swimsuit
- 4. A membership card
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A kickboard: The man states "We provide all other equipment like kickboards and flotation devices."
❌ 2. A flotation device: The man specifically mentions that the center provides flotation devices.
✅ 3. A swimsuit: The man says "You'll need a swimsuit, a towel, and a swim cap is recommended but not required."
❌ 4. A membership card: There is no mention of needing a membership card for the lessons.

**Q4:** Why does the woman choose the evening sessions?
- 1. She has family commitments in the morning
- 2. She is employed during daytime hours
- 3. The morning classes are already full
- 4. The evening instructor is more experienced
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She has family commitments in the morning: No family commitments are mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. She is employed during daytime hours: The woman explicitly states "The evening sessions would definitely be better. I work during the day."
❌ 3. The morning classes are already full: There is no mention of morning classes being full.
❌ 4. The evening instructor is more experienced: No information about instructor experience is provided.

**Q5:** What will the woman most likely do after completing the swimming course?
- 1. Sign up for advanced swimming lessons
- 2. Consider purchasing a center membership
- 3. Apply for a job at the community center
- 4. Register her children for swimming lessons
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Sign up for advanced swimming lessons: There is no mention of advanced lessons in the conversation.
✅ 2. Consider purchasing a center membership: The woman states "I'm not a member yet, but I might consider it if I enjoy the lessons."
❌ 3. Apply for a job at the community center: There is no indication she wants to work at the center.
❌ 4. Register her children for swimming lessons: There is no mention of children in the conversation.

**Q6:** Why does the man ask the woman to complete a health questionnaire?
- 1. To determine if she qualifies for a discount
- 2. To assess her swimming ability level
- 3. It's part of the registration process
- 4. To recommend appropriate private instructors
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To determine if she qualifies for a discount: No discounts related to health are mentioned.
❌ 2. To assess her swimming ability level: The questionnaire is described as a health questionnaire, not a skill assessment.
✅ 3. It's part of the registration process: The man states "And I'll need you to fill out a brief health questionnaire before we process your registration."
❌ 4. To recommend appropriate private instructors: There is no mention of matching instructors based on health information.

## Practice 507: Brake Inspection Booking | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a vehicle maintenance center. The woman is a customer service representative, and the man is a customer who has brought his car in for service.

### Audio Transcript
$4f

**Q1:** Why did the man bring his car to the maintenance center?
- 1. For a complete vehicle inspection
- 2. Because his car wouldn't start
- 3. For routine maintenance and a noise issue
- 4. To have his tires replaced
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. For a complete vehicle inspection: The man specifically mentioned he came for an oil change and tire rotation, plus a noise issue, not a complete inspection.
❌ 2. Because his car wouldn't start: There's no mention of starting problems in the conversation.
✅ 3. For routine maintenance and a noise issue: The man clearly states he's there for "regular maintenance" (oil change and tire rotation) and also mentions a "strange noise when I brake."
❌ 4. To have his tires replaced: The man mentions tire rotation, not replacement.

**Q2:** What time does the man need to leave by?
- 1. 11:00 AM
- 2. 12:00 PM
- 3. 1:00 PM
- 4. 2:00 PM
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 11:00 AM: This time is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. 12:00 PM: The woman says "We should have you out of here by noon at the latest" after the man mentions his 2:00 PM meeting.
❌ 3. 1:00 PM: This time is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. 2:00 PM: While the man mentions having a meeting at 2:00 PM, this is not when he needs to leave the service center by.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q3:** What did the technician discover about the man's car?
- 1. The oil was completely depleted
- 2. The tires needed immediate replacement
- 3. The brake pads were worn and a rotor was warped
- 4. The entire brake system needed to be replaced
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The oil was completely depleted: This issue is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. The tires needed immediate replacement: There's no mention of tire replacement needs.
✅ 3. The brake pads were worn and a rotor was warped: The woman specifically states "The front brake pads are quite worn down, and one of the rotors is showing signs of warping."
❌ 4. The entire brake system needed to be replaced: Only the front brake pads and one rotor needed replacement, not the entire system.

**Q4:** How does the man feel about the situation?
- 1. Angry about the poor condition of his car
- 2. Concerned about the unexpected cost
- 3. Relieved that the problem was identified
- 4. Eager to get the repairs done immediately
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Angry about the poor condition of his car: The man doesn't express anger in the conversation.
✅ 2. Concerned about the unexpected cost: The man says "That's quite a bit more than I was expecting to spend today" and asks about postponing the repair, showing concern about the cost.
❌ 3. Relieved that the problem was identified: The man doesn't express relief about the diagnosis.
❌ 4. Eager to get the repairs done immediately: The man actually considers postponing the repairs, showing he's not eager to get them done right away.

**Q5:** What is the total cost of all recommended services?
- 1. $$79.95
- 2. $$300.00
- 3. $$385.00
- 4. $$464.95
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $79.95: This is only the cost of the oil change and tire rotation.
❌ 2. $300.00: This amount is not mentioned as a total cost.
❌ 3. $385.00: This is only the cost of the brake work.
✅ 4. $464.95: The woman states the brake work would cost "approximately $385, plus the $79.95 you're already paying for the oil change and tire rotation," which totals $464.95.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q6:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She insists the repairs must be done immediately
- 2. She offers flexible options to accommodate the customer
- 3. She is unwilling to check warranty information
- 4. She recommends replacing the entire brake system
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She insists the repairs must be done immediately: She actually says he can drive home safely but shouldn't postpone for too long.
✅ 2. She offers flexible options to accommodate the customer: She mentions a payment plan, offers to check warranty coverage, and assures him he can come back later for the brake work if he prefers.
❌ 3. She is unwilling to check warranty information: She readily agrees to check the warranty information.
❌ 4. She recommends replacing the entire brake system: She only recommends replacing the front brake pads and one damaged rotor.

**Q7:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He refuses to pay for any repairs
- 2. He believes his warranty might cover the brake repairs
- 3. He has already arranged for payment through his insurance
- 4. He wants to trade in his car instead of repairing it
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He refuses to pay for any repairs: He's considering options but never refuses to pay.
✅ 2. He believes his warranty might cover the brake repairs: He specifically says "I just remembered that my wife mentioned our extended warranty might cover brake repairs."
❌ 3. He has already arranged for payment through his insurance: Insurance payment is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. He wants to trade in his car instead of repairing it: Trading in the car is not mentioned as an option.

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will cancel all services and leave
- 2. The woman will check if the warranty covers the brake repairs
- 3. The technician will begin working on a different vehicle
- 4. The man will pay the full amount immediately
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will cancel all services and leave: There's no indication he wants to cancel the services already performed.
✅ 2. The woman will check if the warranty covers the brake repairs: The man asks about his extended warranty coverage, and the woman agrees to check this, saying "I'll have one of our staff bring you the documents."
❌ 3. The technician will begin working on a different vehicle: The conversation indicates they're still discussing the current vehicle.
❌ 4. The man will pay the full amount immediately: The man is hesitant about the cost and is exploring warranty coverage first.

## Practice 509: Community Garage Sale | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two neighbors discussing a community garage sale.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why is the woman considering participating in the garage sale?
- 1. She wants to meet new neighbors.
- 2. She has items from decluttering her basement.
- 3. She needs to raise money for a community project.
- 4. She wants to support the neighborhood event.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She wants to meet new neighbors.: The conversation doesn't mention the woman wanting to meet new neighbors as her reason for participating.
✅ 2. She has items from decluttering her basement.: The woman specifically states "I've been decluttering the basement and have boxes of old books, kitchen gadgets, and some furniture." This is her reason for considering participation.
❌ 3. She needs to raise money for a community project.: There is no mention of a community project in the conversation.
❌ 4. She wants to support the neighborhood event.: While she's participating in a community event, her primary motivation is to sell her decluttered items, not to support the event itself.

**Q2:** What time did the man suggest they should start setting up?
- 1. 6:00 AM
- 2. 7:00 AM
- 3. 8:30 AM
- 4. 9:00 AM
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 6:00 AM: This time is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. 7:00 AM: The man specifically says "Let's plan on setting up together at 7 on Saturday morning."
❌ 3. 8:30 AM: This is the time the woman initially thought of starting, not what the man suggested.
❌ 4. 9:00 AM: This is mentioned as the "official start time" from the previous year, not when they plan to set up.

**Q3:** What pricing strategy did the man recommend?
- 1. Charge 50% of the original price
- 2. Price items at 25% of what he paid originally
- 3. Set high prices and expect to lower them
- 4. Match prices with local thrift stores
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Charge 50% of the original price: This percentage is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. Price items at 25% of what he paid originally: The man specifically states "I usually go with about 25% of what I paid originally."
❌ 3. Set high prices and expect to lower them: While the man mentions being prepared to haggle, he doesn't suggest setting high prices initially.
❌ 4. Match prices with local thrift stores: There is no mention of thrift stores or matching their prices in the conversation.

**Q4:** How does the woman probably feel about the man's experience with garage sales?
- 1. She thinks he's exaggerating his success.
- 2. She's impressed by how much money he made.
- 3. She's grateful for his advice and assistance.
- 4. She's concerned his approach is too complicated.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She thinks he's exaggerating his success: There's no indication she doubts his claims.
❌ 2. She's impressed by how much money he made: While the man mentions making $300, the woman doesn't specifically express being impressed by this amount.
✅ 3. She's grateful for his advice and assistance: Throughout the conversation, she asks for his advice, responds positively to his suggestions, and ultimately accepts his offer to share space and borrow equipment, showing gratitude for his experience and help.
❌ 4. She's concerned his approach is too complicated: She shows no signs of concern about complexity; rather, she embraces his suggestions.

**Q5:** What will likely happen on Saturday morning?
- 1. The neighbors will compete for customers.
- 2. The woman will set up in her own driveway.
- 3. The man will help the woman price her items.
- 4. The neighbors will hold a combined garage sale.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The neighbors will compete for customers: There's no indication they will compete; in fact, they plan to collaborate.
❌ 2. The woman will set up in her own driveway: They actually agreed to combine their sales at the man's driveway.
❌ 3. The man will help the woman price her items: While he gives pricing advice, there's no specific plan for him to help price her items.
✅ 4. The neighbors will hold a combined garage sale: They explicitly agree to this when the woman suggests "Maybe we could even combine our sales?" and the man responds positively, saying "Let's plan on setting up together."

## Practice 510: Greenview Community Garden Membership | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman about a community garden program.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why is the man interested in joining the community garden?
- 1. He wants to meet new people in the neighborhood.
- 2. He misses having space to grow vegetables.
- 3. He wants to learn how to garden for the first time.
- 4. He needs fresh produce to save money on groceries.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He wants to meet new people in the neighborhood: While he does express interest in meeting neighbors later, this is not his primary reason for joining.
✅ 2. He misses having space to grow vegetables: The man explicitly states "I just moved into an apartment nearby and really miss having a garden. I used to grow vegetables at my old place, but now I only have a small balcony."
❌ 3. He wants to learn how to garden for the first time: The man mentions he used to grow vegetables at his old place, indicating he has gardening experience.
❌ 4. He needs fresh produce to save money on groceries: There's no mention of saving money as a motivation.

**Q2:** What will the man most likely do after this conversation?
- 1. Visit the garden on a Saturday to meet other gardeners.
- 2. Purchase his own gardening tools and seeds.
- 3. Complete the application form and make a payment.
- 4. Research which vegetables grow best in small plots.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Visit the garden on a Saturday to meet other gardeners: While the man shows interest in meeting other gardeners on Saturdays, this is not his immediate next action.
❌ 2. Purchase his own gardening tools and seeds: The woman mentions that shared tools and basic seeds are included in the plot fee, so this isn't necessary.
✅ 3. Complete the application form and make a payment: At the end of the conversation, the man says "I think I'll take a plot then," and the woman responds with "Yes, I have the forms right here. You can fill it out now, and we accept payment by cash, check, or e-transfer."
❌ 4. Research which vegetables grow best in small plots: The man already mentioned what he wants to grow (tomatoes, peppers, herbs) and the woman confirmed these are good choices.

**Q3:** How long does the garden season last?
- 1. Four months
- 2. Five months
- 3. Six months
- 4. Seven months
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Four months: This is shorter than the actual season mentioned.
❌ 2. Five months: This is shorter than the actual season mentioned.
✅ 3. Six months: The woman states that the season "runs from May 1st to October 31st," which is exactly six months.
❌ 4. Seven months: This is longer than the actual season mentioned.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the community garden program?
- 1. It focuses primarily on growing organic vegetables.
- 2. It emphasizes both gardening skills and community building.
- 3. It requires previous gardening experience to join.
- 4. It allows members to sell their produce at local markets.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It focuses primarily on growing organic vegetables: While chemical pesticides are prohibited, there's no explicit focus on organic vegetables mentioned.
✅ 2. It emphasizes both gardening skills and community building: The program offers gardening workshops, mentoring for newcomers, community tasks, and social gatherings (Saturday work parties followed by coffee and snacks), showing a dual focus on gardening and community.
❌ 3. It requires previous gardening experience to join: Sarah specifically recommends smaller plots for beginners, indicating that no experience is necessary.
❌ 4. It allows members to sell their produce at local markets: There is no mention of selling produce in the conversation.

**Q5:** What is the main requirement for garden members beyond maintaining their plot?
- 1. Attending the bi-monthly gardening workshops.
- 2. Contributing volunteer time to community tasks.
- 3. Sharing excess produce with other gardeners.
- 4. Participating in Saturday morning social events.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Attending the bi-monthly gardening workshops: The workshops are offered as a benefit but are not mentioned as a requirement.
✅ 2. Contributing volunteer time to community tasks: Sarah specifically states "we ask everyone to contribute 2 hours per month to community garden tasks like path maintenance or compost turning" as one of the main rules.
❌ 3. Sharing excess produce with other gardeners: There's no mention of this requirement in the conversation.
❌ 4. Participating in Saturday morning social events: While Saturday gatherings are mentioned, they're described as optional social opportunities, not requirements.

**Q6:** Why does Sarah suggest a tour of the garden?
- 1. To introduce the man to other gardeners.
- 2. To show him how to use the shared tools.
- 3. To help him see the available plot options.
- 4. To demonstrate proper gardening techniques.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To introduce the man to other gardeners: Sarah doesn't mention meeting other gardeners during the tour.
❌ 2. To show him how to use the shared tools: There's no mention of demonstrating tool usage during the tour.
✅ 3. To help him see the available plot options: Sarah specifically says "I can give you a quick tour of the garden now so you can see the available spaces."
❌ 4. To demonstrate proper gardening techniques: Teaching gardening techniques is not mentioned as a purpose of the immediate tour.

- 4. The instructor will demonstrate advanced photography techniques
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will go shopping for a memory card: This won't happen as the instructor is providing a memory card.
✅ 2. The man will attend the workshop in the Pine Room: The woman tells him "The workshop starts in about 15 minutes in the Pine Room, just down that hallway on the right."
❌ 3. The woman will help the man set up his equipment: There's no mention of the woman helping with equipment setup.
❌ 4. The instructor will demonstrate advanced photography techniques: While this might happen during the workshop, it's not what will happen next based on the conversation.

## Practice 516: Moving Downsizing Sale | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two neighbors about a garage sale.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why is Mark moving to a new place?
- 1. To be closer to his wife's workplace
- 2. To have a bigger house for his family
- 3. To be near better schools for his children
- 4. To reduce his commute time to work
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. To be closer to his wife's workplace: Mark specifically says they found a place in Oakridge that's 'closer to my wife's new job at the hospital'.
❌ 2. To have a bigger house for his family: Mark actually mentions the new place is 'a bit smaller than our current place'.
❌ 3. To be near better schools for his children: Schools are not mentioned as a reason for moving in the conversation.
❌ 4. To reduce his commute time to work: Mark only mentions his wife's job location as a factor, not his own commute.

**Q2:** What is Mark planning to do with his extra belongings?
- 1. Donate them to charity
- 2. Sell them at a garage sale
- 3. Give them to his neighbors
- 4. Put them in storage
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Donate them to charity: There's no mention of donating items to charity.
✅ 2. Sell them at a garage sale: Mark clearly states 'We're planning a garage sale next Saturday from 8 to 3' to sell their extra belongings.
❌ 3. Give them to his neighbors: While he offers Sarah first pick, he's still selling the items, not giving them away.
❌ 4. Put them in storage: There's no mention of storing any items.

**Q3:** What item is Sarah particularly interested in purchasing?
- 1. A dining table
- 2. Kitchen appliances
- 3. A bookshelf
- 4. Children's toys
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A dining table: While Mark mentions having a dining table, Sarah doesn't express interest in it.
❌ 2. Kitchen appliances: Sarah doesn't mention wanting any kitchen appliances.
✅ 3. A bookshelf: Sarah confirms she's still looking for a bookshelf saying 'Yes! I still am. The one in my home office is practically falling apart.'
❌ 4. Children's toys: Sarah doesn't express interest in buying any children's toys.

**Q4:** Why does Mark appreciate Sarah's offer to help?
- 1. He doesn't know how to organize a garage sale
- 2. He'll be alone with his children that morning
- 3. He has too many items to arrange by himself
- 4. He wants company during the garage sale
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He doesn't know how to organize a garage sale: There's no indication Mark lacks knowledge about organizing garage sales.
✅ 2. He'll be alone with his children that morning: Mark explicitly states 'My wife is working that morning, so I'll be handling the kids and trying to organize everything.'
❌ 3. He has too many items to arrange by himself: While he has many items, he specifically mentions the challenge is managing both the setup and his children.
❌ 4. He wants company during the garage sale: The help offered is for setup before the sale, not for company during the actual sale.

**Q5:** What does Sarah probably think about Mark and his family?
- 1. They are making a mistake by moving
- 2. They should have waited until summer to move
- 3. They are valued neighbors she will miss
- 4. They are selling their belongings for too little money
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They are making a mistake by moving: Sarah never suggests the move is a mistake; she calls Oakridge 'a lovely area'.
❌ 2. They should have waited until summer to move: There's no mention of timing or seasons being an issue in the conversation.
✅ 3. They are valued neighbors she will miss: Sarah explicitly states 'we'll miss having you as neighbors' showing she values them.
❌ 4. They are selling their belongings for too little money: Sarah never comments on the pricing of the items being sold.

## Practice 517: Riverside Book Club Enrolment | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a library.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why is the woman interested in joining the book club?
- 1. She wants to improve her reading skills.
- 2. She is looking to socialize with other readers.
- 3. She needs to fulfill a community service requirement.
- 4. She wants to learn about different literary genres.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She wants to improve her reading skills.: The woman doesn't mention wanting to improve her reading skills.
✅ 2. She is looking to socialize with other readers.: The woman explicitly states 'I've been looking for a way to meet new people who enjoy reading as much as I do' and later says 'It's exactly what I've been looking for.'
❌ 3. She needs to fulfill a community service requirement.: There's no mention of any community service requirement.
❌ 4. She wants to learn about different literary genres.: While she mentions being 'open to different genres,' her primary stated motivation is social.

**Q2:** What will the woman most likely do after this conversation?
- 1. Purchase all three books for the spring session.
- 2. Suggest new book titles for future meetings.
- 3. Borrow the first book from the library.
- 4. Prepare discussion questions for the group.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Purchase all three books for the spring session.: The woman specifically states 'I think I'd prefer to borrow them first, then maybe buy copies of the ones I really enjoy.'
❌ 2. Suggest new book titles for future meetings.: There's no indication she plans to suggest titles; the man mentions they vote as a group on future selections.
✅ 3. Borrow the first book from the library.: The woman clearly states she prefers to borrow the books first, and the man confirms multiple copies are available to borrow.
❌ 4. Prepare discussion questions for the group.: The man explicitly tells her, 'For your first meeting, just read the book and come ready to share your thoughts' and mentions that the facilitator will guide the conversation.

**Q3:** When does the book club meet?
- 1. Every Thursday evening
- 2. The last Thursday of each month
- 3. Three times per week
- 4. On weekday afternoons
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Every Thursday evening: The club doesn't meet every Thursday, only on a specific Thursday each month.
✅ 2. The last Thursday of each month: The man clearly states 'Our book club meets on the last Thursday of every month from 6:30 to 8:00 PM.'
❌ 3. Three times per week: There's no mention of meeting three times per week; they meet monthly.
❌ 4. On weekday afternoons: The meeting time is from 6:30 to 8:00 PM, which is evening, not afternoon.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the book club members?
- 1. They are mostly professional writers.
- 2. They prefer to read only fiction.
- 3. They come from diverse backgrounds.
- 4. They have been meeting for many years.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They are mostly professional writers.: There is no information suggesting the members are professional writers.
❌ 2. They prefer to read only fiction.: While fiction is mentioned (contemporary fiction, mystery, historical fiction), the man says 'We try to have a good variety' suggesting they read other genres too.
✅ 3. They come from diverse backgrounds.: The man explicitly states 'It's a friendly group with a good mix of ages and backgrounds.'
❌ 4. They have been meeting for many years.: There is no information about how long the book club has been running.

**Q5:** What is the main purpose of the registration fee?
- 1. To cover refreshments and special events
- 2. To purchase books for all members
- 3. To pay the discussion facilitator
- 4. To reserve the community room
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. To cover refreshments and special events: The man specifically states the $15 registration fee 'helps cover refreshments and occasional author visit expenses.'
❌ 2. To purchase books for all members: The man mentions that books are available to borrow or purchase separately; the fee doesn't cover book purchases.
❌ 3. To pay the discussion facilitator: There's no mention that the fee is used to pay the facilitator.
❌ 4. To reserve the community room: There's no indication that the fee is used for room reservation.

**Q6:** Why does the man mention that some members like to make notes in their books?
- 1. To encourage the woman to participate actively
- 2. To explain why some people purchase their own copies
- 3. To suggest a technique for better comprehension
- 4. To warn against writing in library books
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To encourage the woman to participate actively: The comment wasn't made to encourage participation.
✅ 2. To explain why some people purchase their own copies: The man mentions this directly after explaining members can borrow or purchase books, indicating it's a reason why some prefer to buy their own copies.
❌ 3. To suggest a technique for better comprehension: He's not suggesting she should take notes, just explaining why some members buy books.
❌ 4. To warn against writing in library books: He doesn't warn against writing in library books; he's explaining why some members prefer to purchase their own.
