## Practice 520: Ski Gear Sizing | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a winter sports equipment rental shop. The woman works at the shop, and the man is a customer looking to rent some equipment.

### Audio Transcript
MAN: Hi there. I'm planning a ski trip to Whistler this weekend with some friends, and I need to rent some equipment. I haven't been skiing in years, so I'm not sure exactly what I need.
WOMAN: No problem at all. We can definitely help you out. Are you looking for a complete package, or do you already have some of your own gear?
MAN: I don't have anything, actually. I used to ski when I was younger, but it's been, um, probably about five years since my last trip.
WOMAN: That's fine. For a complete setup, you'll need skis, boots, poles, and I'd recommend a helmet as well. Do you know your skill level? Beginner, intermediate, or advanced?
MAN: I'd say intermediate. I'm a bit rusty, but I can handle blue runs without much trouble.
WOMAN: Perfect. And how tall are you and what's your weight? We need that to size the equipment properly.
MAN: I'm 5'10" and about 180 pounds.
WOMAN: Great. And when exactly are you heading up to Whistler? Just so I know how many days to set up the rental for.

**Q1:** Why is the man at the rental shop?
- 1. To purchase ski equipment
- 2. To rent equipment for a ski trip
- 3. To return previously rented equipment
- 4. To get his equipment repaired
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To purchase ski equipment: The man is renting, not purchasing equipment.
✅ 2. To rent equipment for a ski trip: The man clearly states at the beginning that he's 'planning a ski trip to Whistler this weekend' and needs 'to rent some equipment.'
❌ 3. To return previously rented equipment: The man is getting equipment for an upcoming trip, not returning anything.
❌ 4. To get his equipment repaired: The man doesn't own equipment that needs repair; he's renting equipment.

**Q2:** How long has it been since the man last went skiing?
- 1. About two years
- 2. About three years
- 3. About five years
- 4. About ten years
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. About two years: The man specifically states it's been about five years, not two.
❌ 2. About three years: The man specifically states it's been about five years, not three.
✅ 3. About five years: The man directly states 'it's been, um, probably about five years since my last trip.'
❌ 4. About ten years: The man specifically states it's been about five years, not ten.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q3:** What is the daily cost of the initial ski package the woman offers?
- 1. $$35
- 2. $$40
- 3. $$45
- 4. $$50
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $35: The woman states the complete package would be $45 per day, not $35.
❌ 2. $40: The woman states the complete package would be $45 per day, not $40.
✅ 3. $45: The woman clearly states 'The complete package with the Rossignol skis would be $45 per day, so $135 total.'
❌ 4. $50: The woman states the complete package would be $45 per day, not $50.

**Q4:** What additional items does the man decide to rent?
- 1. Goggles and gloves
- 2. A helmet and goggles
- 3. A jacket and pants
- 4. Gloves and a hat
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Goggles and gloves: The man doesn't mention renting goggles or gloves.
❌ 2. A helmet and goggles: While a helmet is part of the basic package, the man doesn't rent goggles.
✅ 3. A jacket and pants: The man specifically asks about renting a jacket and pants, saying 'I think I'll need a large jacket and, um, probably large pants as well.'
❌ 4. Gloves and a hat: The man doesn't mention renting gloves or a hat.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q6:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He is an advanced skier
- 2. He already owns some ski equipment
- 3. He is concerned about the weather conditions
- 4. He has never been to Whistler before
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He is an advanced skier: The man describes himself as intermediate, not advanced.
❌ 2. He already owns some ski equipment: The man states 'I don't have anything, actually' when asked if he has his own gear.
✅ 3. He is concerned about the weather conditions: The man changes his ski selection after remembering his friend mentioned fresh snow, showing concern about weather conditions.
❌ 4. He has never been to Whistler before: There's no information in the conversation about whether he has been to Whistler before.

**Q7:** Why does the man change his ski selection?
- 1. The original skis were too expensive
- 2. His preferred skis weren't available
- 3. He wants skis better suited for fresh snow
- 4. His friend recommended a different brand
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The original skis were too expensive: Price wasn't the reason; the Salomon skis were actually $5 more expensive per day.
❌ 2. His preferred skis weren't available: Availability wasn't the issue; both ski options were available.
✅ 3. He wants skis better suited for fresh snow: The man changes his selection after remembering his friend's mention of fresh snow, and the woman confirms the Salomon skis would 'give you better float' in fresh powder.
❌ 4. His friend recommended a different brand: His friend only mentioned the weather conditions, not a specific ski brand.

**Q8:** How does the woman feel about the man's final ski choice?
- 1. Disappointed
- 2. Supportive
- 3. Indifferent
- 4. Confused
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Disappointed: The woman responds positively, calling it 'a wise choice,' showing support, not disappointment.
✅ 2. Supportive: The woman responds by saying 'A wise choice,' clearly showing support for his decision.
❌ 3. Indifferent: The woman is not indifferent; she actively affirms his choice as 'wise.'
❌ 4. Confused: There's no indication the woman is confused by the man's choice.

**Q9:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will try on the equipment
- 2. The man will pay for his rental
- 3. The woman will offer additional accessories
- 4. The woman will explain the cancellation policy
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will try on the equipment: There's no indication he'll try on equipment now; he's planning to pick it up later.
✅ 2. The man will pay for his rental: The man directly states 'I'll pay now' after the woman asks if he wants to pay now or when picking up the equipment.
❌ 3. The woman will offer additional accessories: The rental transaction is concluding, and the woman has already offered all necessary items.
❌ 4. The woman will explain the cancellation policy: There's no mention of a cancellation policy in the conversation.

## Practice 522: Campus Computer Club | Listening Practice Test

$4d

**Q1:** What is the main reason the woman is hesitant about joining the Computer Club?
- 1. She doesn't have enough time in her schedule.
- 2. She thinks she lacks the necessary skills.
- 3. She's concerned about the membership fee.
- 4. She doesn't know anyone in the club.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She doesn't have enough time in her schedule: The woman never mentions schedule conflicts as a concern.
✅ 2. She thinks she lacks the necessary skills: The woman explicitly states "I'm not exactly a coding expert or anything" and later says "I'm just worried everyone will be way more advanced than me. I barely know how to use Excel properly."
❌ 3. She's concerned about the membership fee: While she asks about fees, she responds that it "sounds worth it" when told the amount.
❌ 4. She doesn't know anyone in the club: The woman never expresses concern about not knowing other members.

**Q2:** What activity particularly interests the woman?
- 1. Web development sessions
- 2. Gaming group activities
- 3. Hardware repair workshops
- 4. Programming competitions
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Web development sessions: The woman doesn't express specific interest in web development.
❌ 2. Gaming group activities: The woman doesn't mention interest in the gaming group.
✅ 3. Hardware repair workshops: The woman specifically says "I'd love to learn more about hardware repair. My laptop's been making this weird noise lately, and I have no idea what's wrong with it."
❌ 4. Programming competitions: Programming competitions aren't mentioned in the conversation.

**Q3:** What does the membership fee cover?
- 1. Only the materials for projects
- 2. Snacks and project materials
- 3. The trip to Vancouver
- 4. Access to the Technology Building

✅ 2. Snacks and project materials: The man states "It's $20 for the whole year... That covers snacks at meetings and materials for some of the projects."
❌ 3. The trip to Vancouver: The trip is mentioned but not specified as covered by the membership fee.
❌ 4. Access to the Technology Building: There's no mention of needing to pay for access to the building.

**Q4:** What does the man think is an important aspect of the club?
- 1. The technical skills you can learn
- 2. The connections with professors
- 3. The social interactions
- 4. The resume-building opportunities
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The technical skills you can learn: While skills are mentioned, the man emphasizes something else as "half the fun."
❌ 2. The connections with professors: The man mentions a professor but doesn't emphasize connections with faculty as particularly important.
✅ 3. The social interactions: The man explicitly states "And honestly, the social aspect is half the fun. We usually go to Maple Street Café after meetings."
❌ 4. The resume-building opportunities: Resume benefits are not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q5:** What will likely happen at noon tomorrow?
- 1. The woman will attend her first club meeting.
- 2. The man will help the woman sign up for the club.
- 3. They will meet Professor Williams for advice.
- 4. They will go to Maple Street Café together.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The woman will attend her first club meeting: The first meeting is mentioned as being next Tuesday, not tomorrow.
✅ 2. The man will help the woman sign up for the club: The man offers "I can meet you there around noon if you want" when discussing the sign-up table at the Student Union Building.
❌ 3. They will meet Professor Williams for advice: There's no plan mentioned to meet Professor Williams tomorrow.
❌ 4. They will go to Maple Street Café together: While the café is mentioned, there's no plan to go there tomorrow.

## Practice 523: Aqua Pool Services | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a customer and a swimming pool maintenance staff member.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why did the woman visit Aqua Pool Services?
- 1. To get a quote for a new pool installation.
- 2. To learn how to maintain her pool properly.
- 3. To complain about a previous service.
- 4. To purchase pool cleaning equipment.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To get a quote for a new pool installation: The woman never mentions wanting to install a new pool.
✅ 2. To learn how to maintain her pool properly: The woman states "I just moved into a house with a swimming pool, and I have absolutely no idea how to maintain it properly. I was hoping you could give me some advice on a regular maintenance schedule."
❌ 3. To complain about a previous service: There is no mention of any previous service or complaint in the conversation.
❌ 4. To purchase pool cleaning equipment: The woman doesn't initially mention wanting to purchase equipment, only seeking advice on maintenance.

**Q2:** Why does the woman prefer to clean her skimmer baskets once a week?
- 1. She doesn't have many trees near her pool.
- 2. She has a smaller pool than most people.
- 3. She has limited time due to her work schedule.
- 4. She believes saltwater pools need less maintenance.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She doesn't have many trees near her pool: The conversation doesn't indicate whether she has trees or not.
❌ 2. She has a smaller pool than most people: While she mentions her pool size (12 by 24 feet), this isn't given as a reason for less frequent cleaning.
✅ 3. She has limited time due to her work schedule: When told to clean skimmer baskets twice a week, she specifically asks "I work long hours, so I'm wondering if I could get away with doing it just once a week?"
❌ 4. She believes saltwater pools need less maintenance: She never expresses this belief; in fact, she's new to pool ownership and seeking advice.

**Q3:** When should the woman backwash her pool filter?
- 1. Every week during summer months.
- 2. When the pressure gauge shows a significant increase.
- 3. After each professional cleaning service.
- 4. Only during the fall and winter seasons.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Every week during summer months: The man recommends backwashing about once a month, not weekly.
✅ 2. When the pressure gauge shows a significant increase: The man specifically states "you should backwash the filter about once a month, or when the pressure gauge rises 8-10 PSI above normal."
❌ 3. After each professional cleaning service: This is never mentioned as a recommendation for backwashing.
❌ 4. Only during the fall and winter seasons: The conversation doesn't restrict backwashing to specific seasons.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the woman's knowledge of pool maintenance?
- 1. She has some experience but needs a refresher.
- 2. She is completely new to pool ownership.
- 3. She previously maintained a chlorine pool.
- 4. She understands the basics but not the technical aspects.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She has some experience but needs a refresher: She explicitly states she has "absolutely no idea how to maintain it properly."
✅ 2. She is completely new to pool ownership: The woman states "I just moved into a house with a swimming pool, and I have absolutely no idea how to maintain it properly."
❌ 3. She previously maintained a chlorine pool: There's no mention of her having any previous pool maintenance experience.
❌ 4. She understands the basics but not the technical aspects: She indicates she has no knowledge of pool maintenance, not even the basics.

**Q5:** What is the main service recommendation the man makes?
- 1. Weekly professional cleaning throughout the year.
- 2. Monthly backwashing service with chemical balancing.
- 3. Quarterly professional maintenance with a yearly package.
- 4. Daily monitoring of the saltwater system.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Weekly professional cleaning throughout the year: The man doesn't recommend weekly professional cleaning.
❌ 2. Monthly backwashing service with chemical balancing: While backwashing is mentioned, it's not offered as a professional service package.
✅ 3. Quarterly professional maintenance with a yearly package: The man states "We also recommend a professional deep cleaning every three months" and offers "a yearly package for $600 that includes all four visits."
❌ 4. Daily monitoring of the saltwater system: No daily professional monitoring is mentioned in the conversation.

**Q6:** Why does the woman want to be home during the first service appointment?
- 1. To ensure the service technicians don't damage her property.
- 2. To learn how to perform routine maintenance herself.
- 3. To show the technician where the equipment is located.
- 4. To discuss additional services she might need.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To ensure the service technicians don't damage her property: This concern is never expressed in the conversation.
✅ 2. To learn how to perform routine maintenance herself: She explicitly states "I'll make sure to be home so you can show me how to do the basic maintenance myself."
❌ 3. To show the technician where the equipment is located: This reason is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. To discuss additional services she might need: While she does discuss services, this isn't given as the reason for being home during the appointment.

✅ 3. 2:20 PM: The woman says the presentation is at 2:30 and recommends "getting there about 10 minutes early to get a good seat," which would be 2:20 PM.
❌ 4. 2:30 PM: This is the start time of the presentation, not the recommended arrival time.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q6:** What is true about the Heritage Gallery?
- 1. It is located near the museum entrance.
- 2. It sells exclusively Indigenous items.
- 3. It closes earlier than the museum.
- 4. It only accepts cash payments.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It is located near the museum entrance: The main gift shop is near the entrance, but the Heritage Gallery is on the lower level.
✅ 2. It sells exclusively Indigenous items: The woman describes it as "a smaller specialty shop called the Heritage Gallery that focuses exclusively on Indigenous arts and crafts."
❌ 3. It closes earlier than the museum: The woman says "They're open until 5:30 today, same as the museum."
❌ 4. It only accepts cash payments: The woman confirms they accept credit cards when she says "Yes, they accept all major credit cards."

**Q7:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He is visiting from another country.
- 2. He is studying Indigenous culture.
- 3. He works at the local hotel.
- 4. He has visited the museum before.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He is visiting from another country: The woman mentions "tax refunds for international visitors" and the man says he doesn't have much Canadian cash, indicating he's from another country.
❌ 2. He is studying Indigenous culture: While he's interested in Indigenous culture, there's no indication he's studying it formally.
❌ 3. He works at the local hotel: There's no mention of him working at a hotel.
❌ 4. He has visited the museum before: There's no indication he has visited before; in fact, he needs help finding exhibits, suggesting this is his first visit.

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will attend another demonstration.
- 2. The man will visit the Heritage Gallery.
- 3. The woman will give the man a guided tour.
- 4. The man will exchange currency at the museum.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will attend another demonstration: There's no indication of another demonstration.
✅ 2. The man will visit the Heritage Gallery: The man expresses interest in purchasing Indigenous artwork, and the woman directs him to the Heritage Gallery, saying "It's on the lower level, just take the stairs or elevator down from the main hall and follow the signs."
❌ 3. The woman will give the man a guided tour: There's no mention of a guided tour.
❌ 4. The man will exchange currency at the museum: The man mentions he doesn't have much Canadian cash, but the woman confirms they accept credit cards, so he won't need to exchange currency.

## Practice 528: Sports Equipment Floater | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a customer and an insurance agent about sports equipment coverage.

### Audio Transcript
$4c

**Q1:** Why did the man call the insurance company?
- 1. To file a claim for stolen sports equipment
- 2. To inquire about coverage for his valuable sports items
- 3. To cancel his current insurance policy
- 4. To report damage to his mountain bike
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To file a claim for stolen sports equipment: The man doesn't mention any theft or need to file a claim.
✅ 2. To inquire about coverage for his valuable sports items: The man states at the beginning, "I recently purchased some expensive sports equipment, and I'm wondering if it's covered under my current plan."
❌ 3. To cancel his current insurance policy: The man shows no intention of canceling his policy.
❌ 4. To report damage to his mountain bike: The man doesn't mention any damage to his bike.

**Q2:** Why is the man concerned about his current insurance coverage?
- 1. His policy is about to expire
- 2. He stores expensive equipment in his garage
- 3. He had a previous claim denied
- 4. His premium recently increased
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. His policy is about to expire: There's no mention of the policy expiring.
✅ 2. He stores expensive equipment in his garage: The man specifically says "I'm concerned because I store them in my garage, and I'm not sure if they're protected."
❌ 3. He had a previous claim denied: No previous claims are mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. His premium recently increased: There's no mention of any premium increase.

**Q3:** What specific detail does the woman mention about the man's current policy?
- 1. It doesn't cover items stored in garages
- 2. It has a $1,000 limit for sports equipment
- 3. It expires at the end of the month
- 4. It doesn't include personal property coverage
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It doesn't cover items stored in garages: The woman never states that garages are excluded from coverage.
✅ 2. It has a $1,000 limit for sports equipment: The woman specifically says "Your current policy does include personal property coverage, but there's a standard limit of $1,000 for sports equipment."
❌ 3. It expires at the end of the month: There is no mention of the policy expiring.
❌ 4. It doesn't include personal property coverage: The woman actually confirms that his policy DOES include personal property coverage.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the Personal Articles Floater?
- 1. It's more comprehensive than the standard policy
- 2. It's primarily designed for professional athletes
- 3. It's a temporary insurance solution
- 4. It's less expensive than upgrading the entire policy
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. It's more comprehensive than the standard policy: This can be inferred because the woman explains it covers the equipment both at home and while traveling, and provides coverage beyond the standard $1,000 limit.
❌ 2. It's primarily designed for professional athletes: The woman actually mentions limitations for professional use, suggesting it's not primarily for professionals.
❌ 3. It's a temporary insurance solution: Nothing suggests this is temporary; it's described as an addition to the existing policy.
❌ 4. It's less expensive than upgrading the entire policy: While it costs $12/month, there's no comparison made to upgrading the entire policy.

**Q5:** What will the man most likely do after this conversation?
- 1. Shop around for better insurance rates
- 2. Move his sports equipment to a safer location
- 3. Send documentation of his equipment to the agent
- 4. Purchase additional sports equipment
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Shop around for better insurance rates: The man seems satisfied with the solution offered and doesn't indicate he'll look elsewhere.
❌ 2. Move his sports equipment to a safer location: There's no suggestion that the man is concerned about the storage location.
✅ 3. Send documentation of his equipment to the agent: The man explicitly states, "I'll send those over this afternoon" when the agent requests photos and receipts.
❌ 4. Purchase additional sports equipment: There's no indication the man plans to buy more equipment.

**Q6:** Why does the man mention that he'll be traveling with his bike?
- 1. To explain why he needs insurance quickly
- 2. To ensure the coverage applies outside his home
- 3. To ask about international coverage options
- 4. To inquire about transportation insurance
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To explain why he needs insurance quickly: The man doesn't express urgency about getting coverage.
✅ 2. To ensure the coverage applies outside his home: The man specifically says "I'd like to make sure everything is fully protected, especially since I'll be traveling with the bike sometimes."
❌ 3. To ask about international coverage options: The man doesn't specifically ask about international coverage.
❌ 4. To inquire about transportation insurance: The man doesn't ask about transportation-specific insurance.

- 2. He recently moved from a warm climate.
- 3. His old clothing is worn out.
- 4. He's preparing for a trip to Edmonton.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He lost his previous winter gear: This is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. He recently moved from a warm climate: The man states, "I just moved to Canada from Australia last month, and I'm completely unprepared for the winter here."
❌ 3. His old clothing is worn out: This is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. He's preparing for a trip to Edmonton: The man is not preparing for a trip; he already lives in Edmonton as he states, "I'm in Edmonton."

**Q2:** What temperature rating does the woman recommend for the man's jacket?
- 1. Minus fifteen degrees
- 2. Minus twenty degrees
- 3. Minus thirty degrees
- 4. Zero degrees
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Minus fifteen degrees: This is the temperature the man experienced yesterday, not the recommended jacket rating.
❌ 2. Minus twenty degrees: This temperature is not mentioned as a recommendation.
✅ 3. Minus thirty degrees: The woman specifically says, "you'll definitely need something rated for at least minus thirty."
❌ 4. Zero degrees: This temperature is only mentioned when the man says he's never experienced anything below zero before.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q3:** What is the man's main concern about the first jacket?
- 1. The style is outdated.
- 2. It's too heavy to wear daily.
- 3. The color doesn't match his preferences.
- 4. It costs more than he planned to spend.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The style is outdated: He doesn't mention any concerns about the style.
❌ 2. It's too heavy to wear daily: Weight is not mentioned as a concern.
❌ 3. The color doesn't match his preferences: Color is not mentioned as a concern for the jacket.
✅ 4. It costs more than he planned to spend: He explicitly states, "I'm a bit concerned about the price. It's quite a bit more than I was planning to spend."

**Q4:** What alternative does the woman suggest for the jacket?
- 1. A different brand with similar features
- 2. Last year's model at 30% off
- 3. A lighter jacket with a removable liner
- 4. A custom-ordered jacket at a discount
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A different brand with similar features: The brand is not mentioned.
✅ 2. Last year's model at 30% off: She specifically offers "last year's design, but the quality is still excellent. It's about 30% off the regular price."
❌ 3. A lighter jacket with a removable liner: This type of jacket is not mentioned.
❌ 4. A custom-ordered jacket at a discount: Custom ordering is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q5:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He has experience with cold weather.
- 2. He wears size 11 shoes.
- 3. He prefers brown boots over black ones.
- 4. He already owns winter accessories.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He has experience with cold weather: He states he's never experienced anything below zero degrees before.
✅ 2. He wears size 11 shoes: He specifically asks for boots in size 11.
❌ 3. He prefers brown boots over black ones: He chooses the black boots when given the option.
❌ 4. He already owns winter accessories: He says "I hadn't even thought about those" when the woman mentions gloves, hat, and scarf.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q6:** What additional items does the woman recommend?
- 1. Thermal underwear and socks
- 2. Sunglasses and sunscreen
- 3. Gloves, hat, and scarf
- 4. Snow pants and goggles
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Thermal underwear and socks: The woman does not mention these items.
❌ 2. Sunglasses and sunscreen: These items are not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. Gloves, hat, and scarf: The woman specifically says, "Your extremities—hands, ears, face—are actually the first to feel the cold" and mentions "matching gloves, hat, and scarf" sets.
❌ 4. Snow pants and goggles: These items are not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q7:** How does the woman feel about the man's situation?
- 1. Sympathetic to his adjustment to the cold
- 2. Frustrated by his limited budget
- 3. Amused by his lack of winter experience
- 4. Concerned he's buying unnecessary items
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Sympathetic to his adjustment to the cold: When the man mentions the cold is a shock, she responds with "That's quite the adjustment!" showing sympathy.
❌ 2. Frustrated by his limited budget: She offers alternatives when he mentions price concerns, showing understanding rather than frustration.
❌ 3. Amused by his lack of winter experience: She doesn't show amusement at his inexperience, but rather helps him professionally.
❌ 4. Concerned he's buying unnecessary items: On the contrary, she suggests additional necessary items he hadn't considered.

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?

- 2. The woman will process the man's purchases with a discount.
- 3. The man will try on more winter clothing items.
- 4. The woman will explain the store's return policy.

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will look for cheaper options elsewhere: The man has decided to purchase items from this store.
✅ 2. The woman will process the man's purchases with a discount: The conversation ends with the woman offering to sign him up for the loyalty program with "10% off today's purchase."
❌ 3. The man will try on more winter clothing items: The man has already decided on his purchases.
❌ 4. The woman will explain the store's return policy: There's no indication this will happen next.

## Practice 532: Kitchen Extension Permit | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a municipal office.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why is the man applying for a housing permit?
- 1. To build a new house in his backyard.
- 2. To expand his kitchen space.
- 3. To renovate his five-year-old house.
- 4. To get approval from his neighbors.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To build a new house in his backyard: The conversation only mentions extending the kitchen, not building a new house.
✅ 2. To expand his kitchen space: The man specifically states "We're looking to add a small extension to our kitchen. It's about 200 square feet, and we want to extend it into our backyard."
❌ 3. To renovate his five-year-old house: While he mentions living in the house for five years, he's specifically applying for a kitchen extension, not general renovations.
❌ 4. To get approval from his neighbors: The man is applying for a municipal permit, not neighbor approval.

**Q2:** Why doesn't the man need neighbor approval for his project?
- 1. Because he already spoke with his neighbors.
- 2. Because the extension is in the backyard only.
- 3. Because the extension is smaller than 250 square feet.
- 4. Because he's owned the property for five years.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Because he already spoke with his neighbors: There is no mention of the man speaking with his neighbors.
❌ 2. Because the extension is in the backyard only: While the extension is in the backyard, this alone is not given as the reason for not needing approval.
✅ 3. Because the extension is smaller than 250 square feet: The woman specifically states "For an extension of less than 250 square feet that doesn't affect the front of the property, you don't need neighbor approval."
❌ 4. Because he's owned the property for five years: The length of ownership is not mentioned as a factor in determining whether neighbor approval is needed.

**Q3:** What document did the man bring that he wasn't sure was necessary?
- 1. His property deed.
- 2. Form B-42.
- 3. His contractor's license.
- 4. Neighbor approval forms.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. His property deed: The man states "Oh, I have my property deed here as well" after the woman mentions he needs proof of property ownership, indicating he brought it but wasn't sure if it was needed.
❌ 2. Form B-42: The man doesn't mention bringing this form; the woman tells him he needs to complete it.
❌ 3. His contractor's license: There is no mention of a contractor's license in the conversation.
❌ 4. Neighbor approval forms: The man asks if he needs "approval from neighbors or something like that," indicating he didn't bring such forms.

**Q4:** What will the man most likely do after this conversation?
- 1. Contact his neighbors for permission.
- 2. Complete and submit Form B-42.
- 3. Pay for the rush processing service.
- 4. Hire a different contractor.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Contact his neighbors for permission: The woman explicitly stated he doesn't need neighbor approval for his project.
✅ 2. Complete and submit Form B-42: The man clearly states at the end, "I'll fill out the Form B-42 right now and get everything submitted today."
❌ 3. Pay for the rush processing service: The man specifically decides against this, saying "I think I'll go with the standard processing time for now."
❌ 4. Hire a different contractor: There's no indication the man is dissatisfied with his current contractor.

**Q5:** What is suggested about the man's contractor?
- 1. He is expensive but highly recommended.
- 2. He has limited availability for projects.
- 3. He helped design the architectural plans.
- 4. He recommended applying for the permit.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He is expensive but highly recommended: There is no mention of the contractor's cost or recommendations in the conversation.
✅ 2. He has limited availability for projects: The man mentions "My contractor has a tight schedule, and I'd really like to secure his services," suggesting the contractor has limited availability.
❌ 3. He helped design the architectural plans: The man specifically states "My contractor helped me prepare them" when referring to the architectural drawings.
❌ 4. He recommended applying for the permit: There is no mention of the contractor recommending the permit application.

**Q6:** Why did the man decide against the rush service?
- 1. To save the additional fee.
- 2. Because his contractor isn't available yet.
- 3. Because he can request it later if needed.
- 4. Because it still wouldn't be fast enough.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To save the additional fee: While this might be a factor, it's not explicitly stated as his reason.
❌ 2. Because his contractor isn't available yet: There's no mention of the contractor not being available yet.
✅ 3. Because he can request it later if needed: The man specifically says "If it looks like it's going to take longer than 20 days, I might come back to request the rush service."
❌ 4. Because it still wouldn't be fast enough: There's no indication that the expedited timeframe of 5-7 days would be insufficient for his needs.

## Practice 538: Greenleaf Garden Plot Availability | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman about a community garden.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why did the woman visit the community garden?
- 1. She was invited by a friend who gardens there.
- 2. She wants to join after discovering it while exploring.
- 3. She needs vegetables for a community event.
- 4. She is researching local gardening practices.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She was invited by a friend who gardens there: The conversation doesn't mention any friend inviting her.
✅ 2. She wants to join after discovering it while exploring: The woman specifically says "I just moved to the neighborhood and I noticed your garden while walking around. I'm really interested in getting a plot."
❌ 3. She needs vegetables for a community event: There's no mention of a community event in the conversation.
❌ 4. She is researching local gardening practices: The woman doesn't indicate she's conducting research; she clearly wants to join the garden.

**Q2:** What will the woman most likely do next?
- 1. Attend the next monthly garden meeting.
- 2. Purchase gardening tools and seeds.
- 3. Return with her completed form and payment.
- 4. Ask her neighbors about their gardening experiences.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Attend the next monthly garden meeting: While meetings are mentioned, the woman's immediate next step is to return with her form and payment.
❌ 2. Purchase gardening tools and seeds: The conversation mentions shared tools are provided, and there's no immediate discussion about buying seeds.
✅ 3. Return with her completed form and payment: The woman explicitly states "I'll take the form and bring it back tomorrow with my payment."
❌ 4. Ask her neighbors about their gardening experiences: There's no indication she plans to do this next.

**Q3:** How many plots became available recently?
- 1. Two plots
- 2. Three plots
- 3. Four plots
- 4. Five plots
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Two plots: The man specifically mentions three plots, not two.
✅ 2. Three plots: The man states "We actually have three plots that just became available last week."
❌ 3. Four plots: The man mentions three plots, not four.
❌ 4. Five plots: The man mentions three plots, not five.

**Q4:** Why are plants taller than six feet not allowed in the garden?
- 1. They might block security cameras in the garden.
- 2. They could prevent sunlight from reaching other plots.
- 3. They are typically invasive species.
- 4. They require more water than the garden can provide.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They might block security cameras in the garden: Security cameras are not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 2. They could prevent sunlight from reaching other plots: The man explains that tall plants are restricted "as they can shade neighboring plots."
❌ 3. They are typically invasive species: While invasive species are not allowed, this is mentioned separately from the height restriction.
❌ 4. They require more water than the garden can provide: Water requirements are not mentioned as a reason for the height restriction.

**Q5:** What is the main community responsibility mentioned for plot holders?
- 1. Contributing volunteer hours for garden maintenance.
- 2. Donating extra produce to local food banks.
- 3. Teaching gardening workshops to new members.
- 4. Helping with fundraising activities.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Contributing volunteer hours for garden maintenance: The man states "we ask that everyone contribute four hours per month to community garden tasks, like maintaining the pathways or composting."
❌ 2. Donating extra produce to local food banks: This is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 3. Teaching gardening workshops to new members: This is not mentioned as a responsibility in the conversation.
❌ 4. Helping with fundraising activities: Fundraising is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q6:** Why does the man mention the garden chat group?
- 1. To warn the woman about required online participation.
- 2. To explain how plot assignments are determined.
- 3. To highlight the social benefits of joining the garden.
- 4. To remind her to bring her phone when gardening.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To warn the woman about required online participation: The chat group is presented as a benefit, not a requirement.
❌ 2. To explain how plot assignments are determined: The man doesn't connect the chat group to plot assignments.
✅ 3. To highlight the social benefits of joining the garden: The man mentions the chat group as a place "where members share tips and organize seed swaps," emphasizing the community aspect.
❌ 4. To remind her to bring her phone when gardening: There's no mention of bringing phones to the garden.

## Practice 546: Dry Cleaning Drop-Off | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a dry cleaning shop. The man works at the dry cleaning shop; the woman is a customer.

### Audio Transcript
$4f

**Q1:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She has used this dry cleaner several times before.
- 2. She was referred to this shop by someone she knows.
- 3. She found the shop through an online advertisement.
- 4. She chose this shop because it is close to her home.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She has used this dry cleaner several times before.: She explicitly said "I've never been to this dry cleaner before."
✅ 2. She was referred to this shop by someone she knows.: She said "A friend of mine recommended it," which means she was referred by someone she knows.
❌ 3. She found the shop through an online advertisement.: There is no mention of an online advertisement; she was recommended by a friend.
❌ 4. She chose this shop because it is close to her home.: There is no mention of the shop's proximity to her home as a reason for choosing it.

**Q2:** How much does the silk dress cleaning cost?
- 1. Fifteen dollars
- 2. Eighteen dollars
- 3. Twenty-two dollars
- 4. Thirty-seven dollars
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Fifteen dollars: Fifteen dollars was the cost of the specialized stain removal, not the dress cleaning itself.
❌ 2. Eighteen dollars: Eighteen dollars was the cost for cleaning the wool coat, not the silk dress.
✅ 3. Twenty-two dollars: The man said "The silk dress is a bit more delicate, so that one would be twenty-two dollars."
❌ 4. Thirty-seven dollars: Thirty-seven dollars is not a price mentioned in the conversation; it does not correspond to any service discussed.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q3:** Why did the man contact the woman?
- 1. Her items were ready earlier than expected.
- 2. He needed her to approve an additional charge.
- 3. A stain was found that required her decision.
- 4. The silk dress was accidentally damaged during cleaning.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Her items were ready earlier than expected.: The items were not ready; the man contacted her because of a problem with the silk dress during cleaning.
❌ 2. He needed her to approve an additional charge.: While an additional charge was involved, the primary reason for contacting her was to inform her about the stain and get her decision on how to proceed.
✅ 3. A stain was found that required her decision.: The man found a red wine stain on the silk dress that wouldn't come out with standard treatment, and he wanted to check with her before trying the specialized process.
❌ 4. The silk dress was accidentally damaged during cleaning.: The dress was not damaged; a pre-existing stain was discovered that needed the woman's input on how to handle.

**Q4:** How does the woman feel about the specialized stain removal?
- 1. She is confident it will work perfectly.
- 2. She is torn between the risk and her need for the dress.
- 3. She is upset about the additional cost.
- 4. She would rather buy a new dress instead.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She is confident it will work perfectly.: She was not confident; she said "That's tricky" and expressed concern about potential damage to the expensive dress.
✅ 2. She is torn between the risk and her need for the dress.: She said the dress was expensive and she'd hate to damage it, but she also really needed to wear it on Saturday, showing she was conflicted.
❌ 3. She is upset about the additional cost.: She did not express upset about the cost; her concern was about potential damage to the fabric.
❌ 4. She would rather buy a new dress instead.: She did not mention buying a new dress; she said she didn't have time to find another dress, so she agreed to the treatment.

**Q5:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He insisted on using the specialized treatment right away.
- 2. He offered a discount on the stain removal service.
- 3. He was upfront about both the cost and the risk involved.
- 4. He guaranteed the stain would be completely removed.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He insisted on using the specialized treatment right away.: He did not insist; he specifically contacted the woman to check with her before proceeding.
❌ 2. He offered a discount on the stain removal service.: He did not offer any discount on the stain removal; the full fifteen dollars was charged.
✅ 3. He was upfront about both the cost and the risk involved.: He told the woman the extra cost was fifteen dollars and honestly mentioned that with silk there's always a small risk the fabric could be affected.
❌ 4. He guaranteed the stain would be completely removed.: He did not guarantee removal; he said there was a risk and wanted to check with her before proceeding.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q6:** What happened when the wool coat was cleaned?
- 1. The cleaning process caused a tear in the lining.
- 2. A pre-existing tear was discovered inside the coat.
- 3. The coat shrank slightly during the cleaning process.
- 4. A hidden stain was found under the collar.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The cleaning process caused a tear in the lining.: The man explicitly said "It wasn't caused by us—it looks like normal wear and tear," so the cleaning did not cause the tear.
✅ 2. A pre-existing tear was discovered inside the coat.: The man said they found a tear along the inside lining during cleaning and that it was from normal wear and tear, meaning it already existed before cleaning.
❌ 3. The coat shrank slightly during the cleaning process.: There is no mention of the coat shrinking at any point in the conversation.
❌ 4. A hidden stain was found under the collar.: No hidden stain was mentioned regarding the coat; the stain issue was with the silk dress.

**Q7:** What does the man suggest the woman do about the coat?
- 1. Bring it back next week for a repair service they offer.
- 2. Visit a nearby tailor who can fix it at a reduced rate.
- 3. Replace the lining entirely at a fabric store.
- 4. Contact the coat's manufacturer for a warranty claim.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Bring it back next week for a repair service they offer.: The man did not offer an in-house repair service; he directed her to a tailor nearby.
✅ 2. Visit a nearby tailor who can fix it at a reduced rate.: The man recommended a tailor named Margaret two doors down and said she would give a ten percent discount if the woman mentioned the dry cleaner.
❌ 3. Replace the lining entirely at a fabric store.: There is no mention of a fabric store or replacing the entire lining.
❌ 4. Contact the coat's manufacturer for a warranty claim.: There is no mention of contacting the manufacturer or any warranty claim.

**Q8:** What will the woman most likely do next?
- 1. Return to the dry cleaner to have the coat repaired.
- 2. Recommend the dry cleaner to people she knows.
- 3. Call the tailor to schedule a fitting appointment.
- 4. Go back inside to ask about the coat's lining.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Return to the dry cleaner to have the coat repaired.: The dry cleaner does not offer repair services; the man referred her to a nearby tailor instead.
✅ 2. Recommend the dry cleaner to people she knows.: The woman explicitly said "I'm definitely going to tell all my friends about this place," making this the most likely next action.
❌ 3. Call the tailor to schedule a fitting appointment.: While the man mentioned a tailor, the woman did not say she would call right away; her stated intention was to recommend the shop to friends.
❌ 4. Go back inside to ask about the coat's lining.: The conversation already addressed the coat's lining issue, and there is no indication she needs to go back inside.

## Practice 547: Dog Grooming Drop-Off | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a pet grooming salon. The woman works at the salon, and the man is a customer bringing his dog in for grooming.

### Audio Transcript
$4f

**Q1:** Why did the man decide to visit this grooming salon?
- 1. A friend recommended it to him recently.
- 2. He wanted a salon nearer to where he lives now.
- 3. His previous groomer had retired from the business.
- 4. He was looking for a salon with special dryers.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A friend recommended it to him recently.: There is no mention of a friend's recommendation. The man found the salon on his own.
✅ 2. He wanted a salon nearer to where he lives now.: The man says "My old groomer was all the way across town, so I figured I'd find somewhere closer" after moving to the neighbourhood.
❌ 3. His previous groomer had retired from the business.: The man does not say his old groomer retired. He simply wanted a closer location.
❌ 4. He was looking for a salon with special dryers.: The man did not know about the low-noise dryer until the woman mentioned it. His reason for visiting was proximity, not equipment.

**Q2:** How long is the grooming expected to take?
- 1. About one hour
- 2. About ninety minutes
- 3. About two hours
- 4. About three hours
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. About one hour: The woman clearly states the grooming "usually takes about two hours," not one hour.
❌ 2. About ninety minutes: The woman says "about two hours," not ninety minutes.
✅ 3. About two hours: The woman explicitly says "It usually takes about two hours."
❌ 4. About three hours: The woman says "about two hours," not three hours.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q3:** How does the man react when the woman mentions the lump?
- 1. He is annoyed that the groomer touched it.
- 2. He is surprised and immediately concerned.
- 3. He is doubtful that it is anything serious.
- 4. He is upset that the vet visit will be costly.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He is annoyed that the groomer touched it.: The man never expresses annoyance. Instead, he says "A lump? I had no idea" and thanks the woman for catching it.
✅ 2. He is surprised and immediately concerned.: The man says "A lump? I had no idea. I never noticed anything when I pet him," showing surprise, and then says "Yeah, absolutely. I'll call right away" about the vet, showing immediate concern.
❌ 3. He is doubtful that it is anything serious.: The man does not dismiss the lump. He immediately agrees to call the vet and even asks for a recommendation, showing he takes it seriously.
❌ 4. He is upset that the vet visit will be costly.: The man never mentions cost or expresses any frustration about the expense of a vet visit.

**Q4:** What did the woman find while grooming Chester?
- 1. A scratch near his left ear
- 2. A small growth hidden under his fur
- 3. Dry, flaky skin around his neck
- 4. A tick buried beneath his ear
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A scratch near his left ear: The woman found a lump, not a scratch.
✅ 2. A small growth hidden under his fur: The woman says she noticed "a small lump underneath the fur" near his left ear, about the size of a marble, which is a small growth hidden under his fur.
❌ 3. Dry, flaky skin around his neck: She did notice dry, flaky skin, but that was a separate observation about his overall skin, not what she specifically found while trimming around his ear.
❌ 4. A tick buried beneath his ear: There is no mention of a tick. She found a lump, not a parasite.

**Q5:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He has been visiting local vets for several weeks.
- 2. He is unfamiliar with services in the neighbourhood.
- 3. He has already scheduled Chester's next checkup.
- 4. He regularly checks Chester's skin for problems.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He has been visiting local vets for several weeks.: The man asks the woman for a vet recommendation, saying he's new to the area, so he hasn't been visiting local vets.
✅ 2. He is unfamiliar with services in the neighbourhood.: He says "we just moved to the neighbourhood about three weeks ago" and asks for vet recommendations because he's new to the area.
❌ 3. He has already scheduled Chester's next checkup.: He called the vet during the grooming visit and scheduled an appointment for the next day, but this was prompted by the woman's discovery, not something he had already planned.
❌ 4. He regularly checks Chester's skin for problems.: He says "I had no idea. I never noticed anything when I pet him," indicating he does not regularly check Chester's skin.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q6:** Why did the woman switch to a different shampoo for Chester?
- 1. The man requested a premium product for his dog.
- 2. The regular shampoo was out of stock that day.
- 3. She observed that Chester's skin appeared irritated.
- 4. Chester had an allergic reaction during the bath.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man requested a premium product for his dog.: The man never requested any specific shampoo. He was surprised when the woman mentioned the switch.
❌ 2. The regular shampoo was out of stock that day.: There is no mention of the regular shampoo being out of stock.
✅ 3. She observed that Chester's skin appeared irritated.: The woman says "His skin seemed a little dry and flaky," which is why she used a hypoallergenic shampoo instead of the regular one.
❌ 4. Chester had an allergic reaction during the bath.: There is no mention of an allergic reaction. The woman simply noticed dry, flaky skin and proactively switched shampoos.

**Q7:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She charged extra for the specialty shampoo.
- 2. She suggested the man visit the vet every six weeks.
- 3. She went beyond basic service to look after the dog's health.
- 4. She recommended a grooming visit every four weeks.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She charged extra for the specialty shampoo.: The woman explicitly says "No, not at all. Same price" when the man asks about an extra charge.
❌ 2. She suggested the man visit the vet every six weeks.: She suggested a grooming visit every six to eight weeks, not a vet visit. She recommended the vet for the lump, not on a recurring schedule.
✅ 3. She went beyond basic service to look after the dog's health.: She noticed the lump and recommended a vet, switched to hypoallergenic shampoo for Chester's dry skin, and used a low-noise dryer — all going beyond basic grooming.
❌ 4. She recommended a grooming visit every four weeks.: She recommended every six to eight weeks for golden retrievers, not every four weeks.

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will purchase a bottle of shampoo before leaving.
- 2. The woman will book an appointment at the veterinary clinic.
- 3. The man will ask for a discount on today's grooming visit.
- 4. The woman will apply a special treatment to Chester's skin.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. The man will purchase a bottle of shampoo before leaving.: The man says "I'll definitely grab one" when the woman mentions the hypoallergenic shampoo bottles at the front counter, so this is the most likely next action.
❌ 2. The woman will book an appointment at the veterinary clinic.: The man already called and booked the vet appointment himself. The woman is not involved in booking vet visits.
❌ 3. The man will ask for a discount on today's grooming visit.: There is no indication the man wants a discount. He is very satisfied with the service.
❌ 4. The woman will apply a special treatment to Chester's skin.: The grooming is already complete. The woman says "here he is! All fresh and handsome," so no further treatment is planned.

## Practice 548: Business Licence Application | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a resident and a clerk at a city licensing office. The man is applying for a home-based business license.

### Audio Transcript
$4f

**Q1:** What is the main reason the man came to the office?
- 1. To renew a business license he already holds
- 2. To register a pet care business formally
- 3. To file a complaint about a neighbor's business
- 4. To inquire about property tax requirements
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To renew a business license he already holds: The man does not have an existing license. He says he wants to "make it official" after doing grooming informally, so he is applying for a new license.
✅ 2. To register a pet care business formally: The man says he wants to apply for a home-based business license for his dog grooming service, which is a pet care business he wants to formalize.
❌ 3. To file a complaint about a neighbor's business: There is no mention of any complaint about a neighbor's business. The man is there to apply for his own license.
❌ 4. To inquire about property tax requirements: The man brought a property tax statement as proof of residence, but his purpose for visiting is to apply for a business license, not to ask about property taxes.

**Q2:** What document did the man bring with him?
- 1. A recent utility bill
- 2. A statement related to property taxes
- 3. A mortgage agreement for his home
- 4. A copy of his driver's license
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A recent utility bill: The woman mentioned a utility bill as one option for proof of residence, but the man said he brought his property tax statement instead.
✅ 2. A statement related to property taxes: The man says "I brought my property tax statement, actually. I came prepared," confirming he brought a document related to property taxes.
❌ 3. A mortgage agreement for his home: There is no mention of a mortgage agreement anywhere in the conversation.
❌ 4. A copy of his driver's license: There is no mention of the man bringing a driver's license. He brought a property tax statement as his proof of residence.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q3:** How does the man feel about the zoning restrictions?
- 1. Relieved that the process is still manageable
- 2. Confused about what the zoning codes mean
- 3. Irritated by the unexpected additional requirement
- 4. Indifferent since he expected complications
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Relieved that the process is still manageable: The man does not express relief when he first hears about the restrictions. He says "That's honestly a bit frustrating," which shows irritation, not relief.
❌ 2. Confused about what the zoning codes mean: While the man didn't know his zoning code, his reaction to the restrictions is frustration, not confusion about what they mean.
✅ 3. Irritated by the unexpected additional requirement: The man explicitly says "That's honestly a bit frustrating" and expresses annoyance that he needs a special-use permit on top of the regular license, especially since he's never had complaints.
❌ 4. Indifferent since he expected complications: The man clearly did not expect this complication. He reacted with surprise ("What? Really?") and frustration, not indifference.

**Q4:** What is true about the man?
- 1. His neighbors have raised concerns about noise from his service.
- 2. He has been providing grooming services to people in his area.
- 3. He recently relocated to a residential neighborhood.
- 4. He already secured approval from the zoning board.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. His neighbors have raised concerns about noise from his service: The man specifically says he has never had a single complaint from his neighbors, and most of them are actually his customers.
✅ 2. He has been providing grooming services to people in his area: The man says he has been doing dog grooming informally for friends and neighbors for about two years, which means he has been providing services to people in his area.
❌ 3. He recently relocated to a residential neighborhood: There is no mention in the conversation of the man recently moving. He appears to be an established resident.
❌ 4. He already secured approval from the zoning board: He has not yet received approval from the zoning board. The woman is submitting his special-use permit request, and the board meeting hasn't happened yet.

**Q5:** When does the zoning board convene?
- 1. Every other Friday of the month
- 2. On the initial Wednesday of each month
- 3. Once every two weeks on Monday
- 4. On the last business day of each month
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Every other Friday of the month: The conversation states the board meets on the first Wednesday of every month, not every other Friday.
✅ 2. On the initial Wednesday of each month: The woman says "They meet on the first Wednesday of every month," which matches "the initial Wednesday of each month."
❌ 3. Once every two weeks on Monday: There is no mention of meetings on Mondays or biweekly meetings. The board meets monthly on Wednesdays.
❌ 4. On the last business day of each month: The board meets on the first Wednesday, not the last business day of each month.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q6:** What is the purpose of the woman's final announcement?
- 1. To inform the man about a funding opportunity for new businesses
- 2. To let the man know about a discount on his licensing fees
- 3. To remind the man about the upcoming home inspection
- 4. To suggest the man attend a mandatory orientation session
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. To inform the man about a funding opportunity for new businesses: The woman tells the man about a small business support program that offers a grant of up to five hundred dollars to help cover startup costs, which is a funding opportunity.
❌ 2. To let the man know about a discount on his licensing fees: The woman does not mention any discount. She mentions a grant program, which is separate from a discount on fees.
❌ 3. To remind the man about the upcoming home inspection: The home inspection was discussed earlier. The final announcement is about the grant program, not the inspection.
❌ 4. To suggest the man attend a mandatory orientation session: There is no mention of any orientation session in the conversation.

**Q7:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She runs the small business support office on another floor.
- 2. She made the man aware of a recently introduced financial aid program.
- 3. She personally carries out the home safety inspections.
- 4. She waived a portion of the licensing fees for the man.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She runs the small business support office on another floor: She directs the man to the small business office on the second floor, but she does not run it. She works at the licensing office.
✅ 2. She made the man aware of a recently introduced financial aid program: The woman told the man about a new small business support program (announced just last month) that offers grants up to five hundred dollars, which is a recently introduced financial aid program.
❌ 3. She personally carries out the home safety inspections: She says "We'll send an inspector out to your address," indicating someone else does the inspections, not her personally.
❌ 4. She waived a portion of the licensing fees for the man: The woman charged the full amount of $195 (seventy-five plus one hundred and twenty). She did not waive any fees.

**Q8:** What will the man most likely do next?
- 1. Head home to get his garage ready for inspection
- 2. Visit another office in the building to apply for a grant
- 3. Schedule a meeting with the zoning board members
- 4. Return to the clerk's desk to complete additional forms
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Head home to get his garage ready for inspection: While an inspection will happen, the man expressed interest in visiting the small business office before it closes at four-thirty, making that his more immediate next step.
✅ 2. Visit another office in the building to apply for a grant: The woman recommended stopping by the small business office on the second floor, and the man said "I'll definitely do that," indicating this is what he will do next.
❌ 3. Schedule a meeting with the zoning board members: The woman already flagged his application as urgent for the upcoming board meeting. There is no indication he needs to personally schedule a meeting.
❌ 4. Return to the clerk's desk to complete additional forms: The woman confirmed there are no more forms, and the man has already paid. His paperwork at this office is complete.

## Practice 549: New Patient Prescription | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a community pharmacy. The woman is the pharmacist; the man is a customer picking up a prescription.

### Audio Transcript
$4f

**Q1:** Why did the man come to the pharmacy?
- 1. To ask about treatment options for his condition
- 2. To collect a medication his doctor ordered for him
- 3. To move his prescription records from another location
- 4. To register his workplace insurance coverage
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To ask about treatment options for his condition: The man came to pick up a specific prescription, not to ask about treatment options.
✅ 2. To collect a medication his doctor ordered for him: The man says "My doctor's office said they called in a prescription for me earlier today," and he is there to pick it up.
❌ 3. To move his prescription records from another location: While his previous pharmacy is mentioned, his primary purpose is to pick up a new prescription, not to transfer records.
❌ 4. To register his workplace insurance coverage: He doesn't even have his insurance information ready and says his employer may not have set it up yet.

**Q2:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He has lived in the area for several months.
- 2. He recently started a position with a new employer.
- 3. He brought along his benefit plan details.
- 4. He contacted the pharmacy to send his prescription.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He has lived in the area for several months.: He says he moved to the neighbourhood "about two weeks ago," not several months.
✅ 2. He recently started a position with a new employer.: He says "I only started the job last Monday," confirming he recently began a new position.
❌ 3. He brought along his benefit plan details.: He says he's "not sure if my new employer has set up my insurance yet" and does not have insurance information with him.
❌ 4. He contacted the pharmacy to send his prescription.: His doctor's office called in the prescription, not the man himself.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q3:** Why does the woman want to reach the doctor's office?
- 1. The medication type does not match the man's condition.
- 2. The prescription appears to have been sent to the wrong pharmacy.
- 3. The prescribed strength is greater than what the man was previously taking.
- 4. The man's medical history is incomplete in the pharmacy's records.

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The medication type does not match the man's condition.: The medication type (Lisinopril for blood pressure) is not questioned. The concern is about the dosage.
❌ 2. The prescription appears to have been sent to the wrong pharmacy.: There is no indication the prescription was sent to the wrong pharmacy.
✅ 3. The prescribed strength is greater than what the man was previously taking.: The woman noticed the man was previously on 5 milligrams but the new prescription is for 10 milligrams — double the dose — and she wants to confirm this increase is intentional.
❌ 4. The man's medical history is incomplete in the pharmacy's records.: The concern is specifically about the dosage increase, not about incomplete medical history.

**Q4:** How does the man feel about the pharmacist raising the dosage concern?
- 1. Irritated that the process is being delayed unnecessarily
- 2. Confident that the doctor prescribed the correct amount
- 3. Thankful that the pharmacist identified the discrepancy
- 4. Embarrassed that he did not pay attention at his appointment

❌ 2. Confident that the doctor prescribed the correct amount: The man did not express confidence about the dosage. He said he "didn't realize" the change and was "a bit distracted" during the appointment.
✅ 3. Thankful that the pharmacist identified the discrepancy: The man explicitly says "I appreciate you catching that," showing gratitude for the pharmacist flagging the issue.
❌ 4. Embarrassed that he did not pay attention at his appointment: While he mentions being distracted, his tone is not one of embarrassment but rather appreciation for the pharmacist's diligence.

**Q5:** What time is it when the woman mentions contacting the doctor?
- 1. 3:30
- 2. 4:00
- 3. 4:30
- 4. 5:00

- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. 3:30: This time is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. 4:00: This time is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. 4:30: The woman says "it's already 4:30, and they might close at 5:00" when she mentions needing to contact the doctor's office.
❌ 4. 5:00: 5:00 is mentioned as the time the doctor's office might close, not the current time when the woman is speaking.

$4e

**Q6:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She spoke with the doctor shortly before the man returned.

- 3. She phoned the man to let him know the prescription was ready.
- 4. She determined that the dosage increase was a mistake.

❌ 2. She successfully contacted the clinic the previous afternoon.: She explicitly says "I tried calling yesterday afternoon, but I couldn't get through before they closed," so she did not successfully contact them the previous afternoon.
❌ 3. She phoned the man to let him know the prescription was ready.: There is no mention of the woman calling the man. The man returned to the pharmacy on his own.
❌ 4. She determined that the dosage increase was a mistake.: The doctor confirmed the increase to 10 milligrams was intentional, so it was not a mistake.

**Q7:** When does the walk-in vaccination clinic begin?

- 2. Tuesday, October 8th
- 3. Tuesday, October 15th
- 4. Tuesday, October 22nd

❌ 2. Tuesday, October 8th: This date is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 3. Tuesday, October 15th: The woman clearly states "Our flu shot clinic starts next Tuesday, October 15th."
❌ 4. Tuesday, October 22nd: This date is not mentioned in the conversation.

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will book an appointment for a flu vaccination.
- 2. The man will purchase his medication and head home.
- 3. The woman will submit the man's insurance claim.
- 4. The woman will call the doctor to confirm the follow-up plan.

✅ 2. The man will purchase his medication and head home.: The man said "I'll pay out of pocket for now" after learning the cost, and the dosage has been confirmed, so the most logical next step is that he pays and leaves.

❌ 4. The woman will call the doctor to confirm the follow-up plan.: The woman already confirmed the dosage with the doctor's office, so there is no need to call again.

- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Confused about how the booking system works: The man understands how to use the app to book rooms; his issue is that no rooms are available, not that he is confused by the system.
✅ 2. Annoyed that the situation is harder than anticipated: The man says "That's a bit frustrating. I really wasn't expecting it to be this difficult to find a quiet space," which directly shows he is annoyed that it was harder than he anticipated.
❌ 3. Disappointed that the phone booths are too small: The man never complains about the size of the phone booths. His concern is that they can't be reserved and fill up quickly.
❌ 4. Angry at the staff for not helping sooner: The man never expresses anger toward the staff. In fact, he thanks the woman for her help and appreciates her efforts.

**Q5:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She guarantees a phone booth will be available for the man.
- 2. She advises the man to reschedule his call to a quieter day.
- 3. She proactively searches for an alternative solution for the man.
- 4. She suggests the man work from the lounge area during his call.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She guarantees a phone booth will be available for the man.: The woman explicitly says phone booths are first come, first served and tend to fill up quickly on busy days. She does not guarantee availability.
❌ 2. She advises the man to reschedule his call to a quieter day.: The woman never suggests rescheduling the call. Instead, she looks for alternative solutions.
✅ 3. She proactively searches for an alternative solution for the man.: The woman says "let me check something for you" and looks for last-minute cancellations on her own initiative, ultimately finding an available meeting room for the man.
❌ 4. She suggests the man work from the lounge area during his call.: The woman never suggests using the lounge for his call. She suggests phone booths and then finds a meeting room.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q6:** What is the main topic of the woman's suggestion in this section?
- 1. How to use the meeting room booking app more effectively
- 2. A membership tier that provides more meeting room access
- 3. A discounted rate on additional meeting room hours
- 4. The cancellation policy for meeting room reservations
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. How to use the meeting room booking app more effectively: The woman does not discuss how to use the app more effectively in this section. Her suggestion is about upgrading the membership plan.
✅ 2. A membership tier that provides more meeting room access: The woman suggests upgrading to the Pro Plan, which costs $275/month and includes 10 hours of meeting room time and priority booking — a higher membership tier with more meeting room access.
❌ 3. A discounted rate on additional meeting room hours: The woman does not mention any discounted rate. She discusses a different plan tier at a higher price.
❌ 4. The cancellation policy for meeting room reservations: The cancellation policy is never discussed. The woman's suggestion is about upgrading to the Pro Plan.

**Q7:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He has committed to paying for the upgraded membership.
- 2. He believes his current allocation of meeting room time is adequate.
- 3. He intends to evaluate a higher plan before making a final decision.
- 4. He prefers to continue using phone booths instead of meeting rooms.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He has committed to paying for the upgraded membership.: The man has not committed to paying. He accepted a free one-week trial and said he wants to "try it out and see if it's worth the upgrade."
❌ 2. He believes his current allocation of meeting room time is adequate.: The man says "3 hours a month definitely won't be enough" since he has client calls at least twice a week, so he clearly finds it inadequate.
✅ 3. He intends to evaluate a higher plan before making a final decision.: The man says "Let me try it out and see if it's worth the upgrade," showing he wants to evaluate the Pro Plan during the free trial before deciding.
❌ 4. He prefers to continue using phone booths instead of meeting rooms.: There is no indication the man prefers phone booths. He was relieved to get a meeting room and is considering upgrading for more meeting room access.

**Q8:** What will the man most likely do next?
- 1. Return to the front desk to complete his plan upgrade.
- 2. Go to the assigned room to prepare for his call.
- 3. Head to the third floor to reserve a phone booth.
- 4. Download the app to book additional meeting room time.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Return to the front desk to complete his plan upgrade.: The man accepted a free trial of the Pro Plan, not a full upgrade. He said he wants to try it out first, and the woman already set it up, so there's no need to return to the desk.
✅ 2. Go to the assigned room to prepare for his call.: The woman tells him Meeting Room B is "just down the hall on your left" and gives him the door code 7721, indicating he will head there next to prepare for his 2:30 call.
❌ 3. Head to the third floor to reserve a phone booth.: The man no longer needs a phone booth since he secured Meeting Room B for his call.
❌ 4. Download the app to book additional meeting room time.: There is no mention of the man needing to download the app at this point. His immediate next step is to go to Meeting Room B.
