## Practice 551: Vegetable Garden Consultation | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a garden center. The man works at the garden center, and the woman is a customer looking for help with her new yard.

### Audio Transcript
$4f

**Q1:** What is the woman's main reason for visiting the garden center?
- 1. To register for a gardening workshop
- 2. To seek guidance on creating a vegetable plot
- 3. To buy indoor plants for her new residence
- 4. To find plants that grow well in the shade
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To register for a gardening workshop: She only learns about the workshop during the conversation; it was not her reason for visiting.
✅ 2. To seek guidance on creating a vegetable plot: The woman says she'd like to start a vegetable garden in her backyard and asks for help, which is her main reason for visiting.
❌ 3. To buy indoor plants for her new residence: She mentions keeping houseplants alive but is specifically looking to start an outdoor vegetable garden.
❌ 4. To find plants that grow well in the shade: Her primary goal is a vegetable garden in a sunny area; the shade plants were an afterthought.

**Q2:** How does the woman feel about beginning an outdoor garden?
- 1. She is confident because of her prior experience.
- 2. She is somewhat uneasy about the undertaking.
- 3. She is frustrated by the limited space available.
- 4. She is disappointed with the amount of sunlight.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She is confident because of her prior experience: She says she has almost no experience and is "a little nervous," so she is not confident.
✅ 2. She is somewhat uneasy about the undertaking: The woman explicitly says "I'm a little nervous about starting something outdoors," showing she feels uneasy.
❌ 3. She is frustrated by the limited space available: She never expresses frustration about space; the man even says her space is great for a beginner.
❌ 4. She is disappointed with the amount of sunlight: She does not express disappointment about sunlight; the man confirms six hours is sufficient for her desired plants.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q3:** What is the regular cost of the organic garden soil per bag?
- 1. $$12.99
- 2. $$15.00
- 3. $$18.99
- 4. $$25.00
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $12.99: This is the sale price of the soil, not the regular price. The man says it's "twelve ninety-nine a bag instead of the regular eighteen ninety-nine."
❌ 2. $15.00: This is the flat fee for the delivery service, not the price of the soil.
✅ 3. $18.99: The man clearly states the regular price is eighteen ninety-nine per bag, and it is currently on sale for twelve ninety-nine.
❌ 4. $25.00: This is the original price of the rosebush on the clearance table, not the soil price.

**Q4:** Why does the man advise against planting hostas in the woman's main garden bed?
- 1. They require significantly more watering than vegetables.
- 2. They cannot tolerate direct sunlight for extended periods.
- 3. They tend to spread and crowd out nearby plants.
- 4. They attract pests that could damage tomato seedlings.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. They require significantly more watering than vegetables: The man never mentions watering requirements for hostas.
✅ 2. They cannot tolerate direct sunlight for extended periods: The man says hostas "actually need full shade to do well" and "wouldn't survive in your sunny garden bed," meaning they cannot handle the six hours of direct sun.
❌ 3. They tend to spread and crowd out nearby plants: The man never mentions hostas spreading or crowding other plants.
❌ 4. They attract pests that could damage tomato seedlings: The man never mentions any pest issues related to hostas.

**Q5:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She has been growing vegetables in containers for years.
- 2. She wants her outdoor space to feature ornamental plants as well.
- 3. She prefers cherry tomatoes over all other vegetable varieties.
- 4. She has already amended the soil in her garden bed.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She has been growing vegetables in containers for years: The woman says she has only kept a few houseplants alive and has no real gardening experience.
✅ 2. She wants her outdoor space to feature ornamental plants as well: The woman says "Everything can't just be vegetables, you know?" and wants decorative plants like hostas alongside her vegetables.
❌ 3. She prefers cherry tomatoes over all other vegetable varieties: While she buys cherry tomatoes, she never states they are her preference over all other varieties; the man recommended them as the most popular.
❌ 4. She has already amended the soil in her garden bed: She is buying soil at the garden center, so she has not yet prepared her garden bed.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q6:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He suggests the woman return on a different day for the soil.
- 2. He checks storage to locate additional stock for the customer.
- 3. He recommends the woman purchase fewer bags to save money.
- 4. He offers to personally deliver the order to the woman's house.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He suggests the woman return on a different day for the soil: He does not suggest she come back; instead, he checks the warehouse for more bags.
✅ 2. He checks storage to locate additional stock for the customer: The man says "Let me check if we have more bags in the back warehouse" and finds three more bags there.
❌ 3. He recommends the woman purchase fewer bags to save money: He never suggests buying fewer bags; he helps her get the six bags she needs.
❌ 4. He offers to personally deliver the order to the woman's house: He offers the store's delivery service, not a personal delivery.

**Q7:** What is the conversation in this section mainly about?
- 1. Selecting the best plants for a shaded area
- 2. Negotiating a lower price on clearance items
- 3. Arranging the logistics for the woman's purchase
- 4. Planning the layout of the woman's new garden
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Selecting the best plants for a shaded area: While hostas for a shaded area are briefly discussed, this is not the main topic of the overall conversation.
❌ 2. Negotiating a lower price on clearance items: There is no negotiation; the man simply mentions a clearance rosebush and the woman accepts the price.
✅ 3. Arranging the logistics for the woman's purchase: The conversation covers selecting items, checking stock availability, dealing with the transportation problem, and scheduling delivery — all logistics related to her purchase.
❌ 4. Planning the layout of the woman's new garden: While some planting advice is given, the conversation focuses more on purchasing products and arranging delivery than on garden layout planning.

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The woman will drive back to pick up the remaining items.
- 2. The woman will receive her purchases at her home.
- 3. The man will help the woman load everything into her car.
- 4. The man will plant the seedlings in the woman's garden.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The woman will drive back to pick up the remaining items: The man arranged a delivery service because the woman's car is too small, so she won't need to come back.
✅ 2. The woman will receive her purchases at her home: The man scheduled a delivery for the next afternoon between two and four, so she will receive everything at home.
❌ 3. The man will help the woman load everything into her car: The woman said her small car can't fit everything, so they arranged delivery instead of loading the car.
❌ 4. The man will plant the seedlings in the woman's garden: There is no mention of the man offering to plant anything at her home.

## Practice 552: Parents&#x27; Anniversary Dinner | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a brother and a sister who are planning a surprise anniversary dinner for their parents.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What is the main purpose of this conversation?
- 1. To decide on a gift for their parents' wedding.
- 2. To organize the details of a celebration for their parents.
- 3. To choose a restaurant for a family reunion.
- 4. To figure out how to afford a party for their parents.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To decide on a gift for their parents' wedding: The gift is briefly mentioned, but the parents are already married — it's their anniversary, not their wedding.
✅ 2. To organize the details of a celebration for their parents: The entire conversation revolves around finalizing plans for a surprise anniversary dinner.
❌ 3. To choose a restaurant for a family reunion: While a restaurant is discussed, the event is specifically an anniversary dinner, not a general family reunion.
❌ 4. To figure out how to afford a party for their parents: Cost is discussed briefly, but the conversation covers many other logistics like timing, the guest list, and the surprise.

**Q2:** Why was the reservation time changed?
- 1. The restaurant was unavailable at the original time.
- 2. Their father tends to get weary in the evening.
- 3. The children need to go to bed early.
- 4. The woman has to work the following morning.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The restaurant was unavailable at the original time: The restaurant's availability was never an issue; they hadn't confirmed yet.
✅ 2. Their father tends to get weary in the evening: The woman says 'Dad gets tired pretty early these days,' so they moved it from seven to six.
❌ 3. The children need to go to bed early: The children's bedtime is never mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. The woman has to work the following morning: The woman's work schedule is not discussed.

**Q3:** What can be inferred about the man's financial situation?
- 1. He is struggling to cover his share of the costs.
- 2. He recently had unexpected expenses.
- 3. He is doing well enough to be generous.
- 4. He is borrowing money to pay for the dinner.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He is struggling to cover his share of the costs: He actually offers to pay more than half, which suggests the opposite.
❌ 2. He recently had unexpected expenses: It is the WOMAN who had recent dental work, not the man.
✅ 3. He is doing well enough to be generous: He says 'Business has been pretty good lately' and volunteers to cover about sixty percent.
❌ 4. He is borrowing money to pay for the dinner: There is no mention of borrowing money anywhere in the conversation.

**Q4:** What gift did the man prepare for their parents?
- 1. A restored photograph printed on fabric material.
- 2. A framed portrait taken at the restaurant.
- 3. A custom painting based on an old family photo.
- 4. A professional photo session for the couple.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. A restored photograph printed on fabric material: He found a wedding photo, had it restored, and printed on canvas — a fabric-based material.
❌ 2. A framed portrait taken at the restaurant: The photo is from their wedding day, not from any restaurant.
❌ 3. A custom painting based on an old family photo: It's a restored photograph printed on canvas, not a painting.
❌ 4. A professional photo session for the couple: He had an existing photo restored, not a new session arranged.

**Q5:** What will the man most likely do on the evening of the dinner?
- 1. Arrive early to decorate the private dining room.
- 2. Pick up the cake and bring it to the restaurant.
- 3. Bring his parents to the restaurant under a false pretense.
- 4. Call the restaurant to confirm the reservation.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Arrive early to decorate the private dining room: Decorating is never discussed; the plan is for everyone to be seated when the parents arrive.
❌ 2. Pick up the cake and bring it to the restaurant: The cake is the woman's responsibility — she says 'I've got the cake.'
✅ 3. Bring his parents to the restaurant under a false pretense: He plans to tell them he's taking them for a casual dinner so they won't suspect the surprise.
❌ 4. Call the restaurant to confirm the reservation: The woman says she will confirm the booking, not the man.

## Practice 553: Riverside Pottery Workshop | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two coworkers. They are discussing a pottery class at a local community centre.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What are the two coworkers mainly discussing?
- 1. How to find creative hobbies outside of work.
- 2. Signing up for an arts course at a nearby facility.
- 3. Comparing different classes offered at a community centre.
- 4. Deciding on a holiday gift idea for their friends.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. How to find creative hobbies outside of work: While creativity comes up, the conversation is specifically about one particular class, not a general search for hobbies.
✅ 2. Signing up for an arts course at a nearby facility: The entire conversation revolves around enrolling in a beginner pottery class at the Riverside Community Centre.
❌ 3. Comparing different classes offered at a community centre: They only discuss one class — pottery — and do not compare multiple offerings.
❌ 4. Deciding on a holiday gift idea for their friends: Holiday gifts are only joked about at the very end; it's not the main topic.

**Q2:** Why did the woman decide against the Wednesday session?
- 1. She has a regular sports commitment that evening.
- 2. She wouldn't have an opportunity to eat beforehand.
- 3. The Thursday instructor received better reviews.
- 4. She prefers to keep Wednesday evenings free for relaxation.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She has a regular sports commitment that evening: The basketball game on Wednesdays is the MAN's conflict, not the woman's.
✅ 2. She wouldn't have an opportunity to eat beforehand: She says on Wednesdays she'd be 'rushing straight from here without time for dinner.'
❌ 3. The Thursday instructor received better reviews: There is no mention of different instructors for each session.
❌ 4. She prefers to keep Wednesday evenings free for relaxation: She doesn't mention wanting Wednesdays free for relaxation.

**Q3:** How much will each person pay if they register before the deadline?
- 1. One hundred and forty dollars.
- 2. One hundred and fifteen dollars.
- 3. Twenty-five dollars less than the standard fee for both combined.
- 4. Half the regular registration fee.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. One hundred and forty dollars: This is the standard price without the early-bird discount.
✅ 2. One hundred and fifteen dollars: The woman says it 'drops down to a hundred and fifteen' per person if they register before October fifteenth.
❌ 3. Twenty-five dollars less than the standard fee for both combined: The twenty-five dollar reduction applies per person, not combined.
❌ 4. Half the regular registration fee: A fifty percent reduction is never mentioned; the discount is twenty-five dollars off.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the man's previous experience with pottery?
- 1. He has formal training from a different community centre.
- 2. He has some limited, informal experience with clay.
- 3. He tried wheel throwing but found it too difficult.
- 4. He took a beginner course several years ago.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He has formal training from a different community centre: He never mentions any formal instruction or classes.
✅ 2. He has some limited, informal experience with clay: He mentions making 'a little bowl' with 'basic stuff with air-dry clay' at a friend's cabin, suggesting casual, minimal experience.
❌ 3. He tried wheel throwing but found it too difficult: Wheel throwing is mentioned as part of the upcoming class; he didn't say he's tried it before.
❌ 4. He took a beginner course several years ago: His previous experience was informal at a cabin, not a structured course.

**Q5:** What will the woman most likely do later that evening?
- 1. Visit the community centre to register in person.
- 2. Email the class instructor to ask about materials.
- 3. Sign up on the website and share the registration link with the man.
- 4. Call the man's work phone to confirm the schedule.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Visit the community centre to register in person: She says online is easier and she plans to register online tonight.
❌ 2. Email the class instructor to ask about materials: She already knows materials are included; she doesn't mention contacting the instructor.
✅ 3. Sign up on the website and share the registration link with the man: She says 'I was going to register tonight. I can send you the link.'
❌ 4. Call the man's work phone to confirm the schedule: The man specifically asks her to send the link to his personal email, not his work contact.

## Practice 554: Charity Fun Run | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two friends who are discussing a local charity fun run.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What is the main purpose of this conversation?
- 1. To decide which charity to donate to.
- 2. To organize their participation in an upcoming race.
- 3. To compare different running events in the area.
- 4. To create a long-term fitness routine together.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To decide which charity to donate to: The charity (children's hospital) is already established; they aren't choosing one.
✅ 2. To organize their participation in an upcoming race: They discuss signing up for the Riverside Run for Hope, choosing the distance, and setting a training schedule.
❌ 3. To compare different running events in the area: The woman mentions a past run, but they aren't comparing events.
❌ 4. To create a long-term fitness routine together: Their training plan is specifically for the five-week period before the charity run, not a long-term routine.

**Q2:** Why does the man decide against the longer race distance?
- 1. He has been experiencing joint pain recently.
- 2. He cannot afford the higher registration fee.
- 3. He acknowledges he has not been physically active for years.
- 4. He does not have enough weeks to prepare for it.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He has been experiencing joint pain recently: It is the WOMAN who mentions knee soreness, not the man.
❌ 2. He cannot afford the higher registration fee: He says thirty-five dollars 'isn't bad at all' and doesn't mention cost as a concern for choosing distance.
✅ 3. He acknowledges he has not been physically active for years: He says 'five K is more than enough for someone who's been sitting at a desk for eight years.'
❌ 4. He does not have enough weeks to prepare for it: He doesn't cite the five-week timeline as the reason; his concern is his fitness level.

**Q3:** What is the discounted registration fee for the race they plan to enter?
- 1. Fifteen dollars
- 2. Thirty-five dollars
- 3. Fifty dollars
- 4. Sixty-five dollars
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Fifteen dollars: Fifteen dollars is the amount the price increases after the early bird deadline.
✅ 2. Thirty-five dollars: The early bird rate for the five K is thirty-five dollars, and they plan to do the five K.
❌ 3. Fifty dollars: Fifty dollars is the early bird price for the ten K, which they decided not to enter.
❌ 4. Sixty-five dollars: This would be the ten K price after the early bird deadline, not their chosen race's discounted fee.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the woman's approach to physical activity?
- 1. She is competitive and wants to finish with a fast time.
- 2. She is cautious and prefers not to push beyond her limits.
- 3. She is inexperienced and unsure about running events.
- 4. She is mainly motivated by raising money for charity.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She is competitive and wants to finish with a fast time: She says she'd 'rather finish feeling good than push too hard,' showing no competitive focus.
✅ 2. She is cautious and prefers not to push beyond her limits: She chooses the shorter distance, mentions being careful with her knee, and wants to add weekend runs only in the final two weeks.
❌ 3. She is inexperienced and unsure about running events: She completed a five K last spring and confidently suggests a training plan, so she is not inexperienced.
❌ 4. She is mainly motivated by raising money for charity: While she mentions supporting the hospital, her planning focuses on training and managing her physical condition.

**Q5:** What will the man and woman most likely do first?
- 1. Begin running on the weekend to build endurance.
- 2. Register for the event and then meet for a training session.
- 3. Visit the community centre to pick up race information.
- 4. Consult a doctor about the woman's knee condition.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Begin running on the weekend to build endurance: They agree to add weekend runs only in the last two weeks, not right away.
✅ 2. Register for the event and then meet for a training session: The man says he'll register tonight, and they agree to meet Tuesday morning at the Birchwood trail.
❌ 3. Visit the community centre to pick up race information: The man already saw the flyer there; they plan to sign up online.
❌ 4. Consult a doctor about the woman's knee condition: While she mentions knee soreness, neither of them suggests seeing a doctor.

## Practice 555: Lakewood First Aid Certification | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation. A man is looking to enroll in a first aid course.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why did the man decide against the Emergency First Aid course?
- 1. It does not include CPR Level C training.
- 2. His company specifically requires the longer course.
- 3. The Emergency course is not available in May.
- 4. He wants more comprehensive training for personal reasons.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It does not include CPR Level C training: While CPR Level C is mentioned, the reason he chose Standard is his employer's requirement, not the CPR component specifically.
✅ 2. His company specifically requires the longer course: He says 'my employer was specific about needing the Standard one' even though he considered Emergency to save money.
❌ 3. The Emergency course is not available in May: The availability of Emergency First Aid in May is never discussed.
❌ 4. He wants more comprehensive training for personal reasons: He actually wanted to save money with Emergency First Aid but his employer dictated the choice.

**Q2:** Where will the training sessions be held?
- 1. At Lakewood Training Centre on Pine Street
- 2. At Greenfield Construction's training facility
- 3. At a community centre on Pine Street
- 4. At a conference room in the west parking complex
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. At Lakewood Training Centre on Pine Street: Lakewood Training Centre is the name of the company offering the course, not the venue.
❌ 2. At Greenfield Construction's training facility: Greenfield Construction is the man's new employer, not a training location.
✅ 3. At a community centre on Pine Street: The woman says it's held at 'the Riverside Community Centre on Pine Street, Room 214.'
❌ 4. At a conference room in the west parking complex: The west lot is mentioned only as a free parking area, not a venue.

**Q3:** What must participants do before the course begins?
- 1. Submit their government-issued photo ID online.
- 2. Finish a preparatory module on the internet.
- 3. Attend a forty-five-minute orientation at the centre.
- 4. Register their parking pass through the website.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Submit their government-issued photo ID online: A photo ID must be brought on the first day, not submitted online beforehand.
✅ 2. Finish a preparatory module on the internet: The woman says 'You'll need to complete an online pre-course module through our website.'
❌ 3. Attend a forty-five-minute orientation at the centre: The 45 minutes refers to the online module, not an in-person orientation.
❌ 4. Register their parking pass through the website: Parking is free in the west lot and no pass registration is mentioned.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the May course session?
- 1. It was added recently to accommodate extra demand.
- 2. It could fill up before the registration deadline.
- 3. It is less popular than the June session.
- 4. It will likely be rescheduled due to low enrollment.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It was added recently to accommodate extra demand: There is no mention of the session being newly added.
✅ 2. It could fill up before the registration deadline: The class holds 16 people and already has 12 registered, suggesting only 4 spots remain and it may sell out soon.
❌ 3. It is less popular than the June session: The May session is nearly full while no enrollment numbers are given for June, suggesting the opposite.
❌ 4. It will likely be rescheduled due to low enrollment: With 12 of 16 spots taken, enrollment is high, not low.

**Q5:** What does the woman mainly advise the man to do?
- 1. Complete the online module as soon as possible.
- 2. Register for the course without delay.
- 3. Choose the June session for a better experience.
- 4. Arrive early on the first day to secure his spot.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Complete the online module as soon as possible: The online module is mentioned as a requirement, but her main recommendation is about registering.
✅ 2. Register for the course without delay: She says 'the May session already has 12 registered. I'd recommend signing up today.'
❌ 3. Choose the June session for a better experience: She mentions June as an alternative but doesn't recommend it over May.
❌ 4. Arrive early on the first day to secure his spot: Arriving early on the course day is never discussed.

**Q6:** What will the man most likely do this weekend?
- 1. Attend the first day of the training course.
- 2. Visit the Riverside Community Centre to confirm his spot.
- 3. Work through the required online learning material.
- 4. Pick up his course textbook from the training centre.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Attend the first day of the training course: The course starts on May 17th, not this coming weekend.
❌ 2. Visit the Riverside Community Centre to confirm his spot: He registers at the store and there is no mention of visiting the venue beforehand.
✅ 3. Work through the required online learning material: He says 'I'll get the online module done this weekend so I'm ready for the 17th.'
❌ 4. Pick up his course textbook from the training centre: No textbook or materials pickup is mentioned in the conversation.

## Practice 556: Street Parking Permit | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation. A man visits a city office to apply for a street parking permit.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why did the man decide not to rent a private parking lot?
- 1. There were no available lots near his home.
- 2. He found the monthly rate unreasonable.
- 3. The lot did not have spaces in his zone.
- 4. He preferred a permit tied to his licence plate.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. There were no available lots near his home: He says he 'thought about renting a private lot nearby,' meaning one was available.
✅ 2. He found the monthly rate unreasonable: He says the private lot 'wanted $150 a month, which seemed excessive.'
❌ 3. The lot did not have spaces in his zone: Zone restrictions apply to street permits, not private lots.
❌ 4. He preferred a permit tied to his licence plate: The non-transferable permit detail is mentioned later and is not his reason for rejecting the private lot.

**Q2:** How much will the man pay for his parking permit?
- 1. $$20 per month until the year ends
- 2. $$180 for a full twelve-month period
- 3. $$90 for the rest of the current year
- 4. $$150 covering October through March
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. $20 per month until the year ends: This is the month-to-month rate, but he chose the prorated annual option.
❌ 2. $180 for a full twelve-month period: This is the full annual price, but it's mid-October so the woman prorated it.
✅ 3. $90 for the rest of the current year: The woman says 'we'd prorate it to $90 for the remaining six months.'
❌ 4. $150 covering October through March: $150 is the private lot's monthly price, not the permit cost.

**Q3:** What does the woman say about changing vehicles on a permit?
- 1. A new permit must be purchased at full price.
- 2. The update can be done at no additional cost.
- 3. Up to two vehicles can share one permit.
- 4. The permit can be transferred to anyone's vehicle.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A new permit must be purchased at full price: She specifically says 'there's no charge for that.'
✅ 2. The update can be done at no additional cost: She says 'you'll need to come back and update it, but there's no charge for that.'
❌ 3. Up to two vehicles can share one permit: Two is the maximum permits per household, not vehicles per permit.
❌ 4. The permit can be transferred to anyone's vehicle: She says permits are 'non-transferable' and tied to a specific licence plate.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the man's current situation?
- 1. He most likely has not yet set up utilities at his new home.
- 2. He probably moved to the city less than two months ago.
- 3. He will likely need to return to Hamilton to update his licence.
- 4. He has probably already exceeded the deadline for updating his licence.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He most likely has not yet set up utilities at his new home: He doesn't mention anything about utilities; the woman offers utility bill OR lease as proof.
✅ 2. He probably moved to the city less than two months ago: He says he's been there 'about two weeks,' and the woman notes he has 60 days to update his licence, implying he's well within the window.
❌ 3. He will likely need to return to Hamilton to update his licence: Licence updates are done with the province, not necessarily in Hamilton.
❌ 4. He has probably already exceeded the deadline for updating his licence: Two weeks is well under the 60-day limit.

**Q5:** What does the woman mainly advise the man to do regarding his identification?
- 1. Update his driver's licence before submitting the application.
- 2. Use his lease agreement to verify where he lives.
- 3. Provide a utility bill as his primary form of identification.
- 4. Bring a second piece of identification from his previous address.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Update his driver's licence before submitting the application: She says his old-address licence is acceptable as long as he has his lease.
✅ 2. Use his lease agreement to verify where he lives: She says 'as long as you bring your lease showing the Birchwood Drive address' it will be fine.
❌ 3. Provide a utility bill as his primary form of identification: A utility bill is mentioned as one option for proof of residency, but she specifically recommends the lease given his situation.
❌ 4. Bring a second piece of identification from his previous address: She does not suggest any document from his old address.

**Q6:** What will the man most likely do next?
- 1. Go home and update his driver's licence online.
- 2. Return the following day with his rental agreement.
- 3. Visit a private lot to compare parking options.
- 4. Complete his application before the office closes today.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Go home and update his driver's licence online: While he needs to update it within 60 days, his immediate plan is to return with documents.
✅ 2. Return the following day with his rental agreement: He says 'I'll be here first thing tomorrow' and plans to bring his lease.
❌ 3. Visit a private lot to compare parking options: He already rejected the private lot as too expensive.
❌ 4. Complete his application before the office closes today: He doesn't have his lease with him today, so he cannot apply now.

## Practice 557: FreshBox Meal Plans | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman about choosing a meal delivery plan.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why does the woman reject the Family Plan?
- 1. The weekly cost exceeds her budget.
- 2. The serving sizes are more than she needs.
- 3. The number of meals doesn't match her schedule.
- 4. The meal variety is too limited for one person.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The weekly cost exceeds her budget: She says the Family Plan sounds like 'great value,' so cost isn't the issue.
✅ 2. The serving sizes are more than she needs: She says 'it's just me, so the portions would be too much.'
❌ 3. The number of meals doesn't match her schedule: She doesn't mention five meals being too many; her concern is portion size.
❌ 4. The meal variety is too limited for one person: Meal variety is never discussed as a drawback of the Family Plan.

**Q2:** How much does each meal cost on the plan the woman selects?
- 1. Eight dollars and twenty-five cents.
- 2. Eleven dollars and seventy-five cents.
- 3. Twelve dollars per meal.
- 4. Ten dollars and fifty cents.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Eight dollars and twenty-five cents: That is the per-meal price for the Family Plan, not the Classic.
❌ 2. Eleven dollars and seventy-five cents: That is the per-meal price for the Lite Plan.
❌ 3. Twelve dollars per meal: Twelve refers to the number of weekly menu options, not a price.
✅ 4. Ten dollars and fifty cents: The Classic Plan costs 'ten fifty per meal,' and the woman chooses the Classic.

**Q3:** What can be inferred about why four meals per week suits the woman?
- 1. She already has plans to dine out one workday.
- 2. She prefers to cook for herself on weekends.
- 3. She wants to save money by eating less during the week.
- 4. She does not work a full five-day week.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. She already has plans to dine out one workday: She says she 'usually eats out on Fridays with colleagues,' so she only needs four weekday meals.
❌ 2. She prefers to cook for herself on weekends: Weekend cooking habits are not discussed.
❌ 3. She wants to save money by eating less during the week: Cost savings on fewer meals isn't her stated reasoning.
❌ 4. She does not work a full five-day week: She explicitly says she works 'Monday through Friday.'

**Q4:** By when must the woman make her meal selections each week?
- 1. By Thursday evening when the options are posted.
- 2. By Wednesday of the delivery week.
- 3. By the end of Saturday.
- 4. By Sunday before the first delivery arrives.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. By Thursday evening when the options are posted: Thursday evening is when the options become available, not the selection deadline.
❌ 2. By Wednesday of the delivery week: Wednesday is the deadline for skipping a week, not for choosing meals.
✅ 3. By the end of Saturday: The man says selections must be made 'until Saturday at midnight.'
❌ 4. By Sunday before the first delivery arrives: Sunday is a delivery day, not a selection deadline.

**Q5:** What does the man mainly recommend for individual customers?
- 1. Starting with the Lite Plan to keep costs manageable.
- 2. Trying the Family Plan for better per-meal savings.
- 3. Skipping weeks occasionally to stay flexible.
- 4. Choosing the Classic Plan for value and coverage.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Starting with the Lite Plan to keep costs manageable: He doesn't recommend the Lite Plan; it actually costs more per meal.
❌ 2. Trying the Family Plan for better per-meal savings: The Family Plan is for larger households, not single customers.
❌ 3. Skipping weeks occasionally to stay flexible: Skipping is mentioned as a feature, not as his main advice.
✅ 4. Choosing the Classic Plan for value and coverage: He says 'Most single customers go with the Classic since the cost per meal is lower, and four meals covers most of the workweek.'

**Q6:** What will the woman most likely do next?
- 1. Provide her contact and address information.
- 2. Browse the twelve meal options on the website.
- 3. Ask about the introductory discount details.
- 4. Call her friend to confirm the recommendation.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Provide her contact and address information: She says 'Let me grab my phone so I can give you the details' after the man asks for her address and email.
❌ 2. Browse the twelve meal options on the website: Meal selection happens later in the week, not during the sign-up.
❌ 3. Ask about the introductory discount details: The discount has already been explained and she acknowledged it.
❌ 4. Call her friend to confirm the recommendation: She has already decided to sign up and doesn't mention calling anyone.

## Practice 562: Weekend Ski Trip Planning | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two friends who are planning a ski trip.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What is the main purpose of this conversation?
- 1. To decide whether to cancel a weekend outing.
- 2. To compare different ski resorts in the area.
- 3. To work out the details for an upcoming trip.
- 4. To figure out how much money they need to budget.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To decide whether to cancel a weekend outing: Both speakers are enthusiastic and never discuss cancelling anything.
❌ 2. To compare different ski resorts in the area: While Blue Mountain and Moonstone are briefly compared, the broader discussion is about finalizing all logistics.
✅ 3. To work out the details for an upcoming trip: The entire conversation revolves around choosing a destination, departure time, equipment rentals, and food for their ski trip.
❌ 4. To figure out how much money they need to budget: Costs come up, but the conversation covers timing, transportation, food, and rentals — not just finances.

**Q2:** Why did the woman decide against Blue Mountain?
- 1. The snow conditions there were poor this weekend.
- 2. The drive would take significantly longer.
- 3. She had a bad experience there previously.
- 4. The other resort is nearer and more affordable.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The snow conditions there were poor this weekend: The woman says snow conditions look amazing but doesn't say Blue Mountain's are bad.
❌ 2. The drive would take significantly longer: She mentions Moonstone is closer, but doesn't say Blue Mountain's drive is significantly longer.
❌ 3. She had a bad experience there previously: She visited Moonstone with her sister, not Blue Mountain, and no negative experience is described.
✅ 4. The other resort is nearer and more affordable: She says Moonstone 'is closer' and lift tickets are fifty-five dollars versus close to ninety at Blue Mountain.

**Q3:** Why does the man need to adjust the proposed departure time?
- 1. He has to pick up rental equipment the night before.
- 2. He finds it difficult to wake up that early on weekends.
- 3. He wants to stop for breakfast on the way.
- 4. He needs extra time to prepare his own ski gear.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He has to pick up rental equipment the night before: He says he needs to stop by SportChek on Friday evening to rent skis, implying he will be out late and six AM would be difficult.
❌ 2. He finds it difficult to wake up that early on weekends: While he says 'six might be tough,' he specifically provides the Friday evening errand as the reason rather than a general dislike of early mornings.
❌ 3. He wants to stop for breakfast on the way: No mention of stopping for breakfast is made in the conversation.
❌ 4. He needs extra time to prepare his own ski gear: He sold his old skis last spring and no longer has his own gear to prepare.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the woman's opinion of the lodge cafeteria?
- 1. She thinks the food quality is excellent but too expensive.
- 2. She feels it does not offer good value for the money.
- 3. She believes it is a convenient place to have a full meal.
- 4. She considers it acceptable if they split the cost.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She thinks the food quality is excellent but too expensive: She says the chili was 'not worth it,' suggesting the quality didn't match the price, not that it was excellent.
✅ 2. She feels it does not offer good value for the money: She calls it 'overpriced' and says paying twelve dollars for chili was not worthwhile.
❌ 3. She believes it is a convenient place to have a full meal: She recommends packing sandwiches instead, which suggests she doesn't favour eating there.
❌ 4. She considers it acceptable if they split the cost: Splitting costs at the cafeteria is never discussed.

**Q5:** What will the woman most likely do after this conversation?
- 1. Book the lift tickets online for Saturday.
- 2. Drive to the rental shop to arrange a discount.
- 3. Contact her friend about a deal on ski equipment.
- 4. Check the weather forecast for the weekend.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Book the lift tickets online for Saturday: Buying tickets in advance is never mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 2. Drive to the rental shop to arrange a discount: Her friend Danielle works at the resort's rental shop; the woman plans to text her, not visit in person.
✅ 3. Contact her friend about a deal on ski equipment: She says 'I'll text her tonight' referring to Danielle, who could offer a discount on rentals.
❌ 4. Check the weather forecast for the weekend: The man already mentioned the weather, and no further checking is planned.

## Practice 563: Bowling Party Booking | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a bowling alley. The woman works at the bowling alley, and the man is a customer.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q1:** Why did the man visit the bowling alley?
- 1. To reserve lanes for a company function
- 2. To celebrate his daughter's birthday today
- 3. To ask about organizing a children's celebration
- 4. To compare pricing with other entertainment venues
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To reserve lanes for a company function: A corporate event is mentioned by the woman, but it belongs to another group, not the man.
❌ 2. To celebrate his daughter's birthday today: The party is planned for 'next Saturday,' not today.
✅ 3. To ask about organizing a children's celebration: The man says he was 'wondering if you do birthday party packages' for his daughter who is turning eight.
❌ 4. To compare pricing with other entertainment venues: The man does not mention comparing prices — he is focused on booking at this location.

**Q2:** What does the Deluxe package offer beyond the Standard option?
- 1. Additional lane time and footwear for each guest
- 2. Unlimited beverages, a cake, and festive decorations
- 3. A second bowling lane and shoe rentals included
- 4. Pizza slices, soft drinks, and party supplies
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Additional lane time and footwear for each guest: Two hours of bowling and shoe rentals are already part of the Standard package.
✅ 2. Unlimited beverages, a cake, and festive decorations: The woman says the Deluxe package 'adds unlimited soft drinks, a birthday cake, and party decorations.'
❌ 3. A second bowling lane and shoe rentals included: The second lane is arranged separately due to the group size, not as part of the Deluxe package.
❌ 4. Pizza slices, soft drinks, and party supplies: Pizza is already included in the Standard package, so it is not an added Deluxe feature.

### Audio Transcript
$4c

**Q3:** What is the main issue discussed in this section?
- 1. The man's desired time slot is already taken
- 2. The bowling alley cannot accommodate fifteen children
- 3. The required deposit is more than the man expected
- 4. The evening corporate event conflicts with the party
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. The man's desired time slot is already taken: The woman says 'both of our party lanes are already reserved at two o'clock next Saturday.'
❌ 2. The bowling alley cannot accommodate fifteen children: The woman solves this by offering two lanes; it is not the central problem.
❌ 3. The required deposit is more than the man expected: The man says the deposit 'seems reasonable,' so cost is not an issue.
❌ 4. The evening corporate event conflicts with the party: The corporate event starts at six and does not directly conflict with the discussed time slots.

**Q4:** How long will the woman hold the reservation for the man?
- 1. Until Saturday morning at eleven
- 2. Until the man's wife gives approval
- 3. Until the following morning
- 4. Until five o'clock the next day
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Until Saturday morning at eleven: Saturday is the party date, not the deadline for confirming.
❌ 2. Until the man's wife gives approval: The man wants to confirm with his wife, but the woman sets a specific time limit.
❌ 3. Until the following morning: The hold extends past the morning — until the evening of the next day.
✅ 4. Until five o'clock the next day: The woman says 'I'll hold it until tomorrow at five o'clock for you.'

**Q5:** How does the man initially feel about the morning time slot?
- 1. He is reluctant because it may inconvenience other families
- 2. He is excited because the venue will be less crowded
- 3. He is annoyed because his daughter dislikes mornings
- 4. He is unconcerned about what time the party takes place
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He is reluctant because it may inconvenience other families: He says 'some of the parents mentioned they have errands in the morning,' showing hesitation.
❌ 2. He is excited because the venue will be less crowded: He warms up to this idea later, but his initial reaction is hesitation, not excitement.
❌ 3. He is annoyed because his daughter dislikes mornings: His daughter's preference about morning times is never mentioned.
❌ 4. He is unconcerned about what time the party takes place: He clearly expresses a preference for the afternoon, saying 'that's a bit early.'

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q6:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She requires the man to pay the deposit in cash
- 2. She charges the man extra for the glow equipment
- 3. She includes a bonus feature because of the group size
- 4. She automatically upgrades the man to the Deluxe tier
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She requires the man to pay the deposit in cash: She says he can pay 'by cash or card,' giving him a choice.
❌ 2. She charges the man extra for the glow equipment: She waives the fee because the group exceeds twelve children.
✅ 3. She includes a bonus feature because of the group size: She says 'since your group is over twelve kids, I can add it at no charge.'
❌ 4. She automatically upgrades the man to the Deluxe tier: The man chose the Deluxe package himself in Section 1; the woman did not upgrade him.

**Q7:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He made the booking decision entirely on his own
- 2. He confirmed the plans with his spouse before returning
- 3. He chose to settle the deposit using cash
- 4. He specifically requested the glow-in-the-dark option
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He made the booking decision entirely on his own: He says 'I called my wife last night and she's completely on board,' showing he consulted her.
✅ 2. He confirmed the plans with his spouse before returning: He states his wife is 'completely on board with the morning time' before finalizing.
❌ 3. He chose to settle the deposit using cash: He says 'I'll pay by card, if that's okay.'
❌ 4. He specifically requested the glow-in-the-dark option: The woman brought up the glow-in-the-dark equipment; the man did not request it.

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will contact the parents about the new time
- 2. The woman will look for a third lane for the party
- 3. The man will ask about additional food options
- 4. The man will complete the deposit transaction
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will contact the parents about the new time: While he may do this eventually, the immediate next step is paying the deposit.
❌ 2. The woman will look for a third lane for the party: Two lanes have already been confirmed as sufficient for the group.
❌ 3. The man will ask about additional food options: All food and drink details are settled through the Deluxe package.
✅ 4. The man will complete the deposit transaction: The woman says she needs the deposit, and the man has already provided his phone number and chosen to pay by card.

## Practice 564: Pawsitive Place Packages | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation. A man is looking for a pet daycare service for his dog.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** How did the man learn about Pawsitive Place?
- 1. He found it through an online pet-care directory.
- 2. A colleague at work suggested it to him.
- 3. His veterinarian gave him a referral.
- 4. He saw the facility while walking his dog nearby.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He found it through an online pet-care directory: No online search is mentioned; he was personally recommended.
✅ 2. A colleague at work suggested it to him: He says 'A coworker recommended this place.'
❌ 3. His veterinarian gave him a referral: He visited the vet recently, but the vet did not refer him here.
❌ 4. He saw the facility while walking his dog nearby: He just moved to the neighbourhood but doesn't say he spotted it on a walk.

**Q2:** Why does the man decide against the Premium package?
- 1. The group sizes in the Premium package are too small for his dog.
- 2. He thinks the Premium weekly rate is beyond his budget.
- 3. He is not satisfied with the grooming service included.
- 4. His dog has already completed obedience training.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The group sizes in the Premium package are too small for his dog: Smaller groups are presented as a benefit, not a drawback he cites.
❌ 2. He thinks the Premium weekly rate is beyond his budget: He says it sounds appealing; cost is not his stated reason for declining.
❌ 3. He is not satisfied with the grooming service included: He never mentions any issue with grooming.
✅ 4. His dog has already completed obedience training: He says 'Biscuit has already finished obedience classes, so I don't think he needs extra training.'

**Q3:** When is Biscuit's trial day scheduled?
- 1. Thursday the eighteenth
- 2. Friday the nineteenth
- 3. Monday the twenty-second
- 4. The following Wednesday
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Thursday the eighteenth: The man initially requested Thursday, but it is fully booked.
❌ 2. Friday the nineteenth: Friday is available, but the man cannot come due to a vet appointment.
✅ 3. Monday the twenty-second: The man says 'Let's go with Monday the twenty-second,' and the woman confirms.
❌ 4. The following Wednesday: No Wednesday date is mentioned in the conversation.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the man's concern regarding pickup times?
- 1. He believes his work schedule allows him to arrive before the deadline.
- 2. He is worried about the late fee because he often works overtime.
- 3. He plans to have a family member pick up Biscuit most days.
- 4. He wants to negotiate a later pickup window with the daycare.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He believes his work schedule allows him to arrive before the deadline: He says 'I should be fine since I usually finish by five,' well before the 6:30 p.m. cutoff.
❌ 2. He is worried about the late fee because he often works overtime: He expresses confidence, not worry, about making it on time.
❌ 3. He plans to have a family member pick up Biscuit most days: No family member is mentioned in connection with pickup.
❌ 4. He wants to negotiate a later pickup window with the daycare: He accepts the stated hours without requesting any changes.

**Q5:** What does the woman strongly recommend for Biscuit?
- 1. Enrolling in additional one-on-one training sessions.
- 2. Taking advantage of a complimentary introductory visit.
- 3. Upgrading to the Premium package for smaller group sizes.
- 4. Scheduling a veterinary checkup before the start date.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Enrolling in additional one-on-one training sessions: Training is part of Premium, but she does not push it after the man declines.
✅ 2. Taking advantage of a complimentary introductory visit: She says 'We also offer a free trial day' and 'I'd strongly recommend it for dogs with separation anxiety.'
❌ 3. Upgrading to the Premium package for smaller group sizes: She mentions he can upgrade later but does not strongly recommend it.
❌ 4. Scheduling a veterinary checkup before the start date: She requires vaccination proof but does not recommend a checkup — his vet visit already happened.

**Q6:** What will the man most likely do after the conversation?
- 1. Register Biscuit for the monthly payment plan immediately.
- 2. Compare prices with other daycare facilities in the area.
- 3. Bring Biscuit for a trial visit and then commit to a monthly plan.
- 4. Schedule a vet visit to update Biscuit's vaccination records.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Register Biscuit for the monthly payment plan immediately: He says he will start with the trial first and sign up only 'if Biscuit settles in.'
❌ 2. Compare prices with other daycare facilities in the area: He expresses satisfaction and books a trial with no mention of looking elsewhere.
✅ 3. Bring Biscuit for a trial visit and then commit to a monthly plan: He says 'I'll start with the trial on Monday, and if Biscuit settles in, I'll sign up for the monthly plan right away.'
❌ 4. Schedule a vet visit to update Biscuit's vaccination records: His vaccinations are already current from a visit the previous week.

## Practice 570: Beginner Pottery Workshop | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation. A man is interested in enrolling in a pottery workshop.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** Why does the woman suggest the Beginner Foundations course for the man?
- 1. He has never tried any form of pottery before.
- 2. His previous experience is too limited for the higher level.
- 3. The intermediate course is already completely full.
- 4. He specifically asked for a course that includes glazing.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He has never tried any form of pottery before: He mentions attending a one-day class at a craft fair three years ago, so he has some experience.
✅ 2. His previous experience is too limited for the higher level: He only took a single one-day class, and the woman says the intermediate course 'assumes solid wheel experience.'
❌ 3. The intermediate course is already completely full: No information is given about the intermediate course being full.
❌ 4. He specifically asked for a course that includes glazing: He never requested glazing; the woman describes what the beginner course covers.

**Q2:** How many students are allowed in each workshop session?
- 1. Seven per session
- 2. Two per session
- 3. Six per session
- 4. Twelve per session
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Seven per session: Seven refers to the number of remaining spots in the Saturday class, not the total capacity.
❌ 2. Two per session: Two refers to the number of remaining spots in the Wednesday class.
❌ 3. Six per session: Six refers to the number of weeks each course runs, not the class size.
✅ 4. Twelve per session: The woman says 'Both are capped at twelve students so everyone gets hands-on time.'

**Q3:** What is the deadline to register for the Saturday class?
- 1. April 28th
- 2. May 3rd
- 3. May 7th
- 4. 48 hours before the session
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. April 28th: The woman says registration must be completed at least five days before the first class, 'so April 28th at the latest for Saturdays.'
❌ 2. May 3rd: This is the start date of the Saturday class itself, not the registration deadline.
❌ 3. May 7th: This is the start date of the Wednesday evening class.
❌ 4. 48 hours before the session: The 48-hour window applies to the cancellation policy, not the registration deadline.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the Wednesday evening session?
- 1. It is less popular than the Saturday morning session.
- 2. It is designed for students with more experience.
- 3. It is closer to being fully enrolled than the Saturday session.
- 4. It costs more because it runs during the evening.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It is less popular than the Saturday morning session: With only two spots left compared to seven on Saturday, Wednesday appears more popular, not less.
❌ 2. It is designed for students with more experience: Both sessions are for the same beginner course; the intermediate course is the one for experienced students.
✅ 3. It is closer to being fully enrolled than the Saturday session: The Wednesday class has only two spots remaining while Saturday still has seven, indicating Wednesday is nearly full.
❌ 4. It costs more because it runs during the evening: No price difference between sessions is mentioned; the fee difference is between course levels.

**Q5:** What is the woman's main practical advice about attending the Saturday class?
- 1. Get to the studio early because parking spaces are limited.
- 2. Register online at least a week before the course begins.
- 3. Bring personal pottery tools to supplement the studio's supplies.
- 4. Wear an apron to protect clothing from clay and glaze.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Get to the studio early because parking spaces are limited: She says the lot 'fills up fast on Saturdays' and suggests arriving by 9:45 for a 10:00 class.
❌ 2. Register online at least a week before the course begins: The deadline is five days, not a week, and online registration isn't specified.
❌ 3. Bring personal pottery tools to supplement the studio's supplies: She says 'We supply all tools and materials.'
❌ 4. Wear an apron to protect clothing from clay and glaze: She advises wearing clothes you don't mind getting messy and bringing an old towel, not an apron.

**Q6:** What will the man most likely do next?
- 1. Sign up for the Wednesday evening session instead.
- 2. Wait until closer to the deadline to decide.
- 3. Provide his contact details to complete registration.
- 4. Ask about the intermediate course schedule.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Sign up for the Wednesday evening session instead: He ruled out Wednesdays due to coaching his daughter's soccer team.
❌ 2. Wait until closer to the deadline to decide: He says he'd like to register 'today.'
✅ 3. Provide his contact details to complete registration: The woman asks for his name and email to finalize the registration he requested.
❌ 4. Ask about the intermediate course schedule: He has already committed to the beginner course and expressed no interest in intermediate details.

## Practice 571: Surprise Birthday Brunch | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two friends who are planning a surprise birthday brunch.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What are the man and woman mainly trying to organize?
- 1. A farewell dinner for a colleague at work
- 2. A surprise meal to celebrate a friend's birthday
- 3. A weekend get-together for their running group
- 4. A casual brunch to try a new restaurant
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A farewell dinner for a colleague at work: No one is leaving; the event is a birthday celebration.
✅ 2. A surprise meal to celebrate a friend's birthday: The entire conversation revolves around planning a surprise birthday brunch for Lisa.
❌ 3. A weekend get-together for their running group: The running club is only mentioned as a source of guests, not the purpose of the event.
❌ 4. A casual brunch to try a new restaurant: Although a new restaurant is chosen, the purpose is Lisa's birthday surprise, not simply trying a restaurant.

**Q2:** Why did they decide against Sunrise Café?
- 1. It was too far from Lisa's yoga studio.
- 2. Its menu was too expensive for the group.
- 3. It did not serve weekend brunch.
- 4. It lacked a separate space for their group.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It was too far from Lisa's yoga studio: Distance to yoga is never discussed as a factor in choosing the venue.
❌ 2. Its menu was too expensive for the group: The woman actually suggested Sunrise Café because it was easier on the budget.
❌ 3. It did not serve weekend brunch: No mention is made of Sunrise Café lacking a brunch menu.
✅ 4. It lacked a separate space for their group: The man says Sunrise Café doesn't have a separate space, and he argues a private room would make the surprise easier.

**Q3:** What flavour of cake does Lisa enjoy?
- 1. Lemon and raspberry
- 2. Vanilla and lemon
- 3. Strawberry and raspberry
- 4. Chocolate and raspberry
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Lemon and raspberry: The woman asks Marcus to get the lemon raspberry one, which Lisa called the best cake she'd ever had.
❌ 2. Vanilla and lemon: Vanilla is never mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 3. Strawberry and raspberry: Strawberry is not mentioned; only lemon and raspberry are.
❌ 4. Chocolate and raspberry: Chocolate is not mentioned; the requested cake is lemon raspberry.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the woman's role in this plan?
- 1. She is responsible for booking and paying for the venue.
- 2. She is mainly focused on ordering the cake in time.
- 3. She has taken the lead on inviting the guests.
- 4. She is leaving all the arrangements to Marcus.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She is responsible for booking and paying for the venue: The man is the one who called Maple and Vine and researched venues.
❌ 2. She is mainly focused on ordering the cake in time: Marcus volunteers to pick up and order the cake, not the woman.
✅ 3. She has taken the lead on inviting the guests: She says she has already started on the guest list and messaged people from the running club and Lisa's work.
❌ 4. She is leaving all the arrangements to Marcus: She is actively contributing — handling the guest list, decorations, and the plan to bring Lisa.

**Q5:** What will the woman most likely do on the morning of the brunch?
- 1. Arrive early to help Marcus set up the private room.
- 2. Pick up the cake from the bakery on the way.
- 3. Bring Lisa to the venue under a made-up reason.
- 4. Drive the guests from the running club to the restaurant.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Arrive early to help Marcus set up the private room: Marcus says he will get there early by ten to set things up; the woman plans to arrive at ten thirty with Lisa.
❌ 2. Pick up the cake from the bakery on the way: Marcus is picking up the cake on Friday evening, not the woman on Saturday morning.
✅ 3. Bring Lisa to the venue under a made-up reason: She plans to tell Lisa she needs help choosing a gift for her mom and then casually suggest stopping for brunch.
❌ 4. Drive the guests from the running club to the restaurant: No mention is made of transporting guests; she only messaged them.

## Practice 573: Weekend Car Rental | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a car rental office. The man works as a rental agent, and the woman is a customer.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q1:** What is the woman's main reason for visiting the rental office?
- 1. To compare vehicle prices for a business trip
- 2. To arrange a car for a weekend trip
- 3. To inquire about long-term rental options
- 4. To reserve a vehicle for her daily commute
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To compare vehicle prices for a business trip: The trip is personal — she is visiting her parents, not traveling for business.
✅ 2. To arrange a car for a weekend trip: The woman says she needs to 'rent a car for this weekend' and is 'driving up to Muskoka' to see her parents.
❌ 3. To inquire about long-term rental options: It is a three-day rental from Friday to Sunday, not a long-term arrangement.
❌ 4. To reserve a vehicle for her daily commute: The car is for a weekend trip to Muskoka, not for commuting.

**Q2:** What extra protection does the man recommend?
- 1. Basic liability coverage at no additional charge
- 2. Roadside assistance for fifteen dollars per day
- 3. A comprehensive insurance plan for the full trip
- 4. Collision coverage for twelve dollars per day
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Basic liability coverage at no additional charge: Basic liability is already included in the rental — it is not what the man recommends as an extra.
❌ 2. Roadside assistance for fifteen dollars per day: Roadside assistance is never mentioned; fifteen percent refers to a discount in a later part of the conversation.
❌ 3. A comprehensive insurance plan for the full trip: The man specifically mentions collision coverage, not a comprehensive plan.
✅ 4. Collision coverage for twelve dollars per day: The man says 'I'd recommend our collision coverage for an extra twelve dollars a day.'

### Audio Transcript
$4c

**Q3:** What is the main problem discussed in this part of the conversation?
- 1. The car the woman wanted has been taken by another customer
- 2. The woman's out-of-province license is not accepted for rentals
- 3. The rental system is experiencing a technical error
- 4. None of the remaining vehicles fit the woman's budget
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. The car the woman wanted has been taken by another customer: The man says someone reserved the Elantra online about ten minutes ago, so it is no longer available.
❌ 2. The woman's out-of-province license is not accepted for rentals: The man explicitly says 'it's not a problem for the rental itself' and that her Nova Scotia license is still valid.
❌ 3. The rental system is experiencing a technical error: There is no technical error; the system correctly shows a hold placed by another customer.
❌ 4. None of the remaining vehicles fit the woman's budget: The Cruze at sixty-eight dollars is only three dollars more per day, and the woman says it 'could work.'

**Q4:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She has been living in the province for over a year.
- 2. She reserved the Elantra through the company's website.
- 3. She moved to the area not long ago for employment.
- 4. She already switched her license to the local province.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She has been living in the province for over a year: She says she moved to Ontario 'about three months ago,' not over a year.
❌ 2. She reserved the Elantra through the company's website: She did not book online — she came in person, and someone else reserved it online.
✅ 3. She moved to the area not long ago for employment: She confirms she moved 'about three months ago for the job I mentioned.'
❌ 4. She already switched her license to the local province: She says she 'hasn't had a chance to update' her license yet.

**Q5:** How does the woman feel about losing the Elantra?
- 1. Unconcerned, since other cars are available at a similar price
- 2. Frustrated that she did not book in advance online
- 3. Grateful that the agent informed her quickly
- 4. Worried that the Cruze will cost significantly more
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Unconcerned, since other cars are available at a similar price: She describes the situation as 'frustrating' and says she is 'a little annoyed,' so she is not unconcerned.
✅ 2. Frustrated that she did not book in advance online: She says 'I'm a little annoyed' and 'I wish I'd booked online instead of coming in.'
❌ 3. Grateful that the agent informed her quickly: While the agent is helpful, her reaction is annoyance, not gratitude about the news.
❌ 4. Worried that the Cruze will cost significantly more: She notes it is 'only three dollars more per day,' so the cost difference is minor — not a significant concern.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q6:** What time does the rental office open on the pickup day?
- 1. Seven-thirty in the morning
- 2. Eight o'clock in the morning
- 3. Nine o'clock in the evening
- 4. Seven o'clock in the evening
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Seven-thirty in the morning: The man says 'We open at seven-thirty' when confirming the Friday morning pickup.
❌ 2. Eight o'clock in the morning: Eight is the time the woman wants to pick up the car, not the office opening time.
❌ 3. Nine o'clock in the evening: Nine is the Sunday closing time, not the opening time on Friday.
❌ 4. Seven o'clock in the evening: Seven is the approximate time the woman plans to return the car on Sunday.

**Q7:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He applied the weekend discount on his own authority.
- 2. He was unable to offer a rate lower than the Elantra's original price.
- 3. He advised the woman to return the car before seven on Sunday.
- 4. He obtained a reduced rate by getting his supervisor's approval.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He applied the weekend discount on his own authority: He says 'let me see if my manager has approved any weekend discounts,' showing he needed permission.
❌ 2. He was unable to offer a rate lower than the Elantra's original price: The discounted Cruze comes to about fifty-eight dollars per day, which is lower than the Elantra's sixty-five dollars.
❌ 3. He advised the woman to return the car before seven on Sunday: He confirms 'We're open until nine on Sundays' and accepts her plan to return around seven, but does not advise returning before that.
✅ 4. He obtained a reduced rate by getting his supervisor's approval: He says 'My manager approved a fifteen percent weekend discount on the Cruze.'

**Q8:** What will the woman most likely do next?
- 1. Head to Muskoka right away with her friend
- 2. Go to a licensing office to update her documents
- 3. Come back on Friday morning to collect the car
- 4. Contact her parents to let them know about the delay
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Head to Muskoka right away with her friend: The trip is on the weekend; she is picking up the car on Friday, not immediately.
❌ 2. Go to a licensing office to update her documents: Although her license needs updating, nothing suggests she will do this before the trip.
✅ 3. Come back on Friday morning to collect the car: The man tells her to 'bring it along with your license on Friday morning, and we'll have the Cruze ready and waiting,' confirming she will return Friday.
❌ 4. Contact her parents to let them know about the delay: There is no delay — everything is settled, and the trip is proceeding as planned.

## Practice 575: School Carpool Adjustment | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two neighbours who are coordinating a carpool schedule for their children.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What are the neighbours mainly trying to work out?
- 1. A plan to enroll their children in new activities
- 2. A schedule for their children's extracurricular lessons
- 3. A temporary arrangement for driving their children to school
- 4. A long-term solution for commuting to work together
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. A plan to enroll their children in new activities: Activities like piano and soccer are mentioned, but they are not enrolling in anything new.
❌ 2. A schedule for their children's extracurricular lessons: Extracurriculars come up briefly, but the main discussion is about school transportation.
✅ 3. A temporary arrangement for driving their children to school: The woman's husband is away for three weeks and she needs help splitting the morning drop-offs.
❌ 4. A long-term solution for commuting to work together: They are coordinating rides for their kids, not for themselves, and the arrangement is temporary.

**Q2:** Why is the woman unable to handle the morning drop-offs by herself?
- 1. Her spouse is temporarily away for a work assignment
- 2. Her clinic requires her to start work earlier than usual
- 3. Her vehicle needs repairs for the next few weeks
- 4. Her husband recently changed his work schedule
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Her spouse is temporarily away for a work assignment: She says Kevin has been sent to Calgary for a work project and will be gone for three weeks.
❌ 2. Her clinic requires her to start work earlier than usual: She mentions the clinic closes at two thirty, but there is no mention of earlier morning hours.
❌ 3. Her vehicle needs repairs for the next few weeks: No car trouble is mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. Her husband recently changed his work schedule: Kevin didn't change his schedule — he was sent away on a project.

**Q3:** On which days will the man drive both children to school?
- 1. Tuesday and Thursday
- 2. Monday, Wednesday, and Friday
- 3. Every weekday morning
- 4. The first and last weekdays of the school week
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Tuesday and Thursday: The woman covers Tuesday, Wednesday, and Thursday mornings, not the man.
❌ 2. Monday, Wednesday, and Friday: The man only handles Monday and Friday; Wednesday mornings are the woman's responsibility.
❌ 3. Every weekday morning: The man only covers two days; the woman does the remaining three.
✅ 4. The first and last weekdays of the school week: He works from home on Mondays and Fridays, so he offers to do the drop-offs on those two days.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about how the woman felt before speaking with David?
- 1. She was annoyed that Kevin left without making arrangements
- 2. She was stressed about managing the driving on her own
- 3. She was confident that David would offer to help
- 4. She was planning to reduce her hours at the clinic
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She was annoyed that Kevin left without making arrangements: She doesn't express frustration with Kevin; she simply explains the situation.
✅ 2. She was stressed about managing the driving on her own: She says she was 'honestly considering hiring someone,' suggesting she was worried about coping alone.
❌ 3. She was confident that David would offer to help: Her relief and gratitude suggest she was uncertain, not confident, about getting help.
❌ 4. She was planning to reduce her hours at the clinic: She mentions the clinic closes at two thirty but never discusses changing her work hours.

**Q5:** What will the woman most likely do before the new arrangement begins?
- 1. Visit Kevin in Calgary to update him on the plan
- 2. Arrange for Rachel to help with Tuesday pickups
- 3. Send David a message to verify the details
- 4. Meet with the school to adjust drop-off times
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Visit Kevin in Calgary to update him on the plan: There is no mention of visiting Kevin; David simply says to tell him not to worry.
❌ 2. Arrange for Rachel to help with Tuesday pickups: Rachel handles Ethan on Wednesdays; Tuesday pickups are the woman's responsibility due to piano.
✅ 3. Send David a message to verify the details: The woman says 'I'll text you Sunday night to confirm everything,' which is the night before they start.
❌ 4. Meet with the school to adjust drop-off times: No school meeting or schedule change is discussed.
