## Practice 576: FitZone Membership Tiers | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a gym staff member. The man is asking about membership options.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What does the Premium plan offer that is not part of the Basic plan?
- 1. Round-the-clock facility access and quarterly fitness evaluations
- 2. Two guided workout sessions each month with a certified trainer
- 3. Extended operating hours from 6 a.m. to midnight
- 4. Unlimited group exercise classes and use of the relaxation facilities
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Round-the-clock facility access and quarterly fitness evaluations: These are Elite tier features, not Premium.
❌ 2. Two guided workout sessions each month with a certified trainer: Personal training sessions are included in Elite, not Premium.
❌ 3. Extended operating hours from 6 a.m. to midnight: Regular hours (6 a.m. to 10 p.m.) apply to both Basic and Premium; only Elite has 24-hour access.
✅ 4. Unlimited group exercise classes and use of the relaxation facilities: The woman says Premium 'adds unlimited group fitness classes and access to the sauna and steam room.'

**Q2:** Why does the man decide against the Premium membership?
- 1. He needs access to personal training for his injury.
- 2. He needs to use the gym outside of standard operating hours.
- 3. He cannot afford the monthly cost of the Premium tier.
- 4. He is not interested in group fitness classes anymore.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He needs access to personal training for his injury: While the training sessions are mentioned as a 'bonus,' his primary reason is schedule flexibility.
✅ 2. He needs to use the gym outside of standard operating hours: He says '24-hour access really appeals to me' because he works shifts at the hospital and his 'schedule is all over the place.'
❌ 3. He cannot afford the monthly cost of the Premium tier: Cost is never mentioned as a concern.
❌ 4. He is not interested in group fitness classes anymore: He says Premium is 'tempting' because of group classes, so he still likes them.

**Q3:** How much will the man pay as a registration fee?
- 1. He will not have to pay anything to register.
- 2. He will pay a discounted fee of $40.
- 3. He will pay the standard fee of $75.
- 4. He will pay an additional $15 on top of his monthly rate.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He will not have to pay anything to register: The promotion waives the sign-up fee entirely for Premium or Elite, and he chose Elite.
❌ 2. He will pay a discounted fee of $40: The $40 reduced fee applies only to Basic memberships during the promotion.
❌ 3. He will pay the standard fee of $75: The standard fee is waived because of the current promotion for his chosen tier.
❌ 4. He will pay an additional $15 on top of his monthly rate: The $15 extra is for month-to-month plans, which the man declined by committing to twelve months.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the man's current health?
- 1. He has recently recovered from a major surgery.
- 2. He has not exercised regularly for a long time.
- 3. He is experiencing a physical problem that requires attention.
- 4. He is in excellent shape but wants to maintain his fitness.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He has recently recovered from a major surgery: Surgery is never mentioned; he only refers to a shoulder issue.
❌ 2. He has not exercised regularly for a long time: He mentions attending group classes at his old gym, suggesting recent activity.
✅ 3. He is experiencing a physical problem that requires attention: He says he's 'been dealing with a shoulder issue' and 'could really use some guidance,' implying an ongoing concern.
❌ 4. He is in excellent shape but wants to maintain his fitness: His shoulder issue suggests he is not in fully excellent condition.

**Q5:** What is the woman's main recommendation for the man?
- 1. She suggests he freeze his membership if his schedule becomes too busy.
- 2. She advises him to work with a specific trainer who focuses on injury rehabilitation.
- 3. She recommends the month-to-month option in case he moves again.
- 4. She suggests he start with Premium and upgrade to Elite later.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She suggests he freeze his membership if his schedule becomes too busy: Freezing is mentioned as a general option, not as her main recommendation.
✅ 2. She advises him to work with a specific trainer who focuses on injury rehabilitation: She says 'I'd recommend booking your first session with Marco' who 'specializes in injury recovery.'
❌ 3. She recommends the month-to-month option in case he moves again: She mentions it as an option but does not recommend it; the man chose twelve months.
❌ 4. She suggests he start with Premium and upgrade to Elite later: She never suggests starting with a lower tier.

**Q6:** What will the man most likely do next?
- 1. Schedule his first training appointment with Marco.
- 2. Go home to get the documents he needs.
- 3. Compare pricing with other fitness centres nearby.
- 4. Return with his identification and payment method to register.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Schedule his first training appointment with Marco: This may happen later, but his immediate next step is completing the sign-up.
❌ 2. Go home to get the documents he needs: He says his wallet is in the car, not at home.
❌ 3. Compare pricing with other fitness centres nearby: He has already decided to sign up and says 'Let me grab my wallet from the car.'
✅ 4. Return with his identification and payment method to register: He says 'Let me grab my wallet from the car. I'll be right back,' and the woman asked for government ID and a credit card or bank account.

You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a post office. The woman is a postal clerk, and the man is a customer.

**Q1:** What is the conversation in Section 1 mainly about?
- 1. Comparing different postal carriers for a shipment
- 2. Arranging timely delivery of a present
- 3. Requesting a reduced rate on postal fees
- 4. Selecting appropriate packaging for a fragile item
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Comparing different postal carriers for a shipment: Only Canada Post is discussed; no other carriers are compared.
✅ 2. Arranging timely delivery of a present: The man needs to send a birthday gift to his mother and wants it to arrive by Saturday, so the conversation focuses on choosing a shipping speed.
❌ 3. Requesting a reduced rate on postal fees: The man comments the price is higher than expected but never asks for a discount.
❌ 4. Selecting appropriate packaging for a fragile item: Packaging is discussed in Section 2, not Section 1.

**Q2:** How much does the man's package weigh?
- 1. Slightly less than two kilograms
- 2. Precisely two kilograms
- 3. A little more than two kilograms
- 4. Approximately two and a half kilograms
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Slightly less than two kilograms: The woman says 'it comes in at just under two kilograms.'
❌ 2. Precisely two kilograms: The woman says 'just under,' not exactly two kilograms.
❌ 3. A little more than two kilograms: The package is under, not over, two kilograms.
❌ 4. Approximately two and a half kilograms: This weight is never mentioned in the conversation.

### Audio Transcript
$4c

**Q3:** How does the man react when the woman suggests better protection for the item?
- 1. Irritated that he needs to spend additional money
- 2. Confident that his original wrapping is adequate
- 3. Worried and eager to take extra precautions
- 4. Unconcerned because the gift has little value
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Irritated that he needs to spend additional money: He doesn't express irritation; he says 'I'd better not take any chances' and voluntarily adds insurance too.
❌ 2. Confident that his original wrapping is adequate: He initially asks 'Do you think that's enough?' showing uncertainty, then agrees to repack.
✅ 3. Worried and eager to take extra precautions: He says 'I'd better not take any chances' and also requests insurance, showing concern and willingness to protect the gift.
❌ 4. Unconcerned because the gift has little value: He paid a hundred and fifty dollars and calls it 'kind of a big deal,' showing he cares greatly.

**Q4:** How much did the man spend on the gift?
- 1. One hundred dollars
- 2. Two hundred dollars
- 3. Three dollars and ninety-five cents
- 4. One hundred and fifty dollars
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. One hundred dollars: This amount is not mentioned as the price of the vase.
❌ 2. Two hundred dollars: Two hundred dollars is the insurance threshold, not the cost of the vase.
❌ 3. Three dollars and ninety-five cents: This is the price of the bubble wrap, not the vase.
✅ 4. One hundred and fifty dollars: The man says 'I paid a hundred and fifty for it.'

**Q5:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He visits antique shops on a regular basis
- 2. He chose the gift to suit his mother's collecting interest
- 3. He initially protected the item with bubble wrap
- 4. He decided the insurance was not worth the cost
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He visits antique shops on a regular basis: He mentions finding the vase at an antique shop, but there is no indication he goes regularly.
✅ 2. He chose the gift to suit his mother's collecting interest: He says 'My mother collects them, so I thought it would be the perfect gift.'
❌ 3. He initially protected the item with bubble wrap: He wrapped it in newspaper and packing peanuts; the woman suggested bubble wrap.
❌ 4. He decided the insurance was not worth the cost: He says 'let's definitely add the insurance,' choosing to purchase it.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q6:** What makes the end of the conversation remarkable?
- 1. The man and his mother unknowingly mailed each other gifts at the same time
- 2. The package from Halifax was delivered faster than the standard timeframe
- 3. The clerk recognized the sender's address on the unclaimed parcel
- 4. The man had forgotten about his mother's birthday until visiting the post office
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. The man and his mother unknowingly mailed each other gifts at the same time: The man says 'We're sending each other gifts at the same time and didn't even know it.'
❌ 2. The package from Halifax was delivered faster than the standard timeframe: No mention is made of how quickly the Halifax package arrived relative to expectations.
❌ 3. The clerk recognized the sender's address on the unclaimed parcel: The woman simply notes it is from Halifax; she does not recognize any name or address.
❌ 4. The man had forgotten about his mother's birthday until visiting the post office: He came specifically to ship a birthday gift, so he already knew about the birthday.

**Q7:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She repacked the vase on the man's behalf
- 2. She waived the insurance fee as a gesture of goodwill
- 3. She warned the man that unclaimed parcels are kept for a limited period
- 4. She advised the man to contact his mother before shipping
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She repacked the vase on the man's behalf: She directed the man to repack it himself at the counter by the window.
❌ 2. She waived the insurance fee as a gesture of goodwill: Insurance was charged at four seventy-five and included in the total of thirty-five twenty.
✅ 3. She warned the man that unclaimed parcels are kept for a limited period: She says 'we only hold packages for fifteen days, so it's good you came in today.'
❌ 4. She advised the man to contact his mother before shipping: She never suggests he call or contact his mother.

**Q8:** What will the man most likely do next?
- 1. Return to the counter to mail another item
- 2. Phone his mother to tell her about the surprise
- 3. Ask the clerk to hold his parcel for a few more days
- 4. Leave the post office with his receipt and his mother's package
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Return to the counter to mail another item: The man has finished his business and thanks the woman; nothing suggests another shipment.
❌ 2. Phone his mother to tell her about the surprise: He seems delighted by the coincidence but does not indicate he will call; doing so could spoil her surprise as well.
❌ 3. Ask the clerk to hold his parcel for a few more days: He has already picked up the package and there is no reason to leave it.
✅ 4. Leave the post office with his receipt and his mother's package: He has paid, shipped his vase, and collected the package from Halifax. The woman says 'Have a wonderful day,' and he thanks her, indicating he is about to leave.

## Practice 581: Pearson Airport Shuttle | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation where a man calls to arrange an airport shuttle service.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What does the woman recommend the man do regarding shuttle options?
- 1. Take the shared shuttle to save money on the trip.
- 2. Choose the direct ride for a more comfortable experience.
- 3. Arrive four hours early to avoid any delays.
- 4. Book a seat on the shared route during off-peak hours.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Take the shared shuttle to save money on the trip: She actually steers him away from the shared shuttle, noting up to six stops and long wait times.
✅ 2. Choose the direct ride for a more comfortable experience: She says 'The private option would be much more relaxed' after explaining the shared route's drawbacks.
❌ 3. Arrive four hours early to avoid any delays: Four hours early is required for the shared shuttle, not her recommendation.
❌ 4. Book a seat on the shared route during off-peak hours: Off-peak hours are never discussed; she mentions midday stops as a problem.

**Q2:** Which terminal will the man depart from?
- 1. Terminal 1
- 2. Terminal 2
- 3. Terminal 5
- 4. Terminal 3
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Terminal 1: This is incorrect; the woman specifies a different terminal for Maple Air.
❌ 2. Terminal 2: This terminal is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 3. Terminal 5: This terminal is not mentioned in the conversation.
✅ 4. Terminal 3: The woman states 'Maple Air operates out of Terminal 3.'

**Q3:** Why does the man decide against the shared shuttle?
- 1. He needs to concentrate on work during the ride.
- 2. He is worried about arriving late for his flight.
- 3. He does not want to share luggage space with other passengers.
- 4. He prefers not to be picked up so early in the morning.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He needs to concentrate on work during the ride: He says he has 'an important business presentation to review' and would 'rather not be distracted.'
❌ 2. He is worried about arriving late for his flight: Arriving late is not his concern; the shared shuttle would get him there with over two hours to spare.
❌ 3. He does not want to share luggage space with other passengers: Luggage space on the shared shuttle is never discussed.
❌ 4. He prefers not to be picked up so early in the morning: While the early pickup bothered him, his stated reason for choosing private was needing to work undisturbed.

**Q4:** How much will the man be charged for the shuttle service?
- 1. Eighty-five dollars at the standard rate.
- 2. Thirty-eight dollars for a single passenger.
- 3. Seventy-six dollars and fifty cents with a discount.
- 4. Seventy-six dollars and fifty cents plus a zone surcharge.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Eighty-five dollars at the standard rate: $85 is the regular private shuttle price before the discount is applied.
❌ 2. Thirty-eight dollars for a single passenger: $38 is the shared shuttle price, which he did not choose.
✅ 3. Seventy-six dollars and fifty cents with a discount: The woman says the 10% booking discount 'brings your total to $76.50.'
❌ 4. Seventy-six dollars and fifty cents plus a zone surcharge: His address is within the regular service zone, so no surcharge applies.

**Q5:** What can be inferred about the man's reason for traveling?
- 1. He is going on vacation to a warmer destination.
- 2. He is visiting family members who live far away.
- 3. He is relocating to a new city for a position.
- 4. He is most likely going on a work-related trip.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He is going on vacation to a warmer destination: There is no mention of vacation or leisure travel.
❌ 2. He is visiting family members who live far away: Family visits are never discussed in the conversation.
❌ 3. He is relocating to a new city for a position: He has only one suitcase and a laptop bag, and his concern about reviewing a presentation suggests a short trip, not a move.
✅ 4. He is most likely going on a work-related trip: He mentions having 'an important business presentation to review,' strongly suggesting a work trip.

**Q6:** What will most likely happen after this phone call?
- 1. The man will get an email confirming his booking details.
- 2. The driver will send a text message to the man.
- 3. The man will transfer the payment electronically.
- 4. The woman will arrange a pickup time with the driver.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. The man will get an email confirming his booking details: The woman says 'You'll receive a confirmation email within the hour with your driver's name and contact number.'
❌ 2. The driver will send a text message to the man: The driver texts 30 minutes before the pickup day, not immediately after the call.
❌ 3. The man will transfer the payment electronically: He chose to pay by credit card, not e-transfer.
❌ 4. The woman will arrange a pickup time with the driver: The pickup time has already been established as 11:15 a.m.; the next step is the confirmation email.

## Practice 582: Weekend Canoe Gear | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two friends who are planning a weekend canoe trip.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What are the speakers mainly doing in this conversation?
- 1. Deciding on a destination for their weekend plans
- 2. Coordinating logistics for an upcoming outdoor excursion
- 3. Shopping for camping equipment they need
- 4. Reviewing what went wrong on a previous trip
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Deciding on a destination for their weekend plans: The destination (Algonquin) is already decided; they're sorting out details like gear and food.
✅ 2. Coordinating logistics for an upcoming outdoor excursion: The entire conversation is about dividing responsibilities for food, gear, timing, and transportation for a canoe trip.
❌ 3. Shopping for camping equipment they need: The woman mentions a sleeping bag at a store, but they ultimately borrow gear rather than shop.
❌ 4. Reviewing what went wrong on a previous trip: A past trip is briefly mentioned, but the focus is on planning the upcoming one.

**Q2:** Why does the woman decide against purchasing a new sleeping bag?
- 1. She found a cheaper alternative at a different shop.
- 2. The store was sold out of the model she wanted.
- 3. She already owns one rated for cold enough temperatures.
- 4. She considers it too expensive for just one outing.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She found a cheaper alternative at a different shop: No other shop or cheaper option is mentioned.
❌ 2. The store was sold out of the model she wanted: Stock availability is never discussed.
❌ 3. She already owns one rated for cold enough temperatures: Her own bag is only rated to ten degrees, which is not warm enough for three-degree weather.
✅ 4. She considers it too expensive for just one outing: She says the bag was two hundred and forty dollars and adds 'No point spending that for one trip.'

**Q3:** Where will the man collect the woman on Friday?
- 1. On Rideau Street
- 2. At her office after her meeting ends
- 3. At the deli on Elgin Street
- 4. At the entrance to the park
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. On Rideau Street: The man says 'I'll pick you up on Rideau Street.'
❌ 2. At her office after her meeting ends: She mentions a meeting, but the pickup location is Rideau Street, not her workplace.
❌ 3. At the deli on Elgin Street: Elgin Street is where the man plans to buy lunch supplies, not the pickup point.
❌ 4. At the entrance to the park: Algonquin is their destination, not where he picks her up.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about their previous canoe trip?
- 1. The weather conditions were worse than predicted.
- 2. They had trouble finding a canoe to rent.
- 3. They underestimated how much food they needed.
- 4. They forgot to bring essential safety equipment.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The weather conditions were worse than predicted: Cold weather is discussed only for the upcoming trip, not a past one.
❌ 2. They had trouble finding a canoe to rent: Rental difficulties are not mentioned for any previous trip.
✅ 3. They underestimated how much food they needed: The man says 'Last time we ran out on the second day,' and the woman responds with a proper meal plan this time.
❌ 4. They forgot to bring essential safety equipment: No mention is made of missing safety gear on a previous trip.

**Q5:** What will most likely happen on Thursday evening?
- 1. The speakers will meet to finalize the route through the park.
- 2. The woman will deliver the paddles to the man's home.
- 3. The man will pick up supplies from the deli.
- 4. The man will receive the canoe and send a confirmation message.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The speakers will meet to finalize the route through the park: No meeting to plan a route is mentioned for Thursday.
❌ 2. The woman will deliver the paddles to the man's home: The woman is bringing the paddles on the trip, not dropping them off beforehand.
❌ 3. The man will pick up supplies from the deli: He mentions buying deli items but doesn't specify doing so on Thursday.
✅ 4. The man will receive the canoe and send a confirmation message: Greg is dropping off the canoe Thursday evening, and the man says 'I'll text you Thursday night to confirm everything.'

## Practice 584: Swimming Lesson Registration | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a woman and a man at a community swimming pool. The woman is a new customer; the man works as a pool attendant at the front desk.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q1:** Why has the woman visited the facility?
- 1. To sign up for a competitive adult swim program
- 2. To ask about swimming instruction for her child
- 3. To purchase a family membership at the pool
- 4. To find out about weekend public swim hours
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To sign up for a competitive adult swim program: While the woman mentions she swam competitively in university, her main reason for visiting is her daughter's lessons.
✅ 2. To ask about swimming instruction for her child: The woman says she was 'wondering if you could tell me about swimming lessons for kids.'
❌ 3. To purchase a family membership at the pool: No family membership is discussed — she asks about lessons and a lap swim pass separately.
❌ 4. To find out about weekend public swim hours: Public free swim is discussed later in Section 2, not as her reason for visiting.

**Q2:** What does the children's class fee include?
- 1. Eight weeks of lessons plus a pool membership
- 2. A kickboard, goggles, and a swimming costume
- 3. Registration, a kickboard, and free swim access
- 4. The complete course along with all necessary gear
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Eight weeks of lessons plus a pool membership: The fee covers eight weeks of lessons and equipment, but no pool membership is mentioned.
❌ 2. A kickboard, goggles, and a swimming costume: Goggles and a kickboard are mentioned, but a swimming costume is never included.
❌ 3. Registration, a kickboard, and free swim access: Free swim requires no registration and is separate from the class fee.
✅ 4. The complete course along with all necessary gear: The man says 'a hundred and twenty dollars for the full eight-week session. That includes all the equipment — goggles, a kickboard, everything she'll need.'

### Audio Transcript
$4c

**Q3:** What is this part of the conversation mainly about?
- 1. A conflict between the desired class time and its availability
- 2. The benefits of registering for the Tuesday evening session
- 3. Comparing the costs of different swimming programs
- 4. Finding a private instructor for the woman's daughter
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. A conflict between the desired class time and its availability: The Saturday class is full, and the woman cannot attend the Tuesday option, creating a scheduling problem.
❌ 2. The benefits of registering for the Tuesday evening session: The Tuesday class is mentioned, but the woman rules it out because of piano lessons.
❌ 3. Comparing the costs of different swimming programs: Pricing is not discussed in this section.
❌ 4. Finding a private instructor for the woman's daughter: Private instruction is never mentioned in the conversation.

**Q4:** How does the woman feel about being placed on the waitlist?
- 1. She is optimistic a spot will become available very soon.
- 2. She accepts it grudgingly as the only available choice.
- 3. She is annoyed and demands to speak with a supervisor.
- 4. She is unconcerned since free swim is offered on weekends.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She is optimistic a spot will become available very soon: It is the man who expresses optimism, saying 'your chances are pretty good.' The woman is less certain.
✅ 2. She accepts it grudgingly as the only available choice: She says 'I suppose that's better than nothing,' indicating reluctant acceptance.
❌ 3. She is annoyed and demands to speak with a supervisor: She never asks for a supervisor — she is frustrated but polite.
❌ 4. She is unconcerned since free swim is offered on weekends: She is clearly concerned about lessons; free swim provides only partial relief.

**Q5:** What can be inferred about the woman?
- 1. She prefers evening activities for her daughter over morning ones.
- 2. She has a flexible daytime schedule during the work week.
- 3. Her weekday commitments limit her daughter's class options.
- 4. She intends to teach her daughter to swim by herself.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She prefers evening activities for her daughter over morning ones: She specifically wants the Saturday morning class; Tuesday evening does not work.
❌ 2. She has a flexible daytime schedule during the work week: She says 'I work Monday through Friday,' indicating an inflexible weekday schedule.
✅ 3. Her weekday commitments limit her daughter's class options: She works weekdays, and her daughter has piano on Tuesday evenings, leaving only Saturday available.
❌ 4. She intends to teach her daughter to swim by herself: She is enrolling her daughter in formal lessons and never mentions teaching her personally.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q6:** What is the discounted cost of the daughter's swimming lessons?
- 1. A hundred and two dollars
- 2. Thirty-eight dollars
- 3. A hundred and twenty dollars
- 4. Forty-five dollars
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. A hundred and two dollars: The man says 'instead of a hundred and twenty, the lessons come to a hundred and two dollars.'
❌ 2. Thirty-eight dollars: Thirty-eight dollars is the reduced price of the woman's monthly lap swim pass, not the lessons.
❌ 3. A hundred and twenty dollars: This is the original price before the community discount is applied.
❌ 4. Forty-five dollars: Forty-five dollars is the original price of the adult monthly lap swim pass.

**Q7:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He created the neighbourhood discount policy himself.
- 2. He notified the waitlisted families before registering the girl.
- 3. He took the initiative to help the woman reduce her costs.
- 4. He asked for documentation of the woman's home address.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He created the neighbourhood discount policy himself: He simply informs the woman about an existing community discount; he did not create it.
❌ 2. He notified the waitlisted families before registering the girl: He registered the daughter immediately 'before the waitlist notifications even go out.'
✅ 3. He took the initiative to help the woman reduce her costs: He proactively asked if she lived in the Riverside district and applied the fifteen percent discount to both registrations.
❌ 4. He asked for documentation of the woman's home address: He accepted her stated address without requesting any proof.

**Q8:** What will the woman and her daughter most likely do next?
- 1. Collect swimming equipment from the front desk.
- 2. Join the public free swim that same afternoon.
- 3. Wait at home for a call confirming the registration.
- 4. Arrive ahead of time on Saturday for an orientation.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Collect swimming equipment from the front desk: Equipment is included in the class fee, but picking it up at the desk is not mentioned.
❌ 2. Join the public free swim that same afternoon: Free swim was discussed as a backup; the daughter is now registered for the Saturday class.
❌ 3. Wait at home for a call confirming the registration: The registration was completed on the spot — no follow-up call is needed.
✅ 4. Arrive ahead of time on Saturday for an orientation: The man tells her to 'arrive fifteen minutes early on the first day for orientation.'

## Practice 588: Buddy Gym Membership | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between two colleagues. The conversation takes place at the office.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** What is the main purpose of this conversation?
- 1. The man is recommending his favourite fitness centre to the woman.
- 2. The colleagues are comparing different gyms they have visited.
- 3. The colleagues are deciding whether to register for a gym deal together.
- 4. The woman is asking for advice about switching fitness facilities.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man is recommending his favourite fitness centre to the woman: FitZone is not a gym he currently belongs to; he's proposing they join together.
❌ 2. The colleagues are comparing different gyms they have visited: While they mention other gyms briefly, the conversation centres on signing up for the buddy promotion.
✅ 3. The colleagues are deciding whether to register for a gym deal together: The man proposes the FitZone buddy membership, they discuss the details, and agree to sign up together.
❌ 4. The woman is asking for advice about switching fitness facilities: The man initiates the conversation, and they're making a joint decision rather than her seeking advice.

**Q2:** What is one reason the woman is unhappy with her current gym?
- 1. The machines there are not up to date.
- 2. The monthly fee is too expensive.
- 3. It does not have a swimming pool.
- 4. It is too far from her workplace.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. The machines there are not up to date: She says 'The equipment is outdated,' which means the machines are not modern.
❌ 2. The monthly fee is too expensive: She pays sixty-five dollars, but she doesn't specifically complain about the cost being too high at PureFit.
❌ 3. It does not have a swimming pool: A pool is mentioned only for FitZone; she doesn't say PureFit lacks one.
❌ 4. It is too far from her workplace: Distance to PureFit is never mentioned in the conversation.

**Q3:** What condition applies to the discounted membership rate?
- 1. Both members must attend group classes each week.
- 2. Both members must register before November tenth.
- 3. Both members must keep their membership active for a full year.
- 4. Both members must sign up for the towel service add-on.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Both members must attend group classes each week: Attending classes is not a requirement of the buddy plan.
❌ 2. Both members must register before November tenth: That's the promotion deadline, not a condition of maintaining the rate once enrolled.
✅ 3. Both members must keep their membership active for a full year: The man says there's a twelve-month commitment and if either cancels early, the other person's rate returns to full price.
❌ 4. Both members must sign up for the towel service add-on: Towel service is optional and separate from the buddy rate conditions.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the man's recent exercise habits?
- 1. He has been exercising regularly at home instead.
- 2. He likely has not been working out at a gym recently.
- 3. He has been attending group fitness classes elsewhere.
- 4. He prefers swimming over other forms of exercise.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He has been exercising regularly at home instead: There is no mention of any home workout routine.
✅ 2. He likely has not been working out at a gym recently: He says he cancelled his previous membership six months ago because he wasn't going enough, and he wants to 'get back into a routine.'
❌ 3. He has been attending group fitness classes elsewhere: He has no current gym membership and mentions wanting to restart exercising.
❌ 4. He prefers swimming over other forms of exercise: The pool is something the woman is excited about; no swimming preference is expressed by the man.

**Q5:** What will the man most likely do before Wednesday?
- 1. Reserve a spot for a group fitness class.
- 2. Cancel his membership at his previous gym.
- 3. Visit FitZone to check the facilities in person.
- 4. Share the gym's website information with the woman.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Reserve a spot for a group fitness class: They haven't signed up yet, so reserving a class would be premature.
❌ 2. Cancel his membership at his previous gym: He already cancelled his GoodLife membership six months ago.
❌ 3. Visit FitZone to check the facilities in person: They plan to visit together on Wednesday, not before.
✅ 4. Share the gym's website information with the woman: He says 'I'll send you the link to their website so you can look it over before we go.'

## Practice 591: Beginner Yoga Selection | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a new member and an instructor at a yoga studio. The man has recently joined and is looking for a suitable class to attend.

### Audio Transcript
$4f

**Q1:** Why has the man come to the yoga studio?
- 1. To purchase equipment for yoga practice
- 2. To sign up for a studio membership
- 3. To find an appropriate class to join
- 4. To get advice on treating his back pain
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. To purchase equipment for yoga practice: The man does not mention buying equipment; the mat purchase happens later after the woman's suggestion.
❌ 2. To sign up for a studio membership: He says he 'signed up for a membership here about a week ago,' so that is already done.
✅ 3. To find an appropriate class to join: He says 'I'm looking to start attending classes' and asks about beginner options.
❌ 4. To get advice on treating his back pain: He mentions his stiff lower back as a consideration for class selection, not as the main reason for visiting.

**Q2:** How many openings remain in the Gentle Flow class?
- 1. About four
- 2. About eight
- 3. About seven
- 4. About fifteen
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. About four: The woman says 'there are about four spots left.'
❌ 2. About eight: Eight is the capacity of the smaller renovation room, not the number of remaining spots.
❌ 3. About seven: This number is not mentioned in the conversation.
❌ 4. About fifteen: Fifteen is the maximum class size, not the number of remaining spots.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q3:** What is the main issue discussed in this part of the conversation?
- 1. The man registered for the wrong type of class
- 2. The studio's online system is permanently offline
- 3. The man forgot to complete his registration on time
- 4. The man cannot attend the class he enrolled in
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man registered for the wrong type of class: He registered for the correct class; the problem is the room change reducing capacity.
❌ 2. The studio's online system is permanently offline: The system had a temporary update error, not a permanent failure.
❌ 3. The man forgot to complete his registration on time: He did register online, but the system didn't reflect the reduced class size.
✅ 4. The man cannot attend the class he enrolled in: Due to renovations and a smaller room, the class is full even though he registered.

**Q4:** How does the man feel about the situation?
- 1. Confused about the registration process
- 2. Disappointed that he cannot participate as planned
- 3. Angry at the woman for not informing him sooner
- 4. Indifferent because he can come another week
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Confused about the registration process: He understands what happened; his frustration is about the outcome, not the process.
✅ 2. Disappointed that he cannot participate as planned: He says 'I was really counting on getting started this week' and 'This is kind of frustrating.'
❌ 3. Angry at the woman for not informing him sooner: He doesn't direct blame at the woman personally; he expresses general frustration.
❌ 4. Indifferent because he can come another week: He is clearly upset, not indifferent — he bought a mat and was eager to begin.

**Q5:** What is true about the woman?
- 1. She thinks the waitlist will take several weeks.
- 2. She believes the man should try a different class.
- 3. She acknowledges the studio made an error.
- 4. She suggests the man come back next Tuesday instead.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. She thinks the waitlist will take several weeks: She says there's 'a decent chance' he'll get in that same evening since he's first on the list.
❌ 2. She believes the man should try a different class: She doesn't suggest another class; she offers the waitlist for the same one.
✅ 3. She acknowledges the studio made an error: She says 'the system didn't update properly' and 'I'm really sorry about the confusion.'
❌ 4. She suggests the man come back next Tuesday instead: She offers the waitlist, not a suggestion to return the following week.

### Audio Transcript
$4e

**Q6:** Where is tonight's class being held?
- 1. In the main studio at the front of the building
- 2. In the first room on the right, near the entrance
- 3. In the room directly beside the water fountain
- 4. In the second room on the left, beyond the water fountain
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. In the main studio at the front of the building: The main studio is under renovation; the class has been moved.
❌ 2. In the first room on the right, near the entrance: The woman says 'second door on the left,' not first on the right.
❌ 3. In the room directly beside the water fountain: The room is past the water fountain, not directly beside it.
✅ 4. In the second room on the left, beyond the water fountain: The woman says 'the second door on the left, past the water fountain.'

**Q7:** What is true about the man?
- 1. He had considered canceling his membership.
- 2. He has already returned his new yoga mat.
- 3. He plans to attend the Saturday morning session.
- 4. He has been practicing yoga for several months.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. He had considered canceling his membership: He says 'I was honestly thinking about asking for a refund on the membership earlier.'
❌ 2. He has already returned his new yoga mat: He bought a mat from the shop and never mentions returning it.
❌ 3. He plans to attend the Saturday morning session: The Saturday Power Yoga class is for his friend, not for him.
❌ 4. He has been practicing yoga for several months: He says he's 'completely new to yoga.'

**Q8:** What will most likely happen next?
- 1. The man will request a refund for his membership.
- 2. The man will head to the room for his first class.
- 3. The woman will call other waitlisted members.
- 4. The woman will check the studio website for his friend.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. The man will request a refund for his membership: He explicitly says he's glad he didn't ask for a refund — he's staying.
✅ 2. The man will head to the room for his first class: He got the spot, the class starts in twenty minutes, and the woman says 'Enjoy the class tonight!'
❌ 3. The woman will call other waitlisted members: The man was the only person on the waitlist, and he already got the spot.
❌ 4. The woman will check the studio website for his friend: She told the man to tell his friend to check the website herself.

## Practice 592: ConnectPlus Mobile Plans | Listening Practice Test

### Task
You will hear a conversation between a man and a woman at a mobile phone store.

### Audio Transcript
$4d

**Q1:** How much is the man currently spending on his phone service?
- 1. Forty-five dollars for five gigabytes of data
- 2. Sixty-five dollars for fifteen gigabytes of data
- 3. Seventy dollars for eight gigabytes of data
- 4. Eighty-five dollars for twenty-five gigabytes of data
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Forty-five dollars for five gigabytes of data: This describes the ConnectPlus Essential Plan, not his current plan.
❌ 2. Sixty-five dollars for fifteen gigabytes of data: This describes the ConnectPlus Premium Plan, not his current plan.
✅ 3. Seventy dollars for eight gigabytes of data: He says 'I'm paying seventy dollars a month for only eight gigabytes.'
❌ 4. Eighty-five dollars for twenty-five gigabytes of data: This describes the ConnectPlus Ultimate Plan, not his current plan.

**Q2:** Why does the man decide against the Ultimate Plan?
- 1. He does not need twenty-five gigabytes of data each month.
- 2. The monthly cost is too high compared to his current plan.
- 3. He has no need for cloud storage on his phone.
- 4. He already contacts his overseas family through applications.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 4
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. He does not need twenty-five gigabytes of data each month: Data amount is not his stated reason for rejecting the Ultimate.
❌ 2. The monthly cost is too high compared to his current plan: He doesn't mention cost as the reason for rejecting the Ultimate.
❌ 3. He has no need for cloud storage on his phone: Cloud storage is not mentioned as a factor in his decision.
✅ 4. He already contacts his overseas family through applications: He says 'I mostly call them through apps,' so international calling is unnecessary.

**Q3:** What is required to receive the promotional pricing?
- 1. Agreeing to a two-year service commitment
- 2. Registering before April third
- 3. Paying an extra ten dollars per month
- 4. Participating in the device trade-in program
- **Correct Answer:** Option 1
- **Explanation:** ✅ 1. Agreeing to a two-year service commitment: The woman says 'the promotion requires a two-year contract.'
❌ 2. Registering before April third: The deadline is April fifteenth, not April third — April third is today's date.
❌ 3. Paying an extra ten dollars per month: The extra ten dollars applies to month-to-month plans, which do NOT qualify for the discount.
❌ 4. Participating in the device trade-in program: The trade-in is a separate option, not a requirement for the promotion.

**Q4:** What can be inferred about the man's current phone?
- 1. It cannot support video calling applications.
- 2. It is likely not working as well as it used to.
- 3. It is still locked to his existing carrier.
- 4. He purchased it within the last few months.
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. It cannot support video calling applications: He says he is 'often on video calls,' so it can handle them.
✅ 2. It is likely not working as well as it used to: He mentions 'my battery's been draining quickly lately,' suggesting the phone is deteriorating.
❌ 3. It is still locked to his existing carrier: Whether his phone is locked or unlocked is never confirmed.
❌ 4. He purchased it within the last few months: He has been with his provider for three years and mentions battery issues, suggesting the phone is older.

**Q5:** What does the woman recommend the man consider doing with his phone?
- 1. Having it unlocked at a nearby service centre
- 2. Replacing its battery to improve performance
- 3. Exchanging it for credit toward a new device
- 4. Keeping it since it is compatible with their network
- **Correct Answer:** Option 3
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Having it unlocked at a nearby service centre: She mentions the phone needs to be unlocked but does not suggest where to do it.
❌ 2. Replacing its battery to improve performance: Battery replacement is never suggested by the woman.
✅ 3. Exchanging it for credit toward a new device: She mentions the trade-in program where he 'could get up to two hundred dollars in credit toward a new device.'
❌ 4. Keeping it since it is compatible with their network: She says he can keep it if it is unlocked, but her proactive suggestion is about the trade-in.

**Q6:** What will the man most likely do when he returns to the store?
- 1. Sign the two-year contract for the Premium Plan
- 2. Have his current phone evaluated for trade-in value
- 3. Provide his identification and previous provider's bill
- 4. Compare prices between the Premium and Ultimate plans
- **Correct Answer:** Option 2
- **Explanation:** ❌ 1. Sign the two-year contract for the Premium Plan: He says he'll go with the Premium Plan 'today,' meaning the sign-up happens now, not tomorrow.
✅ 2. Have his current phone evaluated for trade-in value: He says 'I'll bring my phone in tomorrow for the trade-in assessment.'
❌ 3. Provide his identification and previous provider's bill: While he may also need to bring his bill, his explicitly stated reason for returning is the trade-in assessment.
❌ 4. Compare prices between the Premium and Ultimate plans: He has already decided on the Premium Plan and shows no interest in reconsidering.
